# CampusOS

Cloud-native, multi-tenant School Operating System. Replaces 8–15 disconnected school software systems with one platform.

## Project Status

**Cycle 20 (Food Service) — original Round 1 build state.** All 10 steps shipped + vertical-slice CAT verified live on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-06. **Cycle 20 ships the M63 Food Service module — 16 new tenant base tables across 3 migrations (068 + 069 + 070).** Tenant base table count 263 → **279**. **~28 intra-tenant FKs**, 0 cross-schema FKs. The Food Service Manager (FSM) is the seventh specialist operator persona after the nurse, counsellor, librarian, athletic director, enrolment officer, and transportation coordinator. **Three structural keystones**: (1) **POS allergen cross-check (SAFETY KEYSTONE)** — `TransactionService.create` reads `fds_student_allergen_alerts WHERE patron_id=$1 AND is_active=true` then intersects against menu item `allergen_codes TEXT[]` in JS. Any `severity='CRITICAL'` match returns 422 BLOCKED with structured `{statusCode, error: 'AllergenBlocked', blocked: [...]}` body until `supervisorOverrideId` is supplied; the supervisor is verified against `hr_employees` and the row is written with the multi-column `override_chk` lockstep CHECK populated atomically (`allergen_override_required=true`, `supervisor_override_id`, `override_reason`). Verified live on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-06: Ethan + Grilled Cheese (matched MILK CRITICAL) → 422 with `blocked` array; retry with Rivera as `supervisorOverrideId` → 201 with override audit columns populated. (2) **GIN-indexed allergen catalogue** — `fds_menu_items.allergen_codes TEXT[]` carries a 14-value FDA-aligned taxonomy (MILK / EGGS / PEANUTS / TREE_NUTS / FISH / SHELLFISH / WHEAT / SOYBEANS / SESAME / CELERY / MUSTARD / SULFITES / LUPIN / MOLLUSCS); GIN INDEX `fds_items_allergen_codes_gin USING GIN (allergen_codes)` accelerates the `&&` array overlap operator the POS check + the catalogue allergen filter use. Verified live: `?codes=MILK,PEANUTS` returns 3 items (Chocolate Milk + Grilled Cheese + PBJ Sandwich). (3) **Health-to-Food read model (ADR-030)** — `fds_student_allergen_alerts` mirrors Cycle 10 `hlth_health_alerts` for severity-tagged food allergens via the same projection pattern used by Cycle 10's `sis_student_active_accommodations`. Manual sync today via `POST /food-service/allergen-alerts/sync` (admin-only on `fds-003:admin`); the partial UNIQUE INDEX `(student_id, allergen_code) WHERE is_active=true` is the schema-side dedup gate. Phase 2 wires a Kafka consumer on `hlth.allergy_alert.changed` to keep the projection idempotent. **Backend module**: FoodServiceModule with **13 services + 1 FoodServiceController + ~38 endpoints** under `fds-001:read/write/admin` (menus) through `fds-004:read/write/admin` (food safety + USDA) + 1 Kafka emit topic (`fds.transaction.completed`). Services across menu.service.ts (MenuCycleService + MenuItemService + DailyMenuService), pos.service.ts (PosService + SessionService + TransactionService SAFETY KEYSTONE + ReconciliationService), dietary-eligibility.service.ts (DietaryProfileService + DietaryUpdateRequestService + AllergenAlertService + EligibilityService + TemperatureLogService + ProductionRecordService). **Step 4 seed**: `seed-food-service.ts` (idempotent, gated on `fds_menu_cycles` row count) — 1 cycle "Week A" (5-day standard rotation) + 8 menu items with allergen codes (Chocolate Milk MILK, Grilled Cheese MILK+WHEAT, PBJ Sandwich PEANUTS+WHEAT+SOYBEANS, Chicken Nuggets WHEAT+SOYBEANS, Apple Slices, Carrot Sticks, Cheese Pizza MILK+WHEAT+SOYBEANS, Salad Bar) + 2 daily menus (today + tomorrow LUNCH) + 1 POS device "Lunch Line 1" + 1 yesterday MEAL session with 3 historical transactions + 3 dietary profiles (Maya STANDARD with PEANUTS allergen, Ethan STANDARD with MILK allergen, Aiden VEGETARIAN) + **2 CRITICAL allergen alerts** (Maya PEANUTS, Ethan MILK — drives the SAFETY KEYSTONE) + 1 NSLP application + APPROVED FREE determination for Maya + 2 historical temperature logs (compliant + non-compliant with corrective action) + 1 cash drawer reconciliation row with `variance=-0.15` and `status=VARIANCE_FLAGGED`. Wired as `seed:food-service`. **`seed-iam.ts` extended**: Teacher gains `FDS-001:read` (66 perms total); Parent gains `FDS-001:read + FDS-003:read` (38 — the parent-active surface for dietary update requests); Student gains `FDS-001:read` (37); Staff (covers FSM) gains `FDS-001..004:read+write` (100); School Admin / Platform Admin retain admin-tier via everyFunction (**454**). Catalogue: 453 → **454** — `FDS-001` (Menu management), `FDS-002` (POS / cashier), `FDS-003` (Dietary + eligibility), `FDS-004` (Food safety + USDA) added to `permissions.json`. **Web side**: new `Food Service` launchpad tile gated on `fds-001:read` using new `UtensilsIcon`; persona-aware copy. **9 web routes**: `/food-service` dashboard with 5-stat header (active sessions / today's transactions / today's sales / non-compliant temps / pending requests) + today's menu + critical allergen alerts; `/food-service/menus` cycles + items list with allergen filter chips + upcoming daily menus; `/food-service/pos` POS Checkout SAFETY KEYSTONE UI with allergen panel showing CRITICAL/WARNING badges + rose-bordered BLOCKED card with structured allergen list + supervisor override input; `/food-service/sessions` open/close sessions + cash reconciliation with variance auto-compute; `/food-service/dietary` pending requests + critical allergen heatmap + all alerts; `/food-service/eligibility` NSLP applications with determination modal; `/food-service/safety` temperature log entry with auto-compliance check + corrective action requirement; `/food-service/usda-claims` monthly claim generator; `/children/[id]/food-service` parent portal with dietary profile + allergen alerts + today's menu with **child-specific allergen highlighting** (rose-700 border on items containing the child's CRITICAL allergens) + meal history + dietary update request modal. **Cycle 19 (Transportation) — original Round 1 build state.** Wave 4 (Campus Operations) opens here. Cycle 19 opens **Wave 4 (Campus Operations)**. Ships the M61 Transportation module — 16 of the 38 ERD tables in scope (22 deferred to Cycle 19.1: real-time GPS telemetry, deep fleet maintenance, driver hours logs, route optimisation engine, materialised fleet status dashboard). The Transportation Coordinator (TC) is the sixth specialist operator persona after the nurse, counsellor, librarian, athletic director, and enrolment officer. **16 new tenant base tables** across 3 migrations (064 + 065 + 066). Tenant base table count 247 → **263**. **~25 intra-tenant FKs**, 0 cross-schema FKs. **Three structural keystones:** (1) **Immutable route change log** — `trn_route_change_log` is append-only at the service layer (only `RouteChangeLogService.recordChange` writes; no UPDATE / no DELETE methods exposed). Every route mutation writes a row inside the same tenant tx with `change_type` (8-value CHECK) + before/after JSONB snapshots. FERPA-style 7-year retention semantics. (2) **No-show detection worker** — `NoShowService.runOnce()` walks expected ridership (`trn_student_assignments`) against actual scans (`trn_ridership_records`) and inserts `trn_no_show_alerts` ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING (UNIQUE(student, route, expected_date, expected_stop) is the schema-side dedup gate) + emits `trn.no_show.detected`. Verified live on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-06 — fresh sweep with no boarding scans inserted 2 alerts; after Maya scanned BOARDING the next sweep inserted only Ethan's alert. (3) **QR-coded bus passes** — `trn_bus_passes.qr_code_token TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE`; `RidershipService.scan` resolves the student via the unique token, validates `is_active + valid_from..valid_to`, then writes a `trn_ridership_records` row in one tenant tx. Invalid / expired / inactive tokens return 400. **Pre-trip inspection FAIL safety gate**: `RunLogService.start` calls `assertVehicleInspectedAndPassing(vehicleId, runDate)` before stamping the run row; missing inspection → 400; FAIL → 400. Verified live on Bus #42. **Parent-active route change requests**: `RouteChangeRequestService.submit` (parent self-service with row scope to own children via `sis_student_guardians`) + `approve` (TC) which locks the request inside one tenant tx, calls `AssignmentService.createOverrideInTx` to land an `is_override=true` student assignment + writes a STUDENT_ADDED change log row, then atomically flips the request to APPROVED per the multi-column `reviewed_chk` lockstep. **Backend module**: TransportModule with **13 services + 1 controller + ~38 endpoints** under `trn-001:read/write/admin` through `trn-005:read/write/admin` + 2 Kafka emit topics (`trn.no_show.detected`, `trn.delay.reported`). Services: RouteChangeLogService (sole writer to the immutable log), RouteService, StopService, AssignmentService, RouteChangeRequestService, VehicleService, InspectionService, DriverCredentialService, BusPassService, RidershipService, RunLogService, NoShowService, DelayReportService. **Step 4 seed**: `seed-transport.ts` (idempotent, gated on `trn_routes` row count) — 2 routes (Route 7 Elm Street AM 5 stops, Route 8 Oak Lane PM 3 stops) + 2 vehicles (Bus #42 capacity 48, Van #3 capacity 12) + 4 vehicle documents + 1 driver (Linda Park stand-in covering the Driver role since the seed only ships 5 personas) + 3 driver credentials (CDL + MEDICAL_CERTIFICATE + BACKGROUND_CHECK, all VALID) + 2 student assignments (Maya stop #2, Ethan stop #4 AM) + 2 ANNUAL bus passes with `BPS-MAYA-…` / `BPS-ETHAN-…` QR tokens + 1 historical pre-trip inspection (yesterday, all 6 items PASS) + 1 historical run log (yesterday COMPLETED, students_boarded=15) + 2 historical ridership records (yesterday BOARDING) + 1 maintenance schedule. Wired as `seed:transport`. **`seed-iam.ts` extended**: Teacher gains `TRN-001:read` (65 perms total); Parent gains `TRN-001:read` + `TRN-005:read+write` (36 — the parent-active surface for route-change requests, the fourth parent-active feature after messaging / conference booking / application submission); Student gains `TRN-001:read` (36); Staff (covers TC) gains `TRN-001..005:read+write` (92); School Admin / Platform Admin retain admin-tier via everyFunction (453). Catalogue stays at **151 functions × 3 tiers = 453** — TRN-001..005 already in `permissions.json` from earlier waves. **Web side**: new `Transportation` launchpad tile gated on `trn-001:read` using new `BusIcon`; persona-aware copy. **8 web routes**: `/transport` dashboard with 4-stat header + active-routes grid (TC view) OR route + bus pass cards (student/parent view); `/transport/routes/[id]` detail with stops + per-stop students + admin-only Change Log timeline; `/transport/fleet` with vehicle cards + 3-stat document expiry summary; `/transport/drivers` with credential status pills; `/transport/scan` driver-facing QR scanner; `/transport/no-shows` TC dashboard with admin-only "Run sweep now" button + Resolve modal; `/transport/inspections/new` driver-facing pre-trip checklist with auto-computed overall status; `/children/[id]/transport` parent portal with QR pass display + 30-day ridership history + Change-request modal. New `apps/web/src/lib/transport-format.ts` + `apps/web/src/hooks/use-transport.ts` with ~30 React Query hooks. **Vertical-slice CAT** at `docs/cycle19-cat-script.md` walks 10 plan scenarios end-to-end. **Splitter trap caught + fixed pre-provision** on `066_trn_ridership_operations.sql` (2 stray `;` inside COMMENT ON TABLE strings rewritten with em-dashes); migrations `064` + `065` clean on first audit. **Twenty-fifth migration in a row to clear the splitter trap on first provision attempt after audit** (Cycles 4–19 unbroken streak). Both `tenant_demo` and `tenant_test` provisioned cleanly. Plan at `docs/campusos-cycle19-implementation-plan.html`; handoff at `HANDOFF-CYCLE19.md`.

**Cycle 18 (Groups & Communities) is COMPLETE + APPROVED at `f091de6` (REVIEW-CYCLE18-CHATGPT — final verdict). Round 1 against `cycle18-complete` at `7e7f33a` returned Reject pending fixes; Round 2 against `f091de6` returned APPROVED.** Reviewer cache-busted each affected file in code and confirmed all 4 BLOCKING fixes (creation authority by scope; tenant validation on invited accounts; roster visibility for non-members; transfer-history visibility) plus the 2 code-level MAJORs (locked-row state transitions; join-race translation) are closed; MAJORs 7–10 correctly carried as Phase 2 punch list items 26–29. **Wave 3 closes here.** Reviewer's accompanying Wave 3 closeout note: Wave 3 is functionally approved but not pilot-clean; the remaining risk is the accumulated Phase 2 / pre-pilot backlog (role-model hardening, outbox/event atomicity, consumer tenant-routing validation, header-based tenant override hardening, platform-scope permission mode, items 1–29 on the module-specific Phase 2 list). A dedicated hardening cycle should land before any real-school pilot. Tagged `cycle18-complete` at `7e7f33a` and `cycle18-approved` at `f091de6`. Round 1 fix details preserved below — 4 BLOCKING + 6 MAJOR (MAJOR 5 locked-row state transitions + MAJOR 6 join-race translation are code-level fixes; MAJORs 7–10 are recommendation-class and move to Phase 2 punch list as items 26–29). Fix commit lands all 4 BLOCKING + the 2 code-level MAJORs with live verification on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-06: (BLOCKING 1) `GroupService.assertCanCreateForScope` enforces SCHOOL/CUSTOM/YEAR_GROUP=admin/STAFF only; CLASS=admin or assigned class teacher (joins sis_class_teachers); ACTIVITY=admin or activity advisor (joins ext_activities.advisor_id) — verified across 6 paths including student SCHOOL → 403, parent CUSTOM → 403, teacher ACTIVITY for own advisorship → 201, teacher ACTIVITY for someone else's → 403. (BLOCKING 2) `MembershipService.assertAccountInCurrentTenant` validates invited platform_users.id against the three current-tenant projections (sis_students via platform_students, sis_guardians, hr_employees) — bogus UUID + admin@ Platform Admin both return 400. (BLOCKING 3) `MembershipService.listForGroup` returns `[]` to non-members of OPEN/APPROVAL_REQUIRED groups; admins + active members see the full roster — verified across 5 paths. (BLOCKING 4) `OwnershipTransferService.list` restricts transfer history to admin + OWNER/ADMIN; non-managers and non-members get `[]` — verified across 4 paths (admin 2, Rivera-as-OWNER 2, Maya-as-MEMBER 0, parent-as-non-member 0). (MAJOR 5) `MembershipService.lockedTransition` helper wraps every membership state mutation (acceptInvite/declineInvite/approveJoin/denyJoin/updateRole/suspend/unsuspend/remove) in a tenant tx with `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE OF m` so concurrent managers can't both pass a status check and land conflicting writes — verified with 3 parallel role updates serialising cleanly without deadlock. (MAJOR 6) `MembershipService.joinGroup` catches the partial-UNIQUE race via `isUniqueViolation` and re-reads the existing row instead of surfacing 23505 — 5 parallel join requests verified live → 5× 201 + exactly 1 grp_members row. The existing CAT continues to apply; the fixes don't change the happy-path scenarios. Wave 3 closes here pending Round 2 APPROVED. Original cycle build details preserved below.

**Cycle 18 (Groups & Communities) — original Round 1 build state.** All 8 steps shipped + vertical-slice CAT verified live on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-06. **Cycle 18 closes Wave 3 (Communications & Community).** Cycle 18 ships the M103 Groups & Communities module — all 8 ERD tables (the plan's 7 + a Cycle-18-shipped `grp_event_rsvps` link table that the plan count omitted but per-RSVP audit required) in a clean greenfield `grp_*` namespace. **8 new tenant base tables** across 2 migrations (061 + 062): `grp_groups`, `grp_members`, `grp_member_notification_prefs`, `grp_ownership_transfers` — Step 1; `grp_announcements`, `grp_announcement_reads`, `grp_events`, `grp_event_rsvps` — Step 2. Tenant base table count 239 → **247**. **9 intra-tenant FKs** (5 + 4); **0 cross-schema FKs**. **30 endpoints** under `grp-001:read/write/admin` across 5 services + 1 controller (GroupService + MembershipService + OwnershipTransferService + GroupAnnouncementService + GroupEventService). **2 new Kafka emit topics** (`grp.announcement.posted`, `grp.event.created`) — both ADR-057 envelopes captured live during the CAT. **1 new permission code** added to `permissions.json`: `GRP-001` (Groups & Communities, group="Communications"). Catalogue: 150 → **151 functions × 3 tiers = 453 permissions**. IAM Teacher / Student / Parent / Staff each gain `GRP-001:read+write`; Admin via everyFunction. Effective access cache rebuilt: admin 453 / teacher 64 / staff 82 / student 35 / parent 33. **Three structural keystones** verified end-to-end: (1) **two-party ownership transfer handshake** — partial UNIQUE(group_id) WHERE status='PENDING' caps pending transfers at one per group; `OwnershipTransferService.accept` opens a tenant transaction, locks the transfer + both grp_members rows with `FOR UPDATE`, demotes the from-member to ADMIN and promotes the to-member to OWNER, stamps `responded_at` per the multi-column `responded_chk` lockstep — all four updates inside one tx so a failure rolls back the entire handshake; live verified with the seeded Rivera→Mitchell PENDING transfer (Rivera as from-member 403 on accept; Mitchell accepts → Rivera ADMIN + Mitchell OWNER atomically). (2) **Scope-aware bindings** — `grp_groups.scope_type` is a 5-value CHECK (CLASS/YEAR_GROUP/SCHOOL/CUSTOM/ACTIVITY); multi-column `scope_pair_chk` enforces SCHOOL/CUSTOM with `scope_id IS NULL` and CLASS/YEAR_GROUP/ACTIVITY with `scope_id IS NOT NULL`; `GroupService.create` validates the supplied scope_id matches an existing row in the matching tenant table (`sis_classes` / `sis_academic_years` / `ext_activities`); Cycle 17's `ext_activities.group_id` soft FK closes the resolution loop in the other direction. (3) **Re-join semantics** — partial UNIQUE(group_id, person_id) WHERE status<>'LEFT' on `grp_members` caps active membership at one row but allows re-join after voluntary leave because LEFT rows drop out of the partial index; the multi-column `left_chk` keeps `left_at` populated only when status='LEFT'. **6-state member lifecycle** ACTIVE / INVITED / PENDING_APPROVAL / SUSPENDED / LEFT / REMOVED covers every transition the join-policy + admin-action matrix needs. **Web side**: new `Groups` launchpad tile gated on `grp-001:read` using `ChatBubbleIcon` with persona-aware copy; **4 new web routes** (`/groups` browse + create with scope filter chips + pending-transfer banner, `/groups/[id]` detail with 4 tabs Announcements / Events / Members / Transfers + Owner-only Transfer-ownership Modal + RSVP cap-aware buttons + read-receipt tracking, `/groups/my` aggregated active memberships, `/groups/feed` upcoming RSVPs). 25 React Query hooks in `use-groups.ts`. Format helpers in `groups-format.ts` covering every enum + relative-date + datetime utilities. **Live verification on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-06**: 8 plan scenarios all green — group browsing across 4 personas with correct visibility; ownership transfer with atomic role swap; announcement create + read receipt + 403 on non-manager POST; `grp.announcement.posted` envelope captured live with full ADR-057 shape; event create with `max_attendees=2` + RSVP cap correctly rejecting 3rd GOING with 400; `grp.event.created` envelope captured live; re-join via partial UNIQUE leaving a LEFT audit row + a fresh ACTIVE row; scope shape rules rejecting SCHOOL+scopeId and CLASS-without-scopeId; 4 permission denial paths (student POST announcement → 403; INVITE_ONLY non-member → 404; transfer recipient binding → 403; OWNER-must-transfer-first → 400). **`seed:groups`** seeds 3 groups (Grade 5 Parents YEAR_GROUP APPROVAL_REQUIRED + Chess Club Community ACTIVITY OPEN linked to Cycle 17 Chess Club + Spring Concert Volunteers CUSTOM with auto_dissolve_at=+60d) + 6 members + 3 notification prefs + 1 PENDING ownership transfer + 2 announcements (1 pinned) + 1 read receipt + 2 events. Idempotent re-runs no-op cleanly. **Splitter trap caught + fixed pre-provision** on both migrations (1 stray `;` in 061 block-comment header, 3 strays in 062 across COMMENT ON TABLE strings + block comment); twenty-third migration in a row to clear the trap on first attempt after audit (Cycles 4–18 unbroken streak). Plan at `docs/campusos-cycle18-implementation-plan.html`; handoff at `HANDOFF-CYCLE18.md`. **Wave 3 closes here.** Wave 4 (Operations & Logistics) is next.

Verified live: Maya tries to register Sofia → 403 "Students can only register themselves as candidates"; Maya registers herself → 201 with is*approved=false; admin registers Sofia on behalf → 201 with is_approved=true. (BLOCKING 3 — parent field-trip projection) `FieldTripService.getById` branches the DTO on `isManager(actor)`: staff/admin keep full inline; guardians get `participants[]` filtered to children linked via `sis_student_guardians` with `consentSigned`/`consentGiven` reflecting only THIS guardian's signature for THIS child; `chaperones[]` returned as `[]` because chaperone names + background-check status are staff-only. (MAJOR 5) `ChaperoneService.assertPersonInCurrentTenant` validates `personId` matches an `hr_employees.person_id` or `sis_guardians.person_id` row in the calling tenant — bogus UUIDs return 400. (MAJOR 6) `ServiceProgrammeService.getLeaderboard(programmeId, actor)` is now admin/staff only — students see own progress via `myProgress`; bogus access from student/parent → 403. (MAJOR 7) `FieldTripService.addParticipant` wraps in `executeInTenantTransaction` with `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` on the trip row + capacity check counting non-WITHDRAWN participants under the lock. Verified live: trip with max=2 + 2 existing participants rejects 3rd add with 400 "Field trip has reached its max_participants cap of 2". **Cycle 16 (Enrolment & Admissions) is COMPLETE + APPROVED at `850fc6d` (REVIEW-CYCLE16-CHATGPT — final verdict).** Round 1 against `cycle16-complete` at `1b19c6c` returned **Reject pending fixes** with 3 BLOCKING + 3 MAJOR follow-ups; Round 2 against `850fc6d` returned **Approved** — reviewer confirmed all 3 BLOCKING findings are closed in code (row-scope on stage/score/onboarding reads via `assertCanReadApplication`, `waiveTask` reuses `transitionTask` lifecycle, offer-accept onboarding generation typed-result + no-broad-try/catch). Reviewer's accepted follow-up: Enrolment Officer (EO) treated as `actor.personType === 'STAFF'` is acceptable for the build phase but should land on the pre-pilot role-model hardening list — EO should become a distinct role rather than generic Staff. Captured as Phase 2 punch list item 22 alongside Counsellor / Nurse / Librarian / AD splits. Tagged `cycle16-complete` at `1b19c6c` and `cycle16-approved` at `850fc6d`. **Cycle 16 ships clean.** Round 1 also flagged 3 MAJOR follow-ups, of which 1 was code-level fixed inline (MAJOR 4 — admissions scoring restricted to admin/EO only via `assertEoOrAdmin`). MAJORs 5 (DB role hardening for stage audit immutability) + 6 (tightening onboarding checklist read visibility to admin/EO if internal task names reveal process) are recommendation-class and move to the Phase 2 punch list. All 3 BLOCKING fixes landed in this commit with live verification on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-06: (BLOCKING 1 — privacy) `ApplicationStageService.list()` + `OnboardingService.getProgress()` + `getProgressForApplication()` are now actor-aware via `assertCanReadApplication(applicationId, actor)` — admin/STAFF (covers EO) sees every application; guardian sees own children's applications only (matched on `enr_applications.guardian_person_id = actor.personId`); everyone else gets a collapsed `404 NotFoundException` rather than the row. **MAJOR 4 implementation:** `ApplicationScoringService.listForApplication()` is now restricted to admin/EO via `assertEoOrAdmin(actor)` (not visible to guardians or students at all — `403`) because admissions scores carry ranked-selection criteria + scorer notes. Verified live across 5 personas: parent 200 on own child stages/onboarding + 404 on others' + 403 on scores; VP (Staff persona, holds `stu-003:read`) sees all reads on every application. (BLOCKING 2 — onboarding keystone) `waiveTask()` previously only flipped the individual task-completion row to WAIVED without locking the parent progress row, recomputing counters, or emitting `enr.student.onboarded`. Refactored into a private `transitionTask(completionId, newStatus, notes, actor)` helper used by both `completeTask()` and `waiveTask()` so a waived mandatory task flows through the same lock-recompute-flip-emit pipeline. Verified live: completed 4 of 5 PENDING tasks via `/complete` (progress 4/8 → 7/8, `onboarded=False`); waived the 5th via `/waive` → progress 8/8 / `overall_status=COMPLETE` / `onboarded=True` + `enr.student.onboarded` envelope captured on the wire. (BLOCKING 3 — atomicity contract) `OfferService.respond()` ACCEPTED branch previously wrapped `generateProgressForApplicationInTx()` in `try { ... } catch { swallow }`. The helper now returns a typed discriminated union `{ status: 'CREATED' | 'EXISTS' | 'NO_CHECKLIST' | 'NO_APPLICATION', progressId? }` so `OfferService.respond` only swallows `NO_CHECKLIST` (legitimate "school has no STANDARD_INTAKE checklist yet" branch); `NO_APPLICATION` rethrows as a transactional anomaly. Unexpected SQL / runtime errors now propagate naturally up the `executeInTenantTransaction` callback and roll back the offer-accept atomically, so the offer never lands as `ACCEPTED` when its onboarding row failed to create. Verified live: Sophia's UNCONDITIONAL ISSUED offer accepted → application flips ACCEPTED → ENROLLED + 1 progress row + 8 task completion rows materialised in a single atomic commit. **MAJORs 5 + 6** carried to Phase 2 punch list (DB role hardening for stage audit immutability; tightening onboarding checklist read visibility to admin/EO if internal task names reveal process). All 10 steps shipped + verified live on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-05 (Round 1 cycle build) and 2026-05-06 (Round 1 fixes). Cycle 16 layers on Cycle 6 (which already shipped 10 of the 16 plan tables) with **6 new logical base tables** across migrations 055 + 056 + 057 (`enr_application_stages` immutable audit, `enr_application_scores` UNIQUE(application, criterion), `enr_onboarding_checklists` template + UNIQUE(school, name, admission_type), `enr_onboarding_tasks` CASCADE on parent template, `enr_student_onboarding_progress` UNIQUE(application, checklist) with multi-column `completed_chk` lockstep + `tasks_completed <= tasks_total` CHECK, `enr_student_onboarding_task_completions` multi-column lockstep keeping `(status, completed_at, completed_by)` consistent). Tenant base table count 217 → **223**. Status enum extensions on `enr_applications` (adds INTERVIEW + ASSESSMENT + OFFERED → 11 values) and `enr_enrollment_periods` (adds OFFERS_ISSUED + COMPLETED → 5 values) via splitter-safe `DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS … ; ADD CONSTRAINT …` pattern. **3 new backend services** (ApplicationStageService with locked-row state-machine + ALLOWED_TRANSITIONS map, ApplicationScoringService with UNIQUE catch into 400, OnboardingService with checklist CRUD + per-student progress generation in same tenant tx as offer-accept + completeTask keystone + waiveTask) + 3 controllers + **12 new endpoints** (2 stages + 4 scoring + 6 onboarding) under `stu-003:read/write/admin`. **`enr.student.onboarded` Kafka emit** is the new Cycle 16 keystone — fires when the last mandatory onboarding task lands, distinct from Cycle 6's `enr.student.enrolled` which keeps firing on offer-accept (PaymentAccountWorker continues to consume it for billing-account allocation). IAM Staff role gains STU-003:read+write covering Enrolment Officer; Admin already has STU-003 admin via everyFunction. Web side ships 3 admissions panels (StagesPanel + ScoresPanel + OnboardingPanel injected into `/admissions/applications/[id]`) + 1 new admin route (`/admissions/onboarding-checklists` template catalogue with create modal). 14 new React Query hooks added to `use-enrollment.ts`. Vertical-slice CAT at `docs/cycle16-cat-script.md` walks 7 plan scenarios end-to-end with **inline-captured live output per scenario** (per the REVIEW-CYCLE15 punch list item 21 convention) — schema preamble + multi-stage advance + illegal-transition rejection + score lifecycle including duplicate-detection + onboarding reads + onboarding keystone with `enr.student.onboarded` envelope captured live + 6 permission denials. `seed:onboarding` seeds 1 STANDARD_INTAKE checklist with 8 tasks across 7 categories + Maya's progress at 3/8 IN_PROGRESS + 1 SUBMITTED stage row. Idempotent re-runs no-op cleanly. Plan at `docs/campusos-cycle16-implementation-plan.html`; handoff at `HANDOFF-CYCLE16.md`.

**Cycle 15 (Meetings & Conferences) is COMPLETE + APPROVED at `39b0d90` (REVIEW-CYCLE15-CHATGPT — final verdict).** Round 1 of REVIEW-CYCLE15-CHATGPT (against `cycle15-complete` at `93b1bd5`) returned **Reject pending fixes** with 3 BLOCKING row-scope / sensitive-data issues + 5 MAJOR follow-ups. Round 2 against `39b0d90` returned **Approved** — reviewer confirmed all 3 BLOCKING findings are closed in code (`IepMeetingRecordService.canReadRecord` three-tier scope, slot identity stripping for non-organiser non-admin readers, agenda actor-scope with collapsed 404) plus the four code-level MAJOR follow-ups (tenant validation, slot booking authority restricted to GUARDIAN/admin/organiser, recording consent participation requirement). MAJORs 6 + 8 correctly carried as Phase 2 punch list items 20 + 21. Tagged `cycle15-complete` at `93b1bd5` and `cycle15-approved` at `39b0d90`. **Cycle 15 ships clean.** Round 1 also flagged 5 MAJOR follow-ups, of which 4 were code-level fixed in the Round 1 closeout commit: All actionable items addressed in the closeout fix commit (live-verified on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-06). MAJOR 6 (`mtg_meeting_participants.source` marker so cancel-by-booking does not delete manually-curated participants) and MAJOR 8 (CAT live-output capture pattern) are recommendation-class and move to the Phase 2 punch list as items 20 + 21. **REVIEW-CYCLE15 fixes (2026-05-06):** (BLOCKING 1 — IEP record privacy) `IepMeetingRecordService.canReadRecord(actor, meetingId, studentId)` replaces the broad `hlt-001:read` check with three tiers — admin all / counsellor (cou-001:write at the role level) own active caseload via `svc_caseloads` / non-counsellor STAFF + meeting participation + `hlt-001:read` for the health-team operator path. Everyone else (parents, teachers, students) gets a collapsed 404. `create()` and `patch()` write authority restricted to admin or counsellor with the student on own active caseload. (BLOCKING 2 — slot identity leak) `SlotService.listForMeeting(meetingId, actor)` resolves whether the caller is admin or the meeting organiser; only those two see the full `bookedBy` + `bookedByName` + `bookedAt` identity columns. Other readers see `is_booked` plus their own booking; every other parent's booking has identity columns nulled in the response. (BLOCKING 3 — agenda enumeration) `AgendaService.listForMeeting(meetingId, actor)` checks participant/organiser/admin via `meetings.isParticipantOrOrganiser`; non-participant non-admin gets a collapsed 404 NotFoundException matching the row-scope-don't-leak-existence pattern. Controller resolves the actor + passes through. (MAJOR 4 — tenant validation) Shared `MeetingService.assertAccountInCurrentTenant(accountId, fieldName)` joins through `sis_students` / `sis_guardians` / `hr_employees` projections to confirm the supplied `platform_users.id` has a projection in the calling tenant; applied to `MeetingService.create` (every supplied participantId), `addParticipant`, `AgendaService.create/patch` (presenterId), `ActionItemService.create` (assigneeId). Bogus UUIDs return 400 with the field name in the message. (MAJOR 5 — slot booking authority) `SlotService.book` refuses non-GUARDIAN actors unless they are school admin OR the meeting organiser (book-on-behalf path). Teachers + non-organiser staff are explicitly refused with the redirect message even though they hold `mtg-002:read`. (MAJOR 7 — recording consent participation) `RecordingService.giveConsent()` admin bypass removed; consent now requires the actor to be a meeting participant or the organiser. Admin who is not on the roster cannot consent through this surface. Live verification on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-06: all 6 code-level fixes verified end-to-end (parent IEP record 404, teacher IEP record 404, counsellor own-caseload 200, slot identity stripped for other parents, agenda 404 for non-participants, bogus participantId 400, teacher slot book 403 with redirect, non-participant admin consent 403). Tagged `cycle15-approved` after Round 2 verdict. All 10 steps done. Vertical-slice CAT at `docs/cycle15-cat-script.md` covers the 10 plan scenarios end-to-end on `tenant_demo`. Cycle 15 builds the M41 Meetings module — 11 of the 13 ERD tables in scope (2 AI-deferred tables — `mtg_transcription_jobs`, `mtg_ai_minutes_jobs` — move to Cycle 15.1 once an external transcription / inference service is integrated). The two product keystones drive the cycle: **(1) parent self-service slot booking** — `mtg_meeting_slots.book` uses `SELECT FOR UPDATE` on the slot row inside `executeInTenantTransaction` so concurrent attempts serialise + the booking auto-creates a participant row + the multi-column `booked_chk` lockstep keeps `(is_booked, booked_by, booked_at)` atomic; **(2) two-layer parent-visibility on meeting notes** — `mtg_meeting_notes.is_parent_visible` AND `mtg_meeting_notes.is_approved` must both be true before parents see notes, with the optional `parent_visible_summary` superseding `notes_text` when populated. Step 1 lands `053_mtg_conferences_meetings.sql` with 5 base tables (`mtg_conference_events` 5-value `conference_type` CHECK + `mtg_meeting_types` per-school catalogue + `mtg_meetings` 4-value status with `meeting_type_id` NO ACTION audit-preserving + nullable `conference_event_id` SET NULL + `mtg_meeting_participants` UNIQUE(meeting_id, participant_id) + 4-value role CHECK + `mtg_meeting_slots` PARENT BOOKING KEYSTONE with multi-column `booked_chk` lockstep + partial INDEX(meeting_id) WHERE is_booked=false for the available-slots picker). 4 new intra-tenant FKs (NO ACTION × 1 + SET NULL × 1 + CASCADE × 2). Step 2 lands `054_mtg_notes_recording_iep.sql` with 6 base tables (`mtg_agenda_items` ordered per-meeting + `mtg_meeting_notes` PARENT-VISIBILITY KEYSTONE with multi-column `approved_chk` keeping is_approved + approved_by + approved_at in lockstep + `mtg_action_items` cross-persona assignees with multi-column `completed_chk` lockstep on status / completed_at + `mtg_recordings` UNIQUE(meeting_id) + 3-value status CHECK + `mtg_recording_consents` UNIQUE(recording_id, participant_id) + `mtg_iep_meeting_records` CROSS-CYCLE INTEGRATION KEYSTONE with soft `iep_plan_id` to Cycle 10 hlth_iep_plans + structured `attendee_roles JSONB`). 7 new intra-tenant FKs (all CASCADE). 0 cross-schema FKs. **Tenant logical base table count: 206 → 217.** Step 3 ships `seed-meetings.ts` (idempotent, gated on `mtg_meeting_types` row count) wired as `seed:meetings`: 4 meeting types (PTC 15min / Staff 60min / IEP 45min video / Department 30min) + 1 PTC conference event "Spring Parent-Teacher Conferences 2026" + 2 meetings (Rivera availability container + Hayes IEP review COMPLETED) + 6 PTC time slots (3 per day across May 20–21, slot #1 booked by David Chen with `booked_chk` lockstep verified) + 4 participants + 2 agenda items on the IEP meeting + 2 meeting notes (PTC parent-visible + approved with parent summary, IEP staff-only) + 3 action items (Rivera self + David parent + Hayes DONE) + 1 IEP meeting record linking to Maya's Cycle 10 IEP plan with attendee_roles JSONB. **`seed-iam.ts` extended:** Teacher gains MTG-001:read+write + MTG-002:read+write (54 → 58); Parent gains MTG-001:read + MTG-002:read (27 → 29); Student gains MTG-001:read (28 → 29); Staff gains MTG-001:read+write + MTG-002:read (67 → 70). Catalogue stays at **450** — MTG-001 + MTG-002 already in `permissions.json`. Step 4 lands MeetingsModule with ConferenceService + MeetingTypeService + MeetingService + SlotService + matching controllers + DTO module + ~14 endpoints + 1 Kafka emit topic (`mtg.meeting.scheduled` AFTER tx commit per ADR-057). MeetingService row-scope = admin all OR (organiser_id=me OR EXISTS participant=me); auto-adds organiser as HOST + auto-adds initial participant ids on POST /meetings. **`SlotService.book` PARENT BOOKING KEYSTONE** — locks slot with FOR UPDATE inside executeInTenantTransaction, validates is_booked=false, stamps booked_chk lockstep atomically, auto-creates participant row via ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING. `cancel` releases slot + removes auto-created participant if no other slots booked by same person. Step 5 lands MeetingNotesService (PARENT-VIS GATE keystone — staff sees full, GUARDIAN sees parent_visible_summary or notes_text only when both flags true, STUDENT sees null) + AgendaService + ActionItemService (cross-persona assignees — assignee may update own status/dueDate, organiser/admin may edit anything) + RecordingService (consent_confirmed auto-flips when every participant has consent_given=true row inside the same tx) + ~14 more endpoints. Step 6 lands IepMeetingRecordService — service-layer `assertHealthAccess` requires admin OR `hlt-001:read` since IEP records are health-sensitive; resolves Cycle 11 `svc_mtss_team_meetings.meeting_id` soft FK + links Cycle 10 `hlth_iep_plans` for IEP-linked meetings. ~4 more endpoints. Steps 7 + 8 + 9 ship the web surface — Meetings launchpad tile gated on `mtg-001:read` (every persona) using CalendarIcon with `routePrefix: '/meetings'`. **`/meetings`** dashboard with persona-aware copy + upcoming conferences + upcoming meetings table + my-action-items badge link. **`/meetings/[id]`** detail with metadata grid + participants chips + agenda + **NotesPanel keystone** (parent-visibility toggle with amber warning + IEP-meeting rose warning + irreversible Approve button with confirm) + **ActionItemsPanel** with "Mark done" inline button for own items + cross-persona Add modal + **RecordingPanel** with consent collection modal (Accept/Decline) + IEP record violet card visible only to staff with hlt-001:read. **`/meetings/conferences/[id]`** PARENT BOOKING GRID KEYSTONE — slot chips grouped by date with available/booked/my-booking pills + click-to-book confirmation modal + own-booking Cancel link. **`/meetings/action-items`** dashboard with status filter chips (Open/In Progress/Done/All) + overdue tinting + per-row Start/Done buttons. **17 new React Query hooks** in `apps/web/src/hooks/use-meetings.ts` covering every Step 4–6 endpoint. Web build clean — 4 new routes ship at modest First Load JS sizes. Step 10 lands `docs/cycle15-cat-script.md` — 5-check schema preamble + 9 plan scenarios + cleanup. Cycle 15 ships clean to the post-cycle architecture review. Plan at `docs/campusos-cycle15-implementation-plan.html`; handoff at `HANDOFF-CYCLE15.md`. Tagged `cycle15-complete` after CI green.

**Cycle 14 (Communications) is COMPLETE + APPROVED at `b36ffc8` (REVIEW-CYCLE14-CHATGPT — final verdict).** Round 1 of REVIEW-CYCLE14-CHATGPT (against `cycle14-complete` at `9d76abd`) returned **Reject pending fixes** with 2 BLOCKING issues on the messaging surface (FLAGGED-message visibility before moderator review + edit/delete row-scope leak via 403) plus 5 MAJOR follow-ups. Round 2 against `b36ffc8` returned **Approved** — reviewer confirmed both BLOCKING findings are closed in code (`MessageService.list()` filter hiding non-APPROVED messages from non-sender non-admin readers + `edit()`/`softDelete()` participant-first row-scope returning collapsed 404) plus the three code-level MAJOR follow-ups (emergency fan-out audience tightened to school-affiliated only, deliveryCount uses RETURNING for accurate counts, Staff COM-004 grant removed to align with `ModerationService.assertAdmin()` contract). MAJOR 5 (`msg_thread_stats` drift on edit/delete — needs new `msg.message.edited` + `msg.message.deleted` Kafka topics + recompute from source) and MAJOR 7 (alert-headers-to-non-recipients — needs audience targeting beyond today's school-wide default) correctly carried as Phase 2 punch list items 18 + 19. Tagged `cycle14-complete` at `9d76abd` and `cycle14-approved` at `b36ffc8`. **Cycle 14 ships clean. Wave 3 (Communications & Community) is now open.** **REVIEW-CYCLE14 fixes (2026-05-05):** (BLOCKING 1 — moderation contract) `MessageService.list()` now appends `AND (m.moderation_status = 'APPROVED' OR m.sender_id = $N::uuid)` for non-admin readers. FLAGGED + ESCALATED + BLOCKED messages stay hidden from recipients until the moderator's `PATCH /messaging/moderation/log/:id/review` with outcome=RELEASED flips the parent message back to APPROVED. The original sender continues to see their own pending-moderation message; school admins see everything for the moderation queue. The Cycle 14 vertical-slice contract ("admin reviews the flagged message queue and releases a previously FLAGGED message") now matches the code. (BLOCKING 2 — privacy leak) `MessageService.edit()` + `softDelete()` now call `threads.isActiveParticipant(thread_id, actor.accountId)` before the sender / admin check. Non-participant non-admin callers receive a collapsed `404 NotFoundException` matching the row-scope-don't-leak-existence pattern from REVIEW-CYCLE13 BLOCKING 2 + REVIEW-CYCLE10. (MAJOR 3 — audience scope) `EmergencyAlertService.issue()` audience query tightened from `WHERE st.name IN ('SCHOOL', 'PLATFORM') AND (sc.scope_ref_id = $1 OR st.name = 'PLATFORM')` to `WHERE st.name = 'SCHOOL' AND sc.scope_ref_id = $1` so emergency alerts default to school-affiliated recipients only. Cross-tenant Platform Admin notification can be re-introduced as an explicit `includePlatformAdmins` option once a real-school operator workflow needs it. (MAJOR 4 — count accuracy) Issue-path INSERT switched to `RETURNING id`; `deliveryCount` increments only when the row was actually inserted, so a future deterministic-id retry path that hits `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING` reports the real delivery surface. (MAJOR 6 — permission contract alignment) `seed-iam.ts` Staff role spec no longer grants COM-004:read+write since `ModerationService.assertAdmin()` requires `actor.isSchoolAdmin` specifically. The 2 stale role-permission rows for the existing Staff role were DELETE'd directly from the DB so the live IAM cache matches the service contract; comment in seed-iam documents the locked product decision (moderation policy + queue + log are admin-only until the AD/role-split pre-pilot work introduces a dedicated Moderator role). Live verification on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-05: 5 scenarios all pass — ESCALATED message hidden from David Chen until admin RELEASE flips it to APPROVED then David's next list call returns it; synthetic-UUID PATCH/DELETE returns 404; emergency audience query no longer includes platform-scope accounts; deliveryCount matches actual insert count; VP/Counsellor cache drops 69 → 67 perms after stale COM-004 row deletion. Build clean, prettier clean, tests pass. Tagged `cycle14-approved` after Round 2 verdict. All 10 steps done. Vertical-slice CAT at `docs/cycle14-cat-script.md` covers the 10 plan scenarios end-to-end against `tenant_demo`. Cycle 14 completes the M40 Communications module that **Cycle 3 substantially built** — Cycle 3 already shipped 12 of the 15 plan tables, plus ThreadService / MessageService / UnreadCountService / ContentModerationService / AnnouncementService / AudienceFanOutWorker / `/messages` + `/announcements` web routes. Cycle 14 adds the genuinely-new pieces: 1 new messaging table (`msg_thread_stats`) + the Kafka consumer that maintains it; 2 new emergency-alert tables (`msg_emergency_alerts`, `msg_emergency_alert_deliveries`) + EmergencyAlertService + AlertTypeService + 1 new Kafka emit (`msg.emergency.issued`); ModerationService admin endpoints (queue + review + policy CRUD); the persistent rose-tinted dismiss-proof emergency alert banner UI + management dashboard; and the admin moderation queue + audit log + policy editor UI. Step 1 lands `051_msg_thread_stats.sql` with the per-thread denormalised summary (`message_count` + `last_message_at` + `last_message_preview LEFT(body,100)` + `last_sender_id` + `updated_at`; INDEX(school_id, last_message_at DESC) for inbox sort). Step 2 lands `052_msg_emergency_alerts.sql` with `msg_emergency_alerts` (multi-column `resolved_chk` keystone keeping (status, resolved_at, resolved_by) in lockstep so the schema never sees a half-resolved row; partial INDEX(school_id) WHERE status=ACTIVE for the live-banner hot path) + `msg_emergency_alert_deliveries` (UNIQUE(alert_id, recipient_id, channel) + partial INDEX(recipient_id) WHERE acknowledged_at IS NULL for the per-recipient unread-emergency hot path) + 3 column additions on `msg_announcements` (`is_recurring`, `recurrence_rule`, `parent_announcement_id`) for forward-compatible recurrence support. **2 new intra-tenant FKs** (1 NO ACTION on alert_type_id audit-preserving, 1 CASCADE on deliveries→alert); 0 cross-schema FKs. Tenant base table count: 203 → **206**. Step 3 ships `seed-emergency.ts` (idempotent, gated on `msg_emergency_alerts` row count) — 2 additional alert types (Severe Weather EMERGENCY [PUSH,APP] requires_acknowledgement=true / Early Dismissal WARNING [APP]) + 1 ACTIVE seed alert "Severe Weather Drill" with 3 deliveries (Rivera PUSH DELIVERED+ack / David Chen APP DELIVERED / Maya APP PENDING) + `msg_thread_stats` backfill from existing seeded threads. `seed-iam.ts` extended: Teacher gains COM-003:read (53 → 54); Parent gains COM-003:read (26 → 27); Student gains COM-003:read (27 → 28); Staff gains COM-002:write + COM-003:read+write + COM-004:read+write (64 → 69). Catalogue stays at **450** — COM-001..004 already in `permissions.json`. Step 4 ships `apps/api/src/messaging/consumers/thread-stats.consumer.ts` — `ThreadStatsConsumer` subscribes to `dev.msg.message.posted` under group `thread-stats-consumer`, reuses the standard `unwrapEnvelope` + `processWithIdempotency` claim-after-success pattern, UPSERTs `msg_thread_stats` keyed on `thread_id` with `message_count = msg_thread_stats.message_count + 1` on conflict. ThreadService.list keeps the existing LATERAL preview (functional, partition-prune optimization is a follow-up) — `msg_thread_stats` is the partition-prune-friendly fallback the consumer maintains. Step 5 ships `apps/api/src/emergency-alerts/` with EmergencyAlertModule + AlertTypeService (3 endpoints under com-003:read / com-004:write) + EmergencyAlertService (5 endpoints under com-003:read / com-003:write) + EmergencyAlertDeliveryController (1 endpoint under com-003:read for recipient ack) + DTO module + 1 new Kafka emit topic `msg.emergency.issued`. **`EmergencyAlertService.issue()` is the keystone** — runs in `executeInTenantTransaction`, validates the alert type is active via `AlertTypeService.loadActiveOrFail`, INSERTs the head row, fans out one delivery row per (recipient, channel) for every active platform user with a school-tier or platform-tier role assignment (mirrors the audience-resolution shape in Cycle 3's AudienceFanOutWorker.audienceAllSchool), then emits `msg.emergency.issued` AFTER tx commit so a Kafka hiccup cannot roll back the alert. `acknowledgeDelivery()` locks the row inside a tx and refuses cross-recipient acknowledgement with a collapsed 404 don't-leak-existence per the row-scope convention. `resolve()` locks the alert row + flips ACTIVE → RESOLVED with `resolved_at` + `resolved_by` stamped atomically per the multi-column `resolved_chk` keystone. Issuer scope = `actor.isSchoolAdmin OR holds com-003:write`. EmergencyAlertsModule wired into AppModule between AthleticsModule and the global guards. Step 6 extends `apps/api/src/messaging/` with `ModerationService` + `ModerationController` — 6 new endpoints under com-004:read / com-004:write (admin-only via `actor.isSchoolAdmin`): `GET /messaging/moderation/policies` lists every active policy across PLATFORM/DISTRICT/BUILDING tiers; `POST /messaging/moderation/policies` creates a building-tier policy (refuses PLATFORM/DISTRICT — those are seed-only); `PATCH /messaging/moderation/policies/:id` updates BUILDING-tier only; `GET /messaging/moderation/queue` lists FLAGGED + ESCALATED + BLOCKED messages whose `review_outcome` is NULL or PENDING; **`PATCH /messaging/moderation/log/:id/review` is the keystone — locks the log row, sets `review_outcome` (CONFIRMED_BLOCK / RELEASED / ESCALATED) + `reviewed_at` + `reviewed_by`, and on RELEASED flips the parent `msg_messages.moderation_status` back to APPROVED so the message becomes visible to its recipient again** all inside one tenant tx; `GET /messaging/moderation/log` paginated audit trail with `flag_type` / date-range / policy filters. ContentModerationService remains the canonical writer of `msg_moderation_log` rows on the outbound-message path. Steps 7 + 8 + 9 ship the web surface — Step 7 carries over from Cycle 3 (no new UI: `/messages` inbox, `/messages/[threadId]` thread view, `/messages/new` compose all already exist); Step 8 ships the **persistent dismiss-proof EmergencyAlertBanner** mounted in `apps/web/src/components/shell/AppLayout.tsx` between TopBar and main (renders for any user with com-003:read who has at least one ACTIVE alert with `requiresAcknowledgement=true` AND `myDelivery.acknowledgedAt=null`; rose-tinted (`bg-rose-100 border-rose-700`) with title + body + Acknowledge button; polls every 30s + refetches on focus) plus `/messages/emergency-alerts` admin dashboard (Active / Resolved tabs + per-alert delivery stats card with totalDeliveries / sent / delivered / acknowledged / pending counts + Issue-alert modal with alert type picker / title / body / channel-override chips + Resolve button on every ACTIVE alert with `window.confirm` guard); Step 9 ships **3 admin moderation routes** under `/messages/moderation/*` — `/messages/moderation` policy editor (3 sections grouped by tier: PLATFORM read-only, DISTRICT read-only, BUILDING admin CRUD with per-policy keyword chips + 3-action pill (BLOCK rose / FLAG_FOR_REVIEW amber / ESCALATE_TO_COUNSELLOR violet) + Active/Inactive toggle + create-policy modal); `/messages/moderation/queue` flagged-message review queue with per-row matched-keyword chips + sender + thread context + Release / Confirm-block buttons opening confirmation modals; `/messages/moderation/log` paginated audit trail with flag-type dropdown + from/to date filters + per-row outcome rendering. **11 new React Query hooks** across `apps/web/src/hooks/use-emergency-alerts.ts` (8 hooks) and `use-moderation.ts` (5 hooks). Step 10 ships `docs/cycle14-cat-script.md` — schema preamble (6 checks: 206 base tables / 3 new tables / 3 new announcement columns / 2 emergency FKs / seed shape / IAM grant distribution) + 10 plan scenarios verified live on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-05. Cycle 14 ships clean to the post-cycle architecture review. **Wave 3 (Communications & Community) is now open.** Plan at `docs/campusos-cycle14-implementation-plan.html`; handoff at `HANDOFF-CYCLE14.md`. Tagged `cycle14-complete` after CI green.

**Cycle 13 (Athletics) is COMPLETE + APPROVED at `ec46e85` (REVIEW-CYCLE13-CHATGPT — final verdict).** Round 1 of REVIEW-CYCLE13-CHATGPT (against `cycle13-complete` at `7001205`) returned **Reject pending fixes** with 2 BLOCKING privacy issues on the injury read surface + 5 MAJOR follow-ups; Round 2 against `ec46e85` returned **Approved** after all fixes landed. Reviewer confirmed each finding is closed in code: `buildVisibility` correctly bounds the injury list, `getById(id, actor)` enforces row scope inside the SELECT and returns collapsed 404, the roster cap is locked under `FOR UPDATE OF r`, `patchResult` refuses outcome changes (with the future reversal-and-re-entry workflow appropriately deferred to Phase 2), `bulkEnter` validates every studentId against active roster members, and `hasResultScope` was rewritten as explicit guard branches. MAJOR 6 (`ATH-005:read` to generic Staff — Athletic Director role split) is documented as a pre-pilot Wave 2 Phase 2 punch list item per the reviewer's gate decision. Tagged `cycle13-complete` at `7001205` and `cycle13-approved` at `ec46e85`. **Cycle 13 ships clean. Wave 2 (Student Services & Enrichment) closes here.** **REVIEW-CYCLE13 fixes (2026-05-05):** (BLOCKING 1 — privacy) `InjuryService.list()` only restricted STUDENT actors; teachers + generic Staff with `ATH-004:read` could enumerate every athletic injury in the tenant. New private `buildVisibility(actor, nextParamPosition)` helper returns admin / AD = full access, STUDENT = own row only (resolved via `actor.personId → platform_students → sis_students`), STAFF + `employeeId` = students enrolled in classes they teach (`sis_class_teachers + sis_enrollments WHERE status='ACTIVE'`) UNION athletes on rosters where they have an active coaching assignment (`ath_coaching_assignments` keyed on `coach_person_id`), everyone else `[]`. The schema-side enrolment + coaching-assignment joins are the actual access boundary — the permission gate alone is not. (BLOCKING 2 — privacy) `GET /athletics/injuries/:id` returned the full DTO without actor-aware row scope and threw a raw `Error` for non-student callers. `InjuryService.getById(id, actor)` now applies the same `buildVisibility` predicate to the WHERE clause; non-authorised callers receive a collapsed `404 NotFoundException` rather than the row. Controller resolves the actor via `actors.resolveActor(...)` and passes it through; the post-fetch student check + raw `Error` throw are removed. (MAJOR 3 — concurrency) `RosterService.addMember()` cap-check + INSERT now wrap inside `executeInTenantTransaction` with `SELECT … FROM ath_rosters r … FOR UPDATE OF r` at the top of the tx so concurrent submissions serialise on the parent roster row; the cap re-check happens inside the same tx as the INSERT, so loser requests re-read the bumped count and 400. (MAJOR 4 — bug) `ResultService.patchResult()` previously updated the result row but never reversed / recomputed `ath_season_records`, so flipping outcome from WIN to LOSS would leave the record stale forever. `patchResult()` now refuses any `outcome` change with a friendly 400 redirecting the caller to the future reverse-and-re-enter correction workflow; score / `score_by_period` / notes corrections still pass because they do not affect the season-record aggregate. The locked-row reverse-and-recompute path is on the Phase 2 punch list. (MAJOR 5 — validation) `StatsService.bulkEnter()` resolves the parent game's `roster_id` inside the existing tenant tx and validates every supplied `studentId` against `ath_roster_members WHERE roster_id = $1 AND removed_at IS NULL AND student_id = ANY($2::uuid[])`; mismatches return a single 400 with the missing UUIDs inlined. The schema-side UNIQUE on `(game, student, category)` remains the dedup belt-and-braces. (MAJOR 7 — readability) `GameService.hasResultScope()` rewritten from a mixed sync/async `||` expression to explicit guard-style `if (actor.isSchoolAdmin) return true; if (await this.programmes.hasAdScope(actor)) return true; return …;`. Live verification (5 scenarios on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-05): teacher Rivera with no AD scope and no coaching assignment GETs /injuries → `[]` (was: every injury); GET /injuries/<seeded-id> → 404 (was: full DTO); after adding a coaching assignment for Rivera on Maya's roster, the same teacher sees Maya's injuries on the list endpoint and getById returns the DTO; 5 parallel POST /rosters/:id/members against a roster at cap-2-of-3 lands 1× 201 + 4× 400; PATCH result with `{outcome:'LOSS'}` against an existing WIN row returns 400 with the redirect message; POST /games/:id/stats with a non-roster studentId returns 400 with the offender UUIDs. Tagged `cycle13-approved` at the fix commit (after Round 2 verdict). All 10 steps done; vertical-slice CAT at `docs/cycle13-cat-script.md` verified live on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-05 — all 10 plan scenarios pass with the GPA eligibility check keystone (reads back into Cycle 2 gradebook), the season record auto-update inside the same tx as result-entry + `ath.game.result.entered` Kafka emit, the **6-step concussion return-to-play protocol with step-sequencing enforcement** (step N+1 cannot start until step N is completed + symptom_free + minimum_duration_hours have elapsed), and the medical-clearance → return-to-play closure that restores roster eligibility atomically all confirmed live. Final cycle totals: **14 ath\_\* base tables** across 3 migrations (046 + 047 + 048); tenant base table count 189 → **203**; **25 intra-tenant FKs** (6 + 10 + 9); **0 cross-schema FKs**; **49 endpoints** across 11 services + 11 controllers + DTO module; **1 Kafka emit topic** (`ath.game.result.entered`); IAM catalogue stays at **450** (ATH-001..010 already in `permissions.json`); **9 web routes** (`/athletics`, `/programmes`, `/programmes/[id]`, `/rosters/[id]`, `/schedule`, `/games/[id]`, `/injuries`, `/injuries/[id]`, `/my`) + Athletics launchpad tile (TrophyIcon, gated on `ath-001:read`) + 23 React Query hooks + ~25 athletics DTOs + format helpers. Cycle 13 introduces the **Athletic Director (AD)** as the fourth specialist operator persona alongside the nurse (Cycle 10), counsellor (Cycle 11), and librarian (Cycle 12). Cycle 13 is **the final cycle of Wave 2 (Student Services & Enrichment)** — Wave 2 closes here. Plan at `docs/campusos-cycle13-implementation-plan.html`; handoff at `HANDOFF-CYCLE13.md`. Tagged `cycle13-complete` after CI green. Step 1 lands `046_ath_programmes_rosters.sql` with 4 logical base tables (ath_programmes with 4-value `season` CHECK FALL/WINTER/SPRING/YEAR_ROUND + non-empty `levels_offered TEXT[]` + optional `min_gpa NUMERIC(3,2)` 0.00-4.00 CHECK + UNIQUE(school, sport_name); ath_seasons with 4-value status CHECK + multi-column `dates_chk` keeping last_game_date >= first_game_date >= first_practice_date; ath_rosters with **multi-column `certified_chk` keystone** keeping is_certified + certified_at + certified_by in lockstep + 4-value level CHECK + UNIQUE(season, level); ath_roster_members with 6-value `eligibility_status` CHECK ELIGIBLE/INELIGIBLE/PENDING_PHYSICAL/PENDING_CONSENT/PENDING_TRANSFER_WAIVER/INJURED_NOT_CLEARED + multi-column `dates_chk` on joined/removed_at + UNIQUE(roster, student) + partial INDEX(roster, eligibility) WHERE removed_at IS NULL). 6 new intra-tenant FKs (CASCADE × 4 + SET NULL × 2). 24-assertion smoke green. Step 2 lands `047_ath_games_results.sql` with 6 logical base tables (ath_games with 3-value location + 6-value status + soft polymorphic opponent_school_id; ath_game_proposals per ADR-069 with 5-value status + multi-column responded_chk + counter_proposal_data JSONB; ath_game_results with UNIQUE(game) + 4-value outcome CHECK; ath_player_game_stats with UNIQUE(game, student, category); ath_season_records with UNIQUE(roster) + 6 non-negative counters; ath_all_time_records with 3-value record_type + UNIQUE(school, sport, type, category)). 10 new intra-tenant FKs. 21-assertion smoke green. Splitter trap caught + fixed pre-provision (2 stray `;` inside a stat-category COMMENT — rewritten with "plus" connectives). Step 3 lands `048_ath_coaching_injuries.sql` with 4 logical base tables (ath_coaching_assignments with `coach_person_id` universal soft ref to platform.iam_person + 4-value role + non-negative stipend + multi-column dates_chk; ath_injuries with 4-value severity + 4-value `return_to_play_status` ACTIVE/SIDELINED/CONCUSSION_PROTOCOL/CLEARED + multi-column `cleared_chk` keeping cleared_at populated only for CLEARED; **`ath_concussion_protocol_steps` SAFETY KEYSTONE** with step_number BETWEEN 1 AND 6 CHECK + minimum_duration_hours > 0 default 24 + multi-column completed_chk + UNIQUE(injury, step_number); `ath_medical_clearances` with document_s3_key + 5-value review_status + multi-column reviewed_chk). 9 new intra-tenant FKs (CASCADE × 4 + NO ACTION × 2 + SET NULL × 3). 24-assertion smoke green. Splitter trap not tripped — **twentieth migration in a row to clear the trap on first attempt** (Cycles 4–13 unbroken streak). Step 4 lands `seed-athletics.ts` (idempotent, gated on `ath_programmes` row count) wired as `seed:athletics`: 2 programmes (Basketball WINTER VARSITY+JV min_gpa=2.0 / Track & Field SPRING VARSITY min_gpa=2.0); 2 seasons (Basketball 2025-2026 ACTIVE / Track UPCOMING); 2 rosters (Basketball VARSITY certified by Mitchell with Coach Rivera / Basketball JV uncertified); 3 roster members (Maya VARSITY #23 Guard `INJURED_NOT_CLEARED` mid-protocol / Ethan VARSITY #11 Forward ELIGIBLE / Aiden Johnson JV #15 Forward ELIGIBLE); 3 games + 2 results + 6 stats (Game 1 vs Jefferson HOME WIN 52-48 with score-by-period JSONB; Game 2 vs Washington AWAY LOSS 41-55; Game 3 vs Roosevelt HOME SCHEDULED; Maya G1 12pts/5reb/3ast + G2 8pts/4reb/2ast); 1 season record (1W-1L-0D / 1CW-1CL-0CD); 1 coaching assignment (Rivera HEAD_COACH $5000 stipend); 1 MODERATE head injury for Maya (CONCUSSION_PROTOCOL); 3 concussion protocol steps (step 1 + 2 COMPLETED, step 3 IN PROGRESS); 0 clearances yet. **`seed-iam.ts` extends Teacher (50 → 53) + Parent (24 → 26) + Student (24 → 27) + Staff (54 → 64)** with ATH-001..005 grants per the plan distribution (Teacher/Student/Parent get programme + game schedule reads; Student additionally gets injury read row-scoped to own; Staff covering AD gets all 5 ATH codes read+write; admin via everyFunction). 32 rows + 18 role-permission rows. Step 5 lands the first 3 athletics services + controllers + 14 endpoints — ProgrammeService (CRUD + UNIQUE catch + active-or-fail helper), SeasonService (locked-row PATCH + multi-column dates_chk → 400 translation), **RosterService with the GPA eligibility check keystone** (`computeLiveGpa(studentId)` reads `cls_grades.grade_value` for `is_published=true` rows, averages across all PUBLISHED grades, bands to 4-point GPA scale; `addMember()` validates max_roster_size_per_level cap + sets eligibility ELIGIBLE/INELIGIBLE/PENDING_PHYSICAL based on the live GPA vs programme min_gpa; `certify()` locks the row + atomically stamps is_certified + certified_at + certified_by per the multi-column certified_chk keystone, refusing if any active member is INELIGIBLE; `checkEligibility()` bulk re-runs across the active roster). Step 6 adds GameService + GameProposalService + ResultService + StatsService + 20 endpoints + the keystone `ath.game.result.entered` Kafka emit (entered AFTER tx commit; ResultService runs entirely in `executeInTenantTransaction` — locks game, refuses double-entry, INSERTs result, flips game status to COMPLETED, UPSERTs season_record + bumps the right counters based on outcome and is_conference_game flag). Step 7 adds CoachingService + InjuryService + ConcussionProtocolService + MedicalClearanceService + 15 endpoints — InjuryService.list row-scopes STUDENT actors to own; on CONCUSSION_PROTOCOL log atomically flips the matching active roster member to INJURED_NOT_CLEARED; CLEARED transition refuses unless all 6 protocol steps complete + ACCEPTED clearance exists. **`ConcussionProtocolService.startStep` is the SAFETY KEYSTONE** — refuses unless step N is completed + symptom_free + minimum_duration_hours elapsed. **`MedicalClearanceService.review` on ACCEPTED** walks the parent injury and atomically flips return_to_play_status=CLEARED + restores roster eligibility=ELIGIBLE only when all 6 protocol steps are complete (for CONCUSSION_PROTOCOL injuries). Steps 8 + 9 ship the 9-route web surface — Athletics tile gated on ath-001:read; `/athletics` persona-aware landing; `/athletics/programmes` list + AD-only New Modal; `/athletics/programmes/[id]` season picker + roster grid; `/athletics/rosters/[id]` member table with GPA-band display + Add/Re-check/Certify actions; `/athletics/schedule` public table; `/athletics/games/[id]` with Enter-result Modal; `/athletics/injuries` AD log with status filters; `/athletics/injuries/[id]` **6-step protocol tracker UI** with per-step Start/Complete buttons enforcing the SAFETY KEYSTONE backend rules; `/athletics/my` student-only portal. **Live verification on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-05** (8-check schema preamble + 10 plan scenarios all green): GPA keystone — Maya's live grades band to GPA=1.0 < 2.5 → eligibility INELIGIBLE; Result keystone — 3-1 WIN flips season record to 1-0-0 conf 1-0-0 atomically; Concussion KEYSTONE — backdate-and-complete loop drives all 6 steps; Clearance KEYSTONE — ACCEPTED → injury CLEARED + roster eligibility ELIGIBLE atomically. Iteration issue caught + fixed during CAT: initial `computeLiveGpa` referenced `cls_grades.percentage` + `publish_status` but actual schema uses `grade_value NUMERIC(6,2)` + `is_published BOOLEAN`; SQL fixed to read `AVG(grade_value) WHERE is_published=true`. Cycle 13 ships clean to the post-cycle architecture review. **Wave 2 (Student Services & Enrichment) is now complete.**

**Cycle 12 (Library) is COMPLETE + APPROVED at `e5620a9` (REVIEW-CYCLE12-CHATGPT — final verdict).** Round 1 against `cycle12-complete` at `41b8736` returned **Reject pending fixes** with 4 BLOCKING items (3 row-scope leaks on `GET /library/checkouts/:id` + `GET /library/holds/:id` + barcode lookup leaking patron identity to catalogue-only readers; 1 cross-tenant patron validation gap on `CheckoutService.resolvePatronType` + `HoldService.placeHold` cross-patron path) + 3 MAJOR follow-ups (teacher review-moderation scope / STAFF reading-log breadth / hold duplicate race) tracked as Phase 2 punch list per reviewer's gate decision. **Round 2 against `e5620a9` returned Approved** — reviewer cache-busted each affected file in code and confirmed all 4 BLOCKING fixes match. All 4 BLOCKING fixes verified live on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-05. Tagged `cycle12-complete` on `41b8736` (original closeout commit + first CAT) and `cycle12-approved` on `e5620a9` (the REVIEW-CYCLE12 fix commit, after Round 2 APPROVED). Vertical-slice CAT at `docs/cycle12-cat-script.md` verified live on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-05; all 10 plan scenarios pass; one ADR-057 wire envelope captured live on `dev.lib.fine.issued` with full envelope shape. Cycle 12 ships the M24 Library module (14 of 20 ERD tables — recommendations / class-set checkouts / interlibrary loans / catalogue import / scan sessions / Library Space deferred). Plan at `docs/campusos-cycle12-implementation-plan.html`; handoff at `HANDOFF-CYCLE12.md`. Cycle 12 is the **first entirely new module prefix** (`lib_*`) since Cycle 10's `hlth_*`, the **second-to-last cycle of Wave 2 Student Services** (Cycle 13 Athletics & Clubs closes the wave), and ships the **second student-input surface in CampusOS** after Cycle 11.1 (students log reading entries + write book reviews). The vertical slice covers librarian adds catalogue item → 3 copies with barcodes → checkout policy → student barcode-scan checkout → hold queue → overdue return + auto-fine + `lib.fine.issued` Kafka emit → reading programme + log + 5-star review → curated reading list. **Step 1 lands `packages/database/prisma/tenant/migrations/043_lib_catalogue.sql`** (renumbered from the plan's `041` because slots 041 + 042 were taken by post-Cycle-11.1 fix migrations — `041_sis_child_link_requests_dedup.sql` from `b58e591` and `042_svc_wellbeing_checkins_dedup.sql` from the Cycle 11.1 Round 2 closeout) with 3 logical base tables: `lib_locations` (named library locations driving the where-is-this-copy tracking on `lib_catalogue_copies`; 6-value `location_type` CHECK SHELF/DISPLAY/BOOK*DROP/PROCESSING/REPAIR/STORAGE; UNIQUE(school_id, name); is_active flag for soft deactivation since the schema-side SET NULL on copies leaves a copy addressable while its location is reassigned); `lib_catalogue_items` (bibliographic record — one row per title; `school_id`, `title NOT NULL`, optional author/isbn/publisher/publish_year/category/dewey_decimal/description/cover_image_url; INDEX(school_id, title) for alphabetical browse; INDEX(isbn) for direct ISBN lookup from the librarian add-copy flow; **the keystone GIN INDEX `USING GIN (to_tsvector('english', title || ' ' || COALESCE(author, '')))`** backs the public catalogue search bar with `ts_rank` scoring + `plainto_tsquery` parsing — the COALESCE(author, '') concatenation ensures a missing author cannot null out the tsvector); `lib_catalogue_copies` (physical copy — one row per book on the shelf; `catalogue_item_id` FK CASCADE — a copy without its bibliographic record is meaningless; `location_id` FK SET NULL — a copy survives a shelf retirement; **UNIQUE `barcode`** across the tenant — the scannable identifier on the spine that the Step 5 `GET /library/copies/barcode/:barcode` keystone resolves to copy + item + checkout state in one call; 5-value `condition` CHECK NEW/GOOD/FAIR/POOR/LOST; `is_available BOOLEAN` denormalised so the catalogue list can compute available-copies count without joining lib_checkouts; 7-value `location_status` CHECK ON_SHELF/IN_BOOK_DROP/IN_PROCESSING/CHECKED_OUT/ON_HOLD_SHELF/IN_REPAIR/LOST; `replacement_value NUMERIC(8,2)` with non-negative CHECK for the future Step 6 FineService when a copy is marked LOST; INDEX(catalogue_item_id, is_available) for the per-item availability hot path; partial INDEX `(location_id) WHERE location_id IS NOT NULL` for per-location browse). 2 new intra-tenant DB-enforced FKs (1 CASCADE on copy → item, 1 SET NULL on copy → location); 0 cross-schema FKs. **Tenant logical base table count after Step 1: 175 → 178**. **Live verification on `tenant_demo` (single BEGIN…ROLLBACK with savepoints, 17 assertions all green):** T1 happy-path INSERT 2 locations + 1 item + 1 copy chained through item + location; T2 location_type_chk rejects BOGUS; T3 UNIQUE(school_id, name) rejects duplicate; T4 + T5 condition + location_status CHECKs reject BOGUS; T6 replacement_value CHECK rejects -1.00; T7 UNIQUE barcode rejects duplicate; T8 + T9 FK rejection on bogus item_id and location_id; **T10 GIN search keystone — `to_tsvector(...) @@ plainto_tsquery('english', 'Giver')` returns The Giver with ts_rank=0.06079271**; **T11 GIN search by author surname — plainto_tsquery 'Lowry' returns The Giver with same rank** (the COALESCE concatenation is exercised); T12 GIN search miss on 'Tolkien' returns 0 hits; T13 ISBN INDEX lookup; T14 partial available-copies INDEX returns count=1; T15 SET NULL on location delete leaves copy intact with location_id=NULL; T16 CASCADE on parent item delete drops 1 copy → 0 copies;
