`?category=` exact-match + `?author=` ILIKE substring filters; limit defaults to 50, capped at 200; without `q` browse alphabetical by title; `getById` inlines copies + rollups (totalCopies / availableCopies / activeHoldsCount / averageRating / reviewCount) computed by sub-selects against the live circulation + reviews state). `CopyService` (3 endpoints + the **`GET /library/copies/barcode/:barcode` keystone** — barcode lookup runs three round-trips: lib_catalogue_copies JOIN lib_locations JOIN lib_catalogue_items finds copy + scopes to school; CatalogueItemService.loadItemOrFail gets parent item with rollups; LEFT JOIN against lib_checkouts + platform.iam_person finds active checkout (if any) with patron_name + days_until_due; the Step 6 CheckoutService will hit this same path on every scan to validate availability before flipping copy state). LibraryModule imports TenantModule + IamModule. Build clean (`pnpm --filter @campusos/api build`); 12 library routes mapped on boot. **Live verification on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-05 (8 scenarios across R1–R8 all green; ~25 individual assertions):** R1 locations browse — all 5 personas (admin / principal / teacher / student / parent) see 3 locations via lib-001:read; **R2 GIN search keystone — `?q=Lowry` returns Number the Stars + The Giver (both Lois Lowry titles); `?q=Sachar` returns Holes (author surname hit); `?q=Tolkien` returns []; `?q=Wonder` returns Wonder; browse returns 5 titles alphabetically**; R3 getById Giver with rollups (totalCopies=3, availableCopies=2, activeHoldsCount=1, reviewCount=0) + 3 copies inlined with locationName='Fiction Shelves' joined; **R4 barcode lookup KEYSTONE — `LIB-FIC-001` resolves with copy + item + activeCheckout=Maya Chen + dueDate=2026-05-14 + daysUntilDue=9 + pendingHolds=1 in one round-trip; `LIB-FIC-002` returns activeCheckout=None; `BOGUS-123` 404; student + parent both 200 (lib-001:read public to authenticated users)**; R5 per-item /copies endpoint with location_name joined; R6 librarian-only writes — student/parent/teacher POST 403, principal POST 201 + duplicate barcode 400 UNIQUE + bogus locationId 400 FK + student PATCH 403 + principal PATCH 200; R7 GIN sees the new write through immediately (`?q=Smoke` returns the just-created Smoke Test Book — Postgres maintains the GIN INDEX on every INSERT/UPDATE); R8 cleanup restores tenant to seed shape exactly. Iteration issues caught + fixed: (1) `ParseIntPipe({ optional: true })` returns 400 with "numeric string is expected" when `?limit=` is missing; replaced with hand-roll `@Query('limit') limit?: string` + `Number(limit)` with isNaN guard. (2) Stale `node apps/api/dist/main.js` processes from earlier session held port 4000; `pkill -f` + explicit `kill -9` against the LISTEN PID cleared them. **Step 6 lands the circulation surface at `apps/api/src/library/`** — extends LibraryModule with **3 more services + 3 controllers + 16 new endpoints + 1 Kafka emit topic (`lib.fine.issued`)**. Cycle 12 endpoint count after Step 6: **28** (12 + 16). LibraryModule now imports KafkaModule on top of TenantModule + IamModule. **`CheckoutService`** is the keystone — `checkout(input, actor)` resolves barcode → copy with `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` to serialise concurrent scans, calls `resolvePatronType(personId)` (joins `platform.platform_students` first then `hr_employees` to determine STUDENT vs STAFF), reads the matching `lib_checkout_policies` row, validates `is_available=true` AND `count(active checkouts) < policy.maxCheckouts`, INSERTs the checkout row with `due_date = CURRENT_DATE + (policy.loanPeriodDays || ' days')::interval`, and UPDATEs the copy to `is_available=false / location_status='CHECKED_OUT'` all inside one tenant tx. **`returnCheckout(id, actor)` is the auto-fine + hold-reassignment keystone** — locks checkout row + computes `days_overdue = GREATEST(CURRENT_DATE - due_date, 0)::int`, stamps `returned_at + status='RETURNED'`, when `days_overdue > 0 AND policy.overdueFinePerDay > 0` INSERTs an OVERDUE fine (amount rounded to 2dp to dodge floating-point imprecision); then walks the hold queue with `SELECT … FROM lib_holds WHERE catalogue_item_id = $1 AND status='PENDING' ORDER BY placed_at ASC LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE` — if a waiting hold exists, the hold flips to READY with `notified_at=now()` + the copy walks to `ON_HOLD_SHELF / is_available=false`; otherwise the copy returns to ON_SHELF. **`lib.fine.issued` emits AFTER the tx commits** so a Kafka hiccup can't roll back the patron's return; envelope payload includes `fineId / checkoutId / patronId / fineType / amount / daysOverdue / status='OUTSTANDING' / sourceRefId`. `renew(id, actor)` validates ACTIVE/OVERDUE + renewal_count < policy.renewalsAllowed, extends due_date, resets to ACTIVE so renewing an OVERDUE checkout clears the flag. **`HoldService`** — `placeHold(input, actor)` defaults `patronId` to `actor.personId` (self-service) with the `hasLibrarianScope` check guarding cross-patron placements; refuses duplicate PENDING/READY hold from same patron; validates parent item has at least one copy. `cancel(id, actor)` patron-or-librarian via service-layer row-scope. `collect(id, actor)` librarian-only, refused on non-READY status. **`FineService`** — `list` row-scopes patrons to own; default OUTSTANDING-first sort; `getById` returns 404 don't-leak-existence to non-owner patrons; `pay` is `hasLibrarianScope`-gated; **`waive` is `actor.isSchoolAdmin`-gated** — financial write-off is admin authority, not librarian. **Live verification on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-05 (11 scenarios across R1–R11 all green):** R1 policy list (STUDENT 5/14d/2/$0.25 + STAFF 20/30d/5/$0); R2 patron self-service list with row-scope (student 2 own / parent 403 / admin 3 all); R3 student/teacher POST /checkouts → 403 (controller + service); **R4 KEYSTONE checkout-by-barcode** principal scans LIB-FIC-002 for Ethan → 201 with status=ACTIVE / dueDate=today+14 / copy flips to CHECKED_OUT atomically; **R5 max_checkouts enforcement** — Maya already has 1 active, 4 more land cleanly, the 5th attempt returns 400 with the policy message; **R6 renewal limit** — renew #1 → renewalCount=1 / dueDate +14d / renew #2 → renewalCount=2 / renew #3 → 400 ("Renewal limit reached (2 for STUDENT patrons)"); **R7 student self-service holds** — POST /holds gated on lib-002:read, student places own → 201 PENDING queuePosition=1, duplicate → 400, cross-patron place → 403 service-layer, own cancel → 200, others-cancel → 403, collect → 403 librarian-only; **R8 KEYSTONE return + auto-fine + hold reassignment** — backdate Maya seed checkout to 3 days overdue, return → 201 status=RETURNED; OVERDUE fine auto-creates `amount=$0.75 / daysOverdue=3`; Ethan's seeded PENDING hold flips to READY with notifiedAt populated; copy LIB-FIC-001 walks to ON_HOLD_SHELF / is_available=false; **R9 fine pay/waive** student PATCH pay → 403, principal PATCH pay → 200 PAID, principal (school admin) PATCH waive with reason → 200 WAIVED; **R10 ADR-057 wire envelope captured live** on `dev.lib.fine.issued` with full shape (event_type=lib.fine.issued / source_module=library / tenant_id / payload.fineType=OVERDUE / amount=$0.75 / daysOverdue=3 / status=OUTSTANDING); R11 cleanup wholesale DELETE in dependency order + seed:library re-run restores post-Step-4 seed shape exactly. **Iteration issue caught + fixed:** first draft gated POST /library/holds on lib-002:write but Student only has lib-002:read; smoke caught the 403 on self-service hold placement; resolved by changing the gate to lib-002:read on POST /holds + PATCH /:id/cancel — the service-layer `hasLibrarianScope` check on cross-patron operations is the actual access boundary, mirroring the Cycle 1 att-001:write self-service pattern. **Step 7 lands the reading + reviews surface at `apps/api/src/library/`** — extends LibraryModule with **4 more services + 4 controllers + 18 new endpoints**. Cycle 12 endpoint count after Step 7: **46** (28 + 18). `ReadingProgrammeService` (5 endpoints — list / get / leaderboard / create / patch; STUDENT actors receive `myProgress` inlined per programme; leaderboard joins programme_progress + sis_students + iam_person sorted by books_read DESC). `ReadingLogService` (4 endpoints, **STUDENT-INPUT KEYSTONE**) — `log()` validates the catalogue item exists + INSERTs the log + on `completedDate` set runs the **programme-progress auto-upsert** via SCHOOL_WIDE / CLASS audience-matching SQL: `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT (programme_id, student_id) DO UPDATE SET books_read += 1, pages_read += pagesRead, last_updated_at = now()` followed by a recompute of `is_complete` against the programme thresholds — all inside one tenant tx. `ReadingListService` (6 endpoints) — multi-column `published_chk` lockstep on PATCH stamps `published_at = now()` on publish + clears it on unpublish atomically; published lists visible to all readers, drafts hidden behind `?includeUnpublished=true` for writers; UNIQUE(school, name, year) name-collision catch + UNIQUE(list, item) duplicate-item catch. `ReviewService` (5 endpoints — list / submit / patch / hide / unhide) — UNIQUE(item, student) duplicate-review catch with PATCH-guidance message; soft-hide via `is_approved=false` preserves the row for audit while filtering it from non-moderator readers. **Authorisation pattern caught + fixed during smoke:** Student holds `lib-003:write` per the IAM seed (for self-service log + review submission) but is NOT a programme/list author or review moderator; `hasWriterScope` + `hasModeratorScope` add `if (actor.personType === 'STUDENT') return false` as the actual access boundary above the IAM check. **Reviews list endpoint switched to `lib-001:read`** (catalogue-read surface) so teachers (who hold lib-001:read but not lib-003:read) see reviews on the catalogue page. **Live verification on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-05 (8 scenarios all green):** R1 programme list with myProgress inlined for student; R2 leaderboard `Maya Chen books=2 pages=313`; R3 reading log row scope (student own / staff requires `?studentId` / parent 403); **R4 STUDENT-INPUT KEYSTONE — Maya logs Wonder with completedDate + pages=235 → programme_progress auto-upserts books_read 2→3, pages_read 313→548 inside the tx**; R5 teacher POST log → 403 (only students); R6 review flow (student POST → 201 / duplicate → 400 / student PATCH own → 200 / principal hide → 200 isApproved=false / teacher GET filters hidden out / admin sees all / unhide → 200); R7 reading list lifecycle (POST creates draft → ?includeUnpublished=true reveals → add 2 items + duplicate catch → PATCH publish stamps published_at → default GET shows it → PATCH unpublish clears published_at via lockstep); R8 permission denials (student POST /programmes 403 personType-guard, student POST /reading-lists 403, student PATCH /reviews/:id/hide 403). Cleanup wholesale DELETE in dependency order + `pnpm seed:library` re-run restores post-Step-4 seed shape exactly (locations=3 / items=5 / copies=11 / checkouts=3 / holds=1 / fines=1 / programmes=1 / progress=1 (2/313) / logs=2 / lists=1 / list_items=3 / reviews=1). **Reviewer attention items** (non-blocking, Phase 2 polish, documented in the CAT): `lib.fine.issued` has no consumer (Cycle 6 payment integration is a polish item gated on a `payment_integration_enabled` school-config flag); Librarian role is school-admin tier today (joins Wave 2 Phase 2 punch list with the Counsellor / Nurse role splits from Cycles 9–11); self-service hold placement gated on lib-002:read (the `hasLibrarianScope` service-layer check is the actual cross-patron access boundary, mirrors Cycle 1 att-001:write self-service pattern); 6 deferred ERD tables (lib_recommendations / lib_class_set_checkouts / lib_interlibrary_loans / lib_catalogue_import_jobs / lib_scan_sessions / lib_space*\*) park as Cycle 12.1 / Wave 3; `lib.programme.completed` emit + completion certificate worker schema-ready but unused; parent `/children/[id]/library` surface is a polish item. **Cycle 12 ships clean to the post-cycle architecture review.** Tagged `cycle12-complete` after the closeout commit + CI green. **REVIEW-CYCLE12 Round 1 fixes (2026-05-05):** (BLOCKING 1) `CheckoutService.getById(id, actor?)` now applies row scope when actor is supplied — librarians/admins see any checkout in tenant; patrons see only their own; non-owners get a 404 don't-leak-existence. The actor-less overload remains for internal post-mutation reloads. Controller resolves the actor and passes it through. Verified live: Maya GETs own → 200, Maya GETs Ethan's → 404, Principal GETs Ethan's → 200. (BLOCKING 2) Identical pattern on `HoldService.getById(id, actor?)` + controller. Verified live with same shape. (BLOCKING 3) `CopyService.lookupByBarcode(barcode, actor?)` splits the response by librarian scope — catalogue-only readers see `activeCheckout: null` regardless of whether the copy is on loan, plus a new `isCheckedOut: boolean` (derived from `copy.is_available`) so they can render an "On loan" badge without learning who has it. Librarians (lib-002:write) and admins still get the full circulation-desk shape with `activeCheckout` populated. `BarcodeLookupResponseDto` gains `isCheckedOut`; controller resolves actor and passes through; OpenAPI summary documents the split. Verified live across 4 personas: librarian sees `patronName=Maya Chen + dueDate`; student/parent/teacher all see `activeCheckout=None + isCheckedOut=true`. (BLOCKING 4) Cross-tenant patron validation — `resolvePatronType` STUDENT branch now joins through current-tenant `sis_students` (`SELECT 1 FROM sis_students s JOIN platform.platform_students ps ON ps.id = s.platform_student_id WHERE ps.person_id = $1`) so a person who exists in `platform.platform_students` from a different school cannot be checked out as a student in this tenant; STAFF branch already tenant-scoped via `hr_employees`. New shared `assertPatronInCurrentTenant(personId)` helper called from `CheckoutService.checkout()` and `HoldService.placeHold()` cross-patron path; `HoldService` now injects `CheckoutService` to reuse the helper. Self-service hold (default `actor.personId`) is implicitly tenant-scoped because the Auth + Tenant guard chain proved the actor belongs. Verified live: Platform Admin person_id (in `iam_person`, NOT in `tenant_demo.sis_students`/`hr_employees`) → 400 on both checkout + hold-on-behalf; self-service hold and valid librarian-on-behalf both still 201. **3 MAJOR follow-ups carried to Wave 2 Phase 2 punch list** (per reviewer's gate decision — pre-pilot role-model hardening): teacher review-moderation scope (`hasModeratorScope` treats any non-student lib-003:write holder as moderator — should split to `LIB-005:moderate` or constrain to class-scoped reviews); STAFF reading-log breadth (`?studentId=` allows any lib-003:read holder to read any student's log — should require librarian scope); `HoldService.placeHold` duplicate race (check-then-insert without partial UNIQUE or advisory lock — small race window, worst case is 2 PENDING holds the librarian cancels). All three join the broader Counsellor / Nurse / Librarian role-split punch list (Cycles 9 + 10 + 11 + 12). Tagged `cycle12-approved` after the Round 2 verdict.

**Cycle 11.1 (Wellbeing Check-Ins) is COMPLETE + APPROVED at `6d2b04c` (REVIEW-CYCLE11.1-CHATGPT — final verdict). Round 1 against pre-`a5abe4e` `main` was misdirected (reviewer pulled stale `origin/main` before the closeout commit was pushed; reviewed Phase 2 Parent Polish state instead — 2 BLOCKINGs verified spurious, 2 real Phase 2 MAJORs fixed in `b58e591`). Round 2 against `b58e591` returned REJECT pending 2 BLOCKING + 4 MAJOR on the actual Cycle 11.1 surface; all 6 fixes landed in `6d2b04c` with live verification on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-05. Round 3 against `6d2b04c` returned APPROVED — final gate decision.** Tagged `cycle11.1-complete` on `a5abe4e` (the original closeout commit + CAT) and `cycle11.1-approved` on `6d2b04c` (the Round 2 fix commit, after Round 3 APPROVED). Round 2 fixes: (BLOCKING 1 — privacy) `CheckinService.list` + `getById` strip `flaggedForFollowUp` + `assignedCounselorId` + `assignedCounselorName` for STUDENT actors via the new `stripCheckinForStudent` helper — students never see follow-up flags or caseload assignments through the API even though the seeded check-in actually has `flagged=true` in the DB; counsellor + admin still see the truth. (BLOCKING 2 — validation) New `assertResponseShape(question, numeric, text)` helper in the submit pre-tx loop enforces per-question-type shape + range: `YES_NO` numeric in {0,1}; `SCALE_1_5` + `EMOJI_SCALE` integer in [1,5]; `SCALE_1_10` integer in [1,10]; `FREE_TEXT` non-blank text only; text rejected on numeric types and vice versa. (MAJOR 3 — teacher row-level access denied) `CheckinService.list` now 403s non-admin / non-counsellor STAFF with the "aggregated wellbeing trends only — the per-check-in list is restricted to the counselling team" message; the previous strip-and-return path leaked counts / completed-vs-pending timing / `flagged=true` filter cardinality even though identity-bearing fields were scrubbed; the unused `stripCheckinForTeacher` helper was removed. (MAJOR 4 — count-match validation) New `assertAllStudentsExist` + `assertAllClassesExist` helpers on `DeploymentService.create()` pre-flight every supplied `CUSTOM_LIST` student id + `CLASS` class id against the tenant before persisting the deployment; mismatches return 400 with the missing ids inlined. Belt-and-braces inside `resolveAudience` at activate time too. (MAJOR 5 — SCHOOL admin-only) Locked product/security decision: `target_type='SCHOOL'` requires school-admin authorisation at both create + activate — counsellors with `cou-004:write` are 403'd at create with the redirect message ("Counsellors should target CASELOAD, CLASS, or CUSTOM_LIST instead"). (MAJOR 6 — schema-side dedup) Tenant migration `042_svc_wellbeing_checkins_dedup.sql` adds partial `UNIQUE INDEX svc_wellbeing_checkins_deployment_student_uq ON (deployment_id, student_id) WHERE deployment_id IS NOT NULL` — caps check-in rows at one per (deployment, student) pair while ad-hoc check-ins (NULL deployment) intentionally allow multiples. The activate keystone's row lock already prevents double-activation; the index is the schema-side belt-and-braces against future backfill / repair / manual insert paths. **Open follow-up carried to Wave 2 Phase 2 backlog:** dedicated aggregate-trend endpoint for teachers (`GET /counselling/wellbeing/trends`) returning pre-aggregated per-template completion counts + anonymised per-domain mean scores — gives teachers something useful without re-introducing row-level access. Vertical-slice CAT at `docs/cycle11.1-cat-script.md` verified live on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-05; all 10 plan scenarios pass; both ADR-057 wire envelopes captured live (`svc.wellbeing.alert.created` × 2 — one with `autoEscalate=false` for Maya's WANTS_TO_TALK, one with `autoEscalate=true` for Ethan's SELF_HARM_INDICATOR — the unconditional auto-escalation keystone).** Final Cycle 11.1 totals: **6 svc*wellbeing*\* base tables** (tenant base table count 169 → **175**); **13 intra-tenant FKs** (4 + 9); **0 cross-schema FKs**; **20 endpoints** across 4 services + 4 controllers; **1 Kafka emit topic** (`svc.wellbeing.alert.created`); IAM catalogue stays at **450** (Cycle 11.1 reuses the existing COU-004 entry — `:read` granted to teachers / counsellors / admin / **students** for own check-ins per the row-scope filter, parents intentionally NOT granted; `:write` granted only to counsellors + admin); **3 student-facing web routes** (the first student-input surface in CampusOS) + **4 counsellor web routes** + **18 React Query hooks**. Tagged `cycle11.1-complete` after CI green.** Plan at `docs/campusos-cycle11.1-implementation-plan.html`; handoff at `HANDOFF-CYCLE11.1.md`. Cycle 11.1 ships the 6 wellbeing check-in tables deferred from Cycle 11 (M27 Student Services Domain 5) and introduces the **first student-input surface in CampusOS** — a dedicated student check-in completion flow at `/wellbeing`. The vertical slice: counsellor creates a "Weekly Emotional Check-In" template with 5 questions across Emotional + Safety domains → deploys to Maya's caseload (CASELOAD targeting) → platform generates a PENDING check-in for Maya → Maya submits responses (Safety score=2, "Do you want to talk?"=YES) → application logic flags the check-in for follow-up and creates a WANTS_TO_TALK alert with a 5-type enum (FEELS_UNSAFE / WANTS_TO_TALK / SIGNIFICANT_SCORE_DROP / PERSISTENT_LOW_SCORE / SELF_HARM_INDICATOR) → counsellor triages the alert → admin views the school-wide wellbeing dashboard. **SELF\*HARM\*INDICATOR auto-escalates to administrators** via the `svc.wellbeing.alert.created` Kafka emit (the unconditional auto-escalation is not configurable by the school). Permission gate: new `COU-004` function (read + write + admin tiers); `:read` granted to teachers (aggregated trends only — service strips individual data), counsellors / admin (full detail), and students (own check-ins / own responses only); parents are NOT granted COU-004 — wellbeing data is student-counsellor confidential. **Step 1 lands `packages/database/prisma/tenant/migrations/039_svc_wellbeing_templates.sql`** with 3 logical base tables: `svc_wellbeing_survey_templates` (counsellor-authored reusable survey definitions; 4-value `frequency_recommendation` CHECK DAILY/WEEKLY/MONTHLY/AS_NEEDED; `is_active` flag for soft deactivation; UNIQUE(school_id, name); `created_by` FK to `hr_employees(id)` NO ACTION because templates carry audit value beyond the authoring counsellor leaving — admin must reassign or archive before the employee row can be removed); `svc_wellbeing_questions` (ordered questions per template; 5-value `question_type` CHECK SCALE_1_5/SCALE_1_10/YES_NO/FREE_TEXT/EMOJI_SCALE; 5-value `domain` CHECK ACADEMIC/SOCIAL/EMOTIONAL/PHYSICAL/SAFETY; CASCADE on parent template delete since question rows are meaningless without their template — the safe path is template deactivation via `is_active=false`; **the SAFETY domain is the trigger surface for the Step 5 alert evaluation** — SAFETY+SCALE_1_5 with `numeric_response<=1` fires SELF_HARM_INDICATOR, SAFETY+YES_NO with `numeric_response=1` fires WANTS_TO_TALK); `svc_wellbeing_deployments` (per-deployment instance targeting a student audience; 5-value `target_type` CHECK CASELOAD/CLASS/YEAR_GROUP/SCHOOL/CUSTOM_LIST; nullable `target_ids UUID[]` (null for CASELOAD which auto-targets the deploying counsellor's active caseload at activation time); 4-value `status` CHECK SCHEDULED/ACTIVE/COMPLETED/CANCELLED; multi-column `window_chk` ensures `expires_at > deploy_at` when set; `template_id` FK NO ACTION — refuses hard-delete of a template with historical deployments). **4 new intra-tenant DB-enforced FKs** (3 NO ACTION on `created_by` / `template_id` / `deployed_by` to `hr_employees(id)` and `svc_wellbeing_survey_templates(id)`, 1 CASCADE on questions → templates); 0 cross-schema FKs. **Tenant logical base table count after Step 1: 169 → 172**. **Live verification on `tenant_demo` (single BEGIN…ROLLBACK with savepoints, 16 assertions all green):** T1 happy-path INSERT of 1 template + 2 questions + 1 deployment; T2 frequency_recommendation_chk + T3 question_type_chk + T4 domain_chk + T5 sort_chk + T6 deployment target_type_chk + T7 deployment status_chk + T8 deployment window_chk + T9 deployment targeted_chk all reject BOGUS / out-of-range inputs; T10 UNIQUE(school_id, name) rejects duplicate template; T11 + T12 + T13 FK rejection on bogus template_id (questions + deployments) and bogus created_by; T14 CASCADE on parent template delete drops 2 questions in one statement (before=2, after=0); T15 NO ACTION on `svc_wellbeing_deployments.template_id` refuses template delete while a deployment row references it; T16 `pg_constraint.confdeltype` catalog readout confirms all 4 FK delete actions exactly as documented (NO ACTION × 3, CASCADE × 1). **Splitter `;`-in-string trap caught + fixed pre-provision** — Python state-machine audit flagged 3 stray `;` inside `COMMENT ON ... IS '...'` strings in the first draft (rewritten with periods); thirteenth migration in a row to clear the trap on first attempt **after** audit (Cycles 4–11.1 unbroken streak). Idempotent re-provision verified (zero new applies on second run; tenant base table count stable at 172). Both `tenant_demo` and `tenant_test` provisioned cleanly. **Step 2 lands `packages/database/prisma/tenant/migrations/040_svc_wellbeing_checkins.sql`** with 3 logical base tables completing the M27 Domain 5 schema phase: `svc_wellbeing_checkins` (one row per (student, deployment) check-in instance, or ad-hoc when `deployment_id` is null; `completed_at` NULL means PENDING — the Step 7 student UI renders these as the to-do list; `flagged_for_follow_up BOOLEAN` set by the Step 5 alert evaluation logic; 4 indexes including the partial follow-up queue index `(flagged_for_follow_up, completed_at) WHERE flagged_for_follow_up=true`); `svc_wellbeing_responses` (per-question response within a check-in; UNIQUE(checkin_id, question_id) means each question gets exactly one answer per check-in; `numeric_response SMALLINT` carries SCALE_1_5 / SCALE_1_10 / EMOJI_SCALE / YES_NO mapped to 1/0; `text_response TEXT` carries FREE_TEXT; `response_shape_chk` requires at least one of the two columns to be populated; CASCADE on parent check-in delete; NO ACTION on question — the Step 4 service blocks question delete app-side, the schema NO ACTION is the safety net); `svc_wellbeing_alerts` (5-type alert system with `alert_type` 5-value CHECK FEELS_UNSAFE/WANTS_TO_TALK/SIGNIFICANT_SCORE_DROP/PERSISTENT_LOW_SCORE/SELF_HARM_INDICATOR; `status` 4-value CHECK NEW/ACKNOWLEDGED/IN_PROGRESS/RESOLVED; **multi-column `acknowledged_chk` keystone strictly pins lockstep** — NEW requires both ack columns NULL, any non-NEW status requires both NOT NULL; the Step 5 service stamps both atomically on the NEW → ACKNOWLEDGED transition; partial INDEX `(status, created_at DESC) WHERE status IN ('NEW','ACKNOWLEDGED','IN_PROGRESS')` for the open-alerts queue; SELF_HARM_INDICATOR auto-escalates to administrators via the Step 5 `svc.wellbeing.alert.created` Kafka emit). **9 new intra-tenant DB-enforced FKs** across the 3 tables (CASCADE × 4 on student/checkin/student/response chains; NO ACTION × 2 on template/question to preserve audit; SET NULL × 3 on deployment_id, assigned_counselor_id, acknowledged_by); 0 cross-schema FKs. **Tenant logical base table count after Step 2: 172 → 175**. **Cycle 11.1 schema phase complete: 6 tables across migrations 039 + 040, 13 intra-tenant FKs (4 + 9), 0 cross-schema FKs.\*\* **Live verification on `tenant_demo` (single BEGIN…ROLLBACK with savepoints, 17 assertions all green):** T1 happy-path multi-table insert (template + questions + deployment + checkin + responses + alert); T2 response\*shape_chk rejects empty response; T3 text-only response accepted (FREE_TEXT shape); T4 alert_type_chk + T5 status_chk reject BOGUS; T6 acknowledged_chk rejects NEW with ack populated (caught a bug in the first draft — original predicate was `(status='NEW') OR (...)` which accepted NEW-with-ack because the first branch passed; tightened to strict lockstep `(status='NEW' AND ack IS NULL AND at IS NULL) OR (status<>'NEW' AND ack IS NOT NULL AND at IS NOT NULL)` and added an idempotent `ALTER TABLE DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS / ADD CONSTRAINT` tail block — splitter-safe pattern matching the Cycle 11 Step 3 BIP caseload_id FK backfill); T7 acknowledged_chk rejects ACKNOWLEDGED without ack; T8 NEW → ACKNOWLEDGED happy path with both fields stamped; T9 UNIQUE(checkin_id, question_id) rejects duplicate response; T10/T11/T12 FK rejection on bogus checkins.student_id / responses.checkin_id / responses.question_id; T13 NO ACTION on question delete refused while a response exists; T14 NO ACTION on template delete refused while a check-in exists; **T15 CASCADE chain verified end-to-end** — DELETE check-in drops 2 responses; the alert referencing the safety response also drops via response CASCADE (responses before=2, after=0; alerts after=0); T16 SET NULL on deployment delete preserves check-in (`deployment_id=NULL`); T17 `pg_constraint.confdeltype` catalog readout confirms all 9 FK delete actions exactly (CASCADE × 4, NO ACTION × 2, SET NULL × 3). **Splitter `;`-in-string trap not tripped — fourteenth migration in a row to clear the trap on first attempt** (Cycles 4–11.1 unbroken streak). SELF_HARM_INDICATOR takes precedence over FEELS_UNSAFE on the same response so a single SAFETY+SCALE_1_5+numeric=1 row creates one alert with autoEscalate=true, not two. REVIEW-CYCLE11 fixes (2026-05-05):** (BLOCKING 1 — privacy) `ReferralService.buildVisibility` was returning the union `(assigned-to-me OR unassigned-SUBMITTED-triage-queue OR own-submitted)` for every STAFF actor with `employeeId`, leaking the unassigned-triage queue (and its referral reason text) to teachers who hold `cou-002:read` but not `cou-001:write`. The helper is now `async` and branches on `hasCounsellorScope(actor)` — counsellors see assigned + queue + own; non-counsellor STAFF see own-submitted only. Verified live: teacher 403 / 404 on every triage-queue path; counsellor + VP + admin still see the queue. (BLOCKING 2 — row scope) `SessionService.create` accepted `input.counselorId` without verifying it equaled `actor.employeeId`; `patch` / `addParticipant` / `markAttendance` only checked counsellor scope and could mutate any other counsellor's session by UUID. Now: non-admin counsellors must own the row (or the parent session for participant updates) — admin bypass; check runs inside the same locking tx as the UPDATE on `patch` so a race cannot bypass it. Verified live with 7 scenarios (Hayes attempts on Park's session 403 × 4; Park-on-own + admin override 200 × 3). (MAJOR 4 — MTSS tier caseload validation) New `assertActorOwnsStudent(actor, studentId)` helper rejects non-admin counsellors writing tiers for students not on their own active caseload. Called from `create` + inside the `patch` lock. Verified live: Hayes-on-Ethan 403 × 2; admin POST + Hayes-on-Maya 200 × 2. (MAJOR 5 — intervention caseload row-scope) Two new helpers `assertActorOwnsTier` + `assertActorOwnsIntervention` called from every read + mutation in `InterventionService`. Verified live: Hayes-on-Ethan 403 × 4; admin / Hayes-on-Maya 200 × 3. (MAJOR 6 — mandated reporter Swagger) `MandatoryReportController.create` `@ApiOperation` summary rewritten to spell out the locked product/security decision — every employee is a mandated reporter under FERPA + state CPS statutes; the legal duty runs against any student a staff member knows of. No code change; read scope on list / getById remains conservative. See `REVIEW-CYCLE11-CHATGPT.md` for the triage table + before/after + verification trail. **All 5 fix paths verified live.** Original Round 1 cycle status preserved below: vertical-slice CAT at `docs/cycle11-cat-script.md` verified live on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-05; all 10 plan scenarios pass; both ADR-057 wire envelopes captured (`svc.referral.created` from S1 + `svc.tier.changed` from S6 with reason=CREATED + oldTier=null).** Final Cycle 11 totals: **14 svc\_\* base tables** + 1 cross-cycle FK backfill on Cycle 9's `svc_behavior_plans.caseload_id` — tenant base table count 155 → **169**; **32 intra-tenant FKs** (8 Step 1 + 21 Step 2 + 3 Step 3); **0 cross-schema FKs**; **48 endpoints** across 10 services (4 from Step 5 + 2 from Step 6 + 4 from Step 7); **2 Kafka emit topics** (`svc.referral.created`, `svc.tier.changed`); **1 dedicated FERPA permission gate** (`student_counseling_record:read` granted only to admin / principal / vp / counsellor — 4 of 7 personas); **8 web routes + 47 React Query hooks**; **3 partial UNIQUE keystones** (caseload primary, caseload (counsellor, student, year) active, MTSS tier (student, year, domain) active) + **1 multi-column lockstep CHECK** (`locked_chk` on session notes — irreversible by service-side discipline + no unlock endpoint by design) + **1 immutability invariant** (mandatory report core fields after FILED enforced via DTO whitelist + service-side defence-in-depth) + **1 intersection gate** (coordinated care notes require BOTH `hlt-001:read` AND `cou-007:read` — neither alone unlocks). IAM catalogue 447 → **450** (149 + 1 functions × 3 tiers; `student_counseling_record` is the only catalogue code intentionally not following the XXX-NNN convention). Cycle 11 completes the M27 Student Services module that Cycle 9 began; the cross-cycle keystone is the Step 3 BIP `caseload_id` FK backfill which converts Cycle 9's forward-compatible soft ref into a real DB-enforced FK ON DELETE SET NULL — verified end-to-end via Step 4 seed UPDATE that links Maya's seeded BIP to her seeded caseload + the CAT S3 readout shows `linkedBipId` inlined on the caseload detail. Tagged `cycle11-complete` after CI green. Cycle 11 ships 16 core tables across 4 domains (caseloads + referrals, sessions + FERPA notes, MTSS/RTI tiers + interventions, coordinated care + mandatory reporting); the 6 wellbeing check-in tables defer to Cycle 11.1. Cycle 11 is the **third cycle of Wave 2 (Student Services)** and the **second-highest sensitivity domain** after Cycle 10 Health: `svc_session_notes` is FERPA-protected counselling content gated on a dedicated `student_counseling_record:read` permission that teachers and parents NEVER hold; `svc_mandatory_reports` core fields are immutable once past FILED. Vertical-slice deliverable: teacher submits referral for Maya → counsellor (Hayes) triages and accepts → caseload created with primary\*concern=SOCIAL_EMOTIONAL → counsellor logs FERPA-protected session note → assigns Maya to MTSS Tier 2 BEHAVIORAL with Social Skills Group intervention → logs progress data point → nurse + counsellor exchange coordinated care note → admin views MTSS dashboard → teacher sees only that referral was accepted (no session content). Plan at `docs/campusos-cycle11-implementation-plan.html`; handoff at `HANDOFF-CYCLE11.md`. **Step 1 lands `packages/database/prisma/tenant/migrations/036_svc_caseloads_referrals.sql`** with 4 logical base tables: `svc_caseloads` (one row per (counsellor, student) assignment for an academic year; 7-value `primary_concern` CHECK ACADEMIC/BEHAVIORAL/SOCIAL_EMOTIONAL/ATTENDANCE/CRISIS/TRANSITION/GENERAL; 3-value `status` CHECK ACTIVE/CLOSED/TRANSFERRED; **two partial UNIQUE keystones** — `svc_caseloads_primary_active_uq` on `(student_id, academic_year_id) WHERE status='ACTIVE' AND is_primary_counselor=true` pinning exactly one primary counsellor per (student, year), and `svc_caseloads_active_assignment_uq` on `(counselor_id, student_id, academic_year_id) WHERE status='ACTIVE'` pinning no duplicate active assignments; the Cycle 9 `svc_behavior_plans.caseload_id` soft ref will be tightened into a real FK by the Step 3 migration); `svc_referral_types` (per-school referral category catalogue with 4-value `default_priority` CHECK + `requires_parent_notification` flag + UNIQUE(school_id, name)); `svc_referrals` (one row per teacher-to-counsellor referral; 7-value `status` CHECK SUBMITTED/TRIAGED/ACCEPTED/IN_PROGRESS/COMPLETED/DECLINED/CANCELLED; 4-value `priority` CHECK; nullable `assigned_counselor_id` so freshly submitted referrals sit unassigned in the triage queue; `assigned_counselor_id → hr_employees(id) ON DELETE SET NULL` so a counsellor leaving the school returns the referral to the unassigned state for re-triage; INDEXes covering admin queue / counsellor queue / student lookup / teacher's-own-submitted views); `svc_referral_activity` (IMMUTABLE per ADR-010 — service-side discipline, no UPDATE / no DELETE methods exposed at the service layer; 6-value `activity_type` CHECK STATUS_CHANGE/ASSIGNMENT_CHANGE/NOTE_ADDED/PARENT_NOTIFIED/ESCALATED/EXTERNAL_CONTACT_MADE; CASCADE on parent referral so an emergency hard-delete takes the audit with it — mirrors Cycle 8 `tkt_ticket_activity` and Cycle 10 `hlth_health_access_log`). **8 new intra-tenant DB-enforced FKs** (5 NO ACTION on counsellor / academic_year / referred_by / referral_type, 2 CASCADE on student_id and parent referral, 1 SET NULL on assigned_counselor); 0 cross-schema FKs. **Tenant logical base table count after Step 1: 155 → 159**. **Live verification on `tenant_demo` (single BEGIN…ROLLBACK transaction with savepoints, 23 assertions all green):** happy-path INSERT across all 4 tables; concern_chk + status_chk + priority_chk × 2 (referral_types + referrals) + activity_type_chk all reject BOGUS; **partial UNIQUE primary keystone fires both directions** — rejects 2nd primary for (Maya, AY1) AND accepts a non-primary consultant on the same (student, year); **partial UNIQUE active\*assignment keystone fires** — rejects 2nd row for (counsellor, student, year) even when is_primary_counselor=false; **CLOSED status releases the primary slot** — UPDATE Hayes's primary to CLOSED then INSERT new primary for Maya in AY1 succeeds with final state `(active=2, closed=1)`; lifecycle UPDATE SUBMITTED → TRIAGED → ACCEPTED with `assigned_counselor_id` populated atomically; FK rejection on bogus counselor_id, academic_year_id, referred_by, referral_type_id; `svc_referral_types` UNIQUE(school_id, name) rejects duplicate; **NO ACTION on referral_types delete with referencing referral fires** (admin must deactivate via is_active=false); all 6 activity_type values accepted in one block; **CASCADE on referral delete drops 9 audit rows in one statement** — IMMUTABLE-but-CASCADE-on-parent shape verified. All 8 FK delete actions confirmed via `pg_constraint.confdeltype` catalog readout for the tenant_demo schema (NO ACTION × 5, CASCADE × 2, SET NULL × 1). Splitter `;`-in-string trap not tripped — tenth migration in a row to clear the trap on first attempt (Cycles 4–11 unbroken streak). Idempotent re-provision verified (zero new applies on second run; tenant base table count stable). Both `tenant_demo` and `tenant_test` provisioned cleanly. **Step 2 lands `packages/database/prisma/tenant/migrations/037_svc_sessions_mtss.sql`** — the largest schema migration of Cycle 11 (8 tables in one file). `svc_sessions` (per-counsellor session log; 6-value `session_type` CHECK INDIVIDUAL/GROUP/CRISIS/CHECK_IN/PARENT_MEETING/CONSULTATION; 4-value `status` CHECK SCHEDULED/COMPLETED/NO_SHOW/CANCELLED; nullable `primary_caseload_id` SET NULL identifies the student for INDIVIDUAL sessions while GROUP leaves it null with participants in the next table; nullable `referral_id` SET NULL preserves session log past referral cleanup); `svc_session_participants` (links students to sessions; 3-value attendance_status CHECK ATTENDED/NO_SHOW/LATE; UNIQUE(session_id, student_id) so a student appears at most once per session); **`svc_session_notes` — FERPA-protected counselling record** (UNIQUE(session_id, student_id) so a GROUP session carries one note per participant; **multi-column `locked_chk` keystone** pins the lock state to one of two shapes — unlocked requires is_locked=false AND locked_at NULL AND locked_by NULL; locked requires all three set; once locked the Step 6 PATCH endpoint refuses any update with 400 — there is no unlock endpoint by design; the Step 6 SessionNoteService gates every read on `student_counseling_record:read` granted only to Staff and Admin); `svc_mtss_tiers` (per-(student, year, domain) MTSS/RTI tier; 3-value tier CHECK TIER_1/TIER_2/TIER_3; 4-value domain CHECK ACADEMIC/BEHAVIORAL/SOCIAL_EMOTIONAL/ATTENDANCE; 4-value status CHECK ACTIVE/EXITED/PROMOTED/DEMOTED; **partial UNIQUE on `(student_id, academic_year_id, domain) WHERE status='ACTIVE'`** pinning exactly one active tier per (student, year, domain); the Step 7 MtssTierService emits `svc.tier.changed` on every status flip and tier value change); `svc_interventions` (per-tier targeted support; 6-value intervention_type CHECK ACADEMIC_SUPPORT/BEHAVIORAL_SUPPORT/SOCIAL_EMOTIONAL_LEARNING/ATTENDANCE_SUPPORT/COUNSELING/EXTERNAL_SERVICE; 3-value status CHECK ACTIVE/COMPLETED/DISCONTINUED; multi-column `dates_chk` enforces end_date >= start_date when both set); `svc_intervention_progress` (append-only progress monitoring; NUMERIC(8,2) score + benchmark for the Step 9 time-series chart; the Step 7 InterventionService.logProgress is the only writer); `svc_mtss_team_meetings` (RTI team review; nullable soft `meeting_id` ref to the future `mtg_meetings` table — no DB FK because the target does not exist yet); `svc_mtss_team_meeting_students` (links a meeting to the students reviewed at it; 5-value outcome CHECK NO_CHANGE/TIER_UP/TIER_DOWN/EXIT/CONTINUE_WITH_ADJUSTMENT or NULL while in-progress; UNIQUE(team_meeting_id, student_id)). **21 new intra-tenant DB-enforced FKs** (7 NO ACTION on counselor / academic_year / assigned_by / facilitated_by / recorded_by, 9 CASCADE chain on session+student+intervention+tier+team_meeting children, 5 SET NULL on caseload / referral / locked_by / provider / tier on team_meeting_students); 0 cross-schema FKs. **Tenant logical base table count after Step 2: 159 → 167\*\*. Cycle 11 running tally: 12 logical svc** tables, 29 intra-tenant FKs (8 from Step 1 + 21 from Step 2). **Live verification on `tenant_demo` (single BEGIN…ROLLBACK with savepoints, 35 assertions all green):\*\* all 7 enum CHECKs reject BOGUS (session\*type, session status, attendance, mtss tier, mtss domain, mtss status, intervention type, outcome); duration_chk rejects 0; intervention dates_chk rejects end<start; **all 4 multi-column `locked_chk` mismatch directions fire** (UPDATE is_locked=true with locked_at NULL; UPDATE is_locked=true+locked_at populated with locked_by NULL; INSERT is_locked=false with locked_at populated; happy path UPDATE all 3 set together accepted); all 6 session_type + 4 session status values accepted in single inserts; **partial UNIQUE active_uq fires both directions** — rejects 2nd ACTIVE BEHAVIORAL tier for same (student, year) AND accepts different domain (ACADEMIC) on same (student, year); **EXITED status releases the BEHAVIORAL slot** — UPDATE BEHAVIORAL to EXITED then INSERT new TIER_3 BEHAVIORAL ACTIVE succeeds with final `(active=1, exited=1)`; partial INDEX query path on sessions (primary_caseload_id WHERE NOT NULL) verified; UNIQUE(session, student) on participants + UNIQUE(session, student) on notes + UNIQUE(meeting, student) on team_meeting_students all reject duplicates; **CASCADE chains verified end-to-end** — DELETE session drops both notes + participants in one statement; DELETE tier drops interventions which cascades through to progress; **SET NULL on caseload delete** clears session.primary_caseload_id without dropping the session; DELETE team_meeting drops all student rows. All 21 FK delete actions confirmed via `pg_constraint.confdeltype` (NO ACTION × 7, CASCADE × 9, SET NULL × 5). Splitter `;`-in-string trap not tripped — eleventh migration in a row to clear the trap (Cycles 4–11 unbroken). Idempotent re-provision verified (zero new applies on second run). One iteration issue caught (test-data only): smoke initially used `:RIVERA` (an `hr_employees.id`) where a `sis_students.id` was needed — rewrote to use Ethan Rodriguez and re-ran cleanly. **Step 3 lands `packages/database/prisma/tenant/migrations/038_svc_coordinated_care_mandatory.sql`** with 2 logical base tables + 1 FK backfill on the existing `svc_behavior_plans`. `svc_coordinated_care_notes` (shared observation thread between nurse and counsellor teams; 2-value `author_role` CHECK NURSE/COUNSELLOR; soft `author_person_id` to platform.iam_person; CASCADE on student delete; the Step 7 CoordinatedCareService gates every read on the **intersection** of `hlt-001:read` AND `cou-007:read` — neither permission alone unlocks it; teachers and parents always 403). `svc_mandatory_reports` (CPS-filing log; 4-value `report_type` CHECK SUSPECTED_ABUSE/SUSPECTED_NEGLECT/IMMINENT_DANGER/OTHER; 4-value `status` CHECK FILED/CPS_CONTACTED/INVESTIGATION_ACTIVE/CLOSED; **NO ACTION on student delete enforces retention** mirroring Cycle 10 hlth_health_access_log; `supporting_docs_s3_keys TEXT[]` for evidentiary documents; **IMMUTABLE core fields after FILED** — service-side discipline; the Step 7 service refuses any PATCH that touches description / report_type / reported_to_authority / report_date with 400; only cps_response and status are mutable). **Cycle 9 BIP `caseload_id` FK backfill — keystone cross-cycle integration:** `ALTER TABLE svc_behavior_plans DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS svc_behavior_plans_caseload_id_fkey; ALTER TABLE svc_behavior_plans ADD CONSTRAINT … FOREIGN KEY (caseload_id) REFERENCES svc_caseloads(id) ON DELETE SET NULL`. The Cycle 9 column was forward-compatibly declared as a soft UUID ref because svc_caseloads did not exist yet; Cycle 11 Step 1 introduced svc_caseloads; Step 3 tightens the ref into a real DB-enforced FK. The DROP IF EXISTS + ADD pattern is splitter-safe (no DO blocks with embedded semicolons) and idempotent. **3 new intra-tenant DB-enforced FKs** (1 CASCADE on care_notes student, 1 NO ACTION on mandatory_reports student for retention, 1 SET NULL on the BIP caseload_id backfill); 0 cross-schema FKs. **Tenant logical base table count after Step 3: 167 → 169**. **Cycle 11 schema phase complete: 14 new tables + 1 FK backfill across migrations 036/037/038, 32 intra-tenant FKs across the cycle (8 + 21 + 3), 0 cross-schema FKs.\*\* **Live verification on `tenant_demo` (single BEGIN…ROLLBACK with savepoints, 16 assertions all green):** role_chk + report_type_chk + report_status_chk all reject BOGUS (T3 also rejects `TEACHER` for author_role — counsellor/nurse intersection is also a schema invariant); FK rejection on bogus student_id; lifecycle UPDATE FILED → CPS_CONTACTED with cps_response populated; supporting_docs_s3_keys array round-trip with cardinality=2; **NO ACTION on mandatory_reports student delete verified via `pg_constraint.confdeltype='a'`**; **BIP caseload_id FK backfill verified end-to-end** — T14 rejects bogus caseload_id; **T15 keystone**: INSERT real caseload + BIP with caseload_id populated → DELETE caseload → re-read BIP shows `caseload_id=NULL` confirming the SET NULL action fired (the BIP row preserved while the caseload was hard-deleted, exactly the documented Cycle 9-to-Cycle 11 cross-cycle integration); CASCADE on care_notes student delete verified via catalog. Splitter `;`-in-string trap not tripped — twelfth migration in a row (Cycles 4–11 unbroken). Idempotent re-provision verified on both tenants — the DROP IF EXISTS + ADD pattern correctly drops then re-adds the FK on each run without erroring. Academic Concern LOW); **1 caseload (Hayes → Maya, primary, SOCIAL_EMOTIONAL, opened 2025-09-15) + the Cycle 9 BIP `caseload_id` UPDATE keystone** — `UPDATE svc_behavior_plans SET caseload_id = <new caseload id> WHERE student_id = Maya AND status='ACTIVE' AND caseload_id IS NULL` lands 1 row updated, completing the cross-cycle integration the Cycle 9 plan documented (the previously-soft column now points at a real caseload row, the Step 3 FK enforces it, the Step 5 BIP UI will surface the linked counsellor + concern); 1 referral (Rivera→Maya, Social/Emotional, MEDIUM, ACCEPTED with assigned_counselor=Hayes, parent_notified=true) + 1 STATUS_CHANGE activity row; 2 sessions (Session 1 INDIVIDUAL COMPLETED 45min ~3 weeks ago linked to caseload + referral; Session 2 CHECK_IN COMPLETED 15min ~1 week ago) + 2 session notes (Note 1 with goals_addressed=['Peer relationship building','Social skills development'], follow_up_required=true; Note 2 brief follow-up; both is_locked=false so the Step 6 lock path can be exercised against fresh data); 1 MTSS Tier (Maya TIER_2 BEHAVIORAL ACTIVE, assigned by Hayes 2025-10-01, review_date today+30d) + 1 Social Skills Group BEHAVIORAL_SUPPORT intervention 2x/week + 1 progress entry (measure_type='Office Referrals per Week', score=2.00, benchmark=1.00, "Down from 4 at baseline"); 1 coordinated care note from Hayes COUNSELLOR about Maya's anxiety around peer teasing over inhaler use. **`packages/database/data/permissions.json` updated** with the new `student_counseling_record` function (name "Counselling Session Notes (FERPA)", group "Counselling & Student Support") — the **only catalogue code that does not follow the XXX-NNN convention**, intentionally a verbatim FERPA-gate name. Catalogue total: 149 → **150 functions × 3 tiers = 450 permissions**. **`seed-iam.ts` updated:** Teacher gains COU-002:read+write + COU-005:read + COU-006:write (42 → 45 perms cached); Parent gains COU-001:read (22 → 23 — caseload assignment summary only, no notes); Staff gains COU-001..003 read+write + COU-005..007 read+write + **`student_counseling_record:read`** (34 → 47 — Staff covers counsellor + VP + admin assistant); School Admin / Platform Admin pick up the new triplet via `everyFunction` (447 → 450). **Live verification of FERPA gate distribution** via `iam_effective_access_cache`: admin / principal / vp / counsellor hold `student_counseling_record:read=true`; teacher / parent / student hold it false. Exactly the contract — 4 of 7 personas, the 4 who are Staff or Admin. **Live counts on `tenant_demo` after seed**: referral_types=2, caseloads=1, BIP-with-caseload_id=1, referrals=1, activity=1, sessions=2, notes=2, mtss_tiers=1, interventions=1, progress=1, coord_care=1 — all match the plan exactly. getById with inlined `sessionCount` + `lastSessionDate` + **`linkedBipId`** — the keystone cross-cycle integration where Cycle 9's BIP surfaces in the caseload detail via the Step 4 backfill; POST counsellor/admin only via `hasCounsellorScope` (admin OR holds cou-001:write — the canonical counsellor signal in the IAM seed) with partial UNIQUE pre-flight surfacing the conflicting caseload id in 400; PATCH; close stamps closure_reason + closed_at + status=CLOSED in one locked tx so the keystone releases atomically; **per-row manager check** `isRowManager(actor, counselor_id)` returns true when actor.employeeId === counselor_id so counsellors see notes on their own caseloads while teachers viewing class students see them stripped). **`ReferralService`** (8 endpoints under cou-002:read/write — list with row-scope OR predicate (`assigned_counselor_id=me OR (NULL+SUBMITTED queue) OR referred_by=me`) so counsellors see assigned + the unassigned-triage queue while teachers see own submitted; getById with inlined activity timeline; POST stamps referred_by from actor.employeeId + copies default_priority from referral_type + writes initial STATUS_CHANGE activity row inside the same `executeInTenantTransaction` + emits `svc.referral.created` outside the tx; **state-machine transitions** triage / accept / start / complete / decline all use `SELECT ... FOR UPDATE` inside `executeInTenantTransaction` and write activity rows in the same tx; counsellor-only via `hasCounsellorScope`; accept with `openCaseload=true` calls `caseloads.createInternal()` to auto-open a primary caseload AND writes a NOTE_ADDED activity row referencing the new caseload id). **`ReferralActivityService`** (IMMUTABLE per ADR-010 — sole writer is `recordActivity(tx, ...)` called by every state transition inside the same locked tx; no UPDATE / no DELETE methods exposed; CASCADE on parent referral hard-delete mirrors Cycle 8 tkt_ticket_activity + Cycle 10 hlth_health_access_log). **`ReferralTypeService`** (3 endpoints — list with optional includeInactive, admin-only POST + PATCH; `assertActive(id)` used by ReferralService.create to copy default_priority + read requires_parent_notification at submission time). **`seed-iam.ts` follow-on caught during smoke**: Step 4's plan grant for Teacher omitted COU-001:read but the Step 5 visibility contract calls for teachers to see class-scoped caseloads with notes stripped server-side. Added COU-001:read to Teacher (45 → 46 perms cached). **Live verification on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-04 (22 scenarios across 5 personas all green):** caseload row-scope per persona (counsellor/parent/teacher/admin/student); inlined sessionCount + linkedBipId on admin getById confirming the cross-cycle integration; **partial UNIQUE keystone fires both directions** — primary keystone with conflicting id in 400 + (counselor, student, year) keystone catching duplicate active assignments; CLOSED status releases the primary slot atomically; teacher submits referral with priority copied from default + ends in unassigned triage queue; counsellor full lifecycle SUBMITTED → TRIAGED → ACCEPTED → IN_PROGRESS → COMPLETED writing 6 audit rows in chronological order; **service-layer counsellor-scope check fires** — teacher attempts triage → 403 "Only counsellors or admins can triage referrals" because cou-001:write (the canonical counsellor signal) is not held; admin POST referral type happy path + soft-deactivate via PATCH isActive=false; teacher POST caseload 403 (gate); parent GET referrals 403 (gate); student POST referral 403 (gate). **ADR-057 envelope captured live on `dev.svc.referral.created`** with full shape (event_type, event_version=1, source_module='counselling', tenant_id populated, correlation_id, payload includes referralId / sourceRefId / studentId / studentName / referralTypeName / priority / requiresParentNotification / referredById / referredByName / referredByAccountId / reason / status='SUBMITTED'). Validates `studentBelongsToSession` before INSERT (prevents notes for non-participant students). UNIQUE(session_id, student_id) catch surfaces 400 "A session note already exists for this (session, student) pair" — group sessions can carry one note per participant student. **`PATCH /session-notes/:id` refuses any update when `is_locked=true`** with 400 "Note is locked and immutable. Create a follow-up session for additional observations." **`PATCH /session-notes/:id/lock` IRREVERSIBLE** — stamps `is_locked=true` + `locked_at=now()` + `locked_by=actor.employeeId` in one UPDATE inside `executeInTenantTransaction` so the multi-column `locked_chk` schema invariant from Step 2 is satisfied atomically; refuses callers without an `hr_employees` row (Platform Admin `admin@` cannot lock — clinical record per Cycle 10 medication administer convention); refuses double-lock with 400 "Note is already locked"; **no unlock endpoint by design** — to correct an observation the counsellor opens a follow-up session and writes a new note. **Live verification on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-04 (24 scenarios across 5 personas all green):** counsellor GET /sessions returns 2 seeded sessions; admin GET /sessions/:id/notes 200 with goals_addressed array intact; **FERPA gate fires for teacher (S5) / parent (S6) / student (S7) — all 403 at the controller layer**; counsellor PATCH unlocked note text succeeds; **counsellor LOCKs the note → is_locked=true + locked_at + lockedByName='Marcus Hayes' all populated atomically**; **PATCH after lock → 400 "Note is locked and immutable. Create a follow-up session..."** (the keystone immutability invariant); **psql confirms locked_chk lockstep** — all three columns (`is_locked=true has_at=true has_by=true`) in sync; second-lock attempt rejected; UNIQUE(session, student) on duplicate POST → 400; non-participant student → 400 with the studentBelongsToSession validator message; **INDIVIDUAL/GROUP shape validators fire both directions** — INDIVIDUAL without `primaryCaseloadId` → 400; GROUP with `primaryCaseloadId` → 400; counsellor creates GROUP session; adds 2 participants (Maya ATTENDED, Ethan LATE); duplicate participant → 400 "already a participant"; PATCH /session-participants/:id flips Maya to NO_SHOW; GET /sessions/:id returns inlined participants[] with both rows; counsellor POST GROUP session note for Maya (the participant) succeeds; **teacher POST session note → 403** (FERPA gate even with valid participant). Cleanup restores tenant to post-Step-4 seed shape (sessions=2, notes=2 with note1 is_locked=false reset, participants=0). **Cycle 10 (Health & Wellness) is COMPLETE + APPROVED at the closeout commit (REVIEW-CYCLE10-CHATGPT — final verdict). Round 1 against `cycle10-complete` at `e631cce` returned REJECT pending 1 BLOCKING privacy fix on the medication-administration-history endpoint; Round 2 returned APPROVED after the fix landed. All 10 steps shipped + the vertical-slice CAT verified live on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-04 — all 9 plan scenarios pass.** CAT lives at `docs/cycle10-cat-script.md` with 8-check schema preamble + cleanup that restores tenant*demo to the post-Step-4 seed shape. **Final Cycle 10 totals:** 15 hlth\_\* tenant base tables + 1 ADR-030 read model (`sis_student_active_accommodations`) — tenant base table count 139 → **155**; 19 intra-tenant FKs; 0 cross-schema FKs; 48 endpoints (Steps 5/6/7 backend + 1 Step 9 listForStudent); 3 Kafka emit topics (`hlth.medication.administered`, `iep.accommodation.updated`, `hlth.nurse_visit.sent_home`) + 1 consumer (`IepAccommodationConsumer` — the ADR-030 keystone reconciler); 8 web routes (Step 8 nurse dashboard + 6-tab record + visit log + medication checklist; Step 9 IEP editor + screening log + dietary alerts + parent `/children/[id]/health`); 5 multi-column CHECK keystones (`missed_chk`, `signed_chk`, `sent_home_chk`, `applies_to_chk`, `dates_chk`) + 3 partial UNIQUE / partial INDEXes (one ACTIVE plan per student, allergen-alert hot path, follow-up queue); 9-value HIPAA `access_type` enum on `hlth_health_access_log` covering every per-domain read shape with IMMUTABILITY enforced service-side per ADR-010. Tagged `cycle10-complete` after CI green; `cycle10-approved` after the post-cycle review. Cycle 10 is the **second cycle of Wave 2 (Student Services)** and ships the M23 Health module — 14 of the 17 ERD tables in scope (3 telehealth tables deferred). Plus the immutable HIPAA access log brings the total to 15 new tenant base tables across the cycle. Cycle 10 is the **most access-restricted module in the system**: the `hlth**`tables are flagged in the ERD for a separate HIPAA-compliant KMS key. For the dev / demo phase the tables ship without field-level encryption but the access control layer is strict from day one — every read endpoint is gated by a dedicated`health_record:read`permission AND writes a row to`hlth_health_access_log`before the response body leaves the server. Plan at`docs/campusos-cycle10-implementation-plan.html`; handoff at `HANDOFF-CYCLE10.md`. **Step 1 lands `packages/database/prisma/tenant/migrations/032_hlth_health_records.sql`** with 4 logical base tables: `hlth_student_health_records` (one record per student, UNIQUE on student*id; `allergies JSONB NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb` structured array of `{allergen, severity, reaction, notes}` rows; `student_id` FK CASCADE — the conservative privacy choice when a student is removed; `school_id` soft ref to `platform.schools`); `hlth_medical_conditions` (3-value `severity` CHECK MILD/MODERATE/SEVERE; `is_active` flag for soft deactivation so resolved conditions stay in the historical timeline; `management_plan TEXT` is staff-side and the Step 5 service strips it from non-manager payloads; FK CASCADE on `hlth_student_health_records`); `hlth_immunisations` (3-value `status` CHECK CURRENT/OVERDUE/WAIVED; `administered_by TEXT` free-form because parent-supplied immunisation records often reference clinic names that have no `hr_employees` row; FK CASCADE; the Step 5 service rolls up status counts for the school-wide compliance dashboard with WAIVED counting as compliant); `hlth_health_access_log` IMMUTABLE per ADR-010 (no UPDATE, no DELETE — service-side discipline; **9-value `access_type` CHECK** VIEW*RECORD/VIEW_CONDITIONS/VIEW_IMMUNISATIONS/VIEW_MEDICATIONS/VIEW_VISITS/VIEW_IEP/VIEW_SCREENING/VIEW_DIETARY/EXPORT covering every per-domain read; `accessed_by` soft to `platform.platform_users(id)` per ADR-001; `student_id` FK NO ACTION refusing delete of a student who has audit log entries — the immutable audit invariant). **4 new intra-tenant DB-enforced FKs** (3 CASCADE chain — student → record → conditions/immunisations + 1 NO ACTION on access_log → students), 0 cross-schema FKs. **Tenant logical base table count after Step 1: 139 → 143**. **Live verification on `tenant_demo` (single BEGIN…ROLLBACK transaction, 11 assertions all green):** happy-path 4-table multi-insert (Maya A+ blood type with peanut SEVERE allergy JSONB, Dr. Lee physician, Asthma MODERATE condition, DTaP CURRENT immunisation, all 9 access_type values inserted in one statement); UNIQUE(student_id) rejects 2nd health record; severity_chk + immunisation status_chk + access_type_chk all reject BOGUS; FK rejection on bogus health_record_id; **NO ACTION on student delete with audit log entries fires correctly** (the immutable-audit invariant — admin must archive the log first); CASCADE on health_record delete drops conditions + immunisations cleanly. All 4 FK delete actions confirmed via `pg_constraint.confdeltype` catalog readout. Splitter `;`-in-string trap not tripped — seventh migration in a row to clear the trap on first attempt (Cycles 4–10 unbroken streak). Idempotent re-provision verified (zero new applies on second run; tenant base table count stable at 143). Both `tenant_demo` and `tenant_test` provisioned cleanly. **Step 2 lands `packages/database/prisma/tenant/migrations/033_hlth_medications.sql`** with 3 logical base tables for the medication management domain: `hlth_medications` (5-value `route` CHECK ORAL/TOPICAL/INHALER/INJECTION/OTHER; `is_self_administered` flag so the Step 6 nurse dashboard sorts student-carried meds like epinephrine pens out of the daily admin checklist; `frequency TEXT` free-form is the prescribing-physician note while structured times live on the schedule child; `is_active` for soft deactivation; FK CASCADE on `hlth_student_health_records`); `hlth_medication_schedule` (TIME-only `scheduled_time` recurring slot; `day_of_week SMALLINT` nullable with CHECK 0..6 OR NULL — NULL meaning every day, the typical case for daily medications; FK CASCADE on `hlth_medications`); `hlth_medication_administrations` per-dose log with **multi-column `missed_chk` keystone** pinning rows to one of two shapes — active dose requires `was_missed=false AND administered_at NOT NULL AND missed_reason NULL`; missed dose requires `was_missed=true AND administered_at NULL AND missed_reason NOT NULL`. Any other combination is rejected. 5-value `missed_reason` CHECK STUDENT_ABSENT/STUDENT_REFUSED/MEDICATION_UNAVAILABLE/PARENT_CANCELLED/OTHER. `schedule_entry_id` is a deliberate soft ref (no DB-enforced FK) so retiring a slot leaves historical administrations pinned to the medication via `medication_id`. `administered_by` FK SET NULL on `hr_employees` (audit survives a nurse leaving). **Partial INDEX on `(schedule_entry_id, was_missed) WHERE was_missed = true`** — the canonical compliance audit query shape. **4 new intra-tenant FKs** (3 CASCADE chain medication→schedule + admin + record→medication; 1 SET NULL on admin→hr_employees), 0 cross-schema FKs. **Tenant logical base table count after Step 2: 143 → 146**. Cycle 10 running tally: 7 logical base tables, 8 intra-tenant FKs. **Live verification on `tenant_demo` (single BEGIN…ROLLBACK, 16 assertions all green):** route_chk + dow_chk + missed_reason_chk all reject BOGUS; **all 4 missed_chk mismatch directions fire correctly** (active without timestamp; active with reason; missed with timestamp; missed without reason); 5 route values + 5 missed_reason values all accepted in single inserts; NULL day_of_week (every day) coexists with specific day_of_week=3 on the same medication; PRN administration with NULL `schedule_entry_id` accepted; partial-INDEX query path returns 5 missed rows for the slot; schedule delete leaves admin rows intact via the soft-ref convention; CASCADE on medication delete drops schedule + administrations cleanly (admin row count 7 → 0). Splitter trap not tripped — eighth migration in a row across Cycles 4–10. 4-value `status` CHECK DRAFT/ACTIVE/REVIEW/EXPIRED; **partial UNIQUE INDEX on `(student_id) WHERE status <> 'EXPIRED'`** — at most one active/draft/review plan per student while expired plans accumulate as history; case_manager_id FK SET NULL on `hr_employees` so audit survives a counsellor leaving); `hlth_iep_goals` (4-value `status` CHECK ACTIVE/MET/NOT_MET/DISCONTINUED; FK CASCADE on plan); `hlth_iep_goal_progress` (append-only progress audit; FK CASCADE on goal; recorded_by FK SET NULL); `hlth_iep_services` (3-value `delivery_method` CHECK PULL_OUT/PUSH_IN/CONSULT; minutes_chk rejects ≤0; FK CASCADE); `hlth_iep_accommodations` (3-value `applies_to` CHECK ALL_ASSESSMENTS/ALL_ASSIGNMENTS/SPECIFIC; **multi-column `applies_to_chk`** pinning SPECIFIC to a non-empty array AND broad scopes to a NULL array; multi-column `dates_chk`; FK CASCADE — the Step 7 IepPlanService emits `iep.accommodation.updated` on every mutation so the ADR-030 IepAccommodationConsumer upserts `sis_student_active_accommodations` for teachers without ever touching `hlth*\*`); `hlth_nurse_visits`(**soft polymorphic`visited_person_id`+`visited_person_type`CHECK STUDENT/STAFF** — STUDENT references sis_students, STAFF references hr_employees, no DB FK because target table differs by row; 2-value`status`CHECK IN_PROGRESS/COMPLETED; **multi-column`signed_chk`** pinning IN_PROGRESS to NULL signed_out_at and COMPLETED to NOT NULL; **multi-column `sent_home_chk`** pinning sent_home=true to a non-NULL timestamp; **partial INDEX on `(school_id, status) WHERE status='IN_PROGRESS'`** for the live nurse office roster query); `hlth_screenings`(4-value`result`CHECK PASS/REFER/RESCREEN/ABSENT or NULL while pending; FK CASCADE on student; **partial INDEX on`(school_id, follow_up_completed) WHERE follow_up_required=true AND follow_up_completed=false`** for the admin follow-up queue); `hlth_dietary_profiles`(UNIQUE on student_id; structured JSONB allergens; **partial INDEX on`(school_id) WHERE pos_allergen_alert=true`** for the future POS / cafeteria integration). **11 new intra-tenant FKs** (6 CASCADE chain — student/plan/goal child trees; 5 SET NULL on hr_employees refs — case_manager / recorded_by / nurse / screened_by; the visited_person_id soft polymorphic ref deliberately has no DB FK), 0 cross-schema FKs. **Tenant logical base table count after Step 3: 146 → 154**. **Cycle 10 schema phase complete: 15 hlth\_\* tables, 19 intra-tenant FKs across the 3 migrations.** **Live verification on `tenant_demo`(single BEGIN…ROLLBACK with savepoints, 36 assertions all green):** all 7 enum CHECKs reject BOGUS (plan_type, status × 3, delivery, applies_to, visited_type, screening result); **partial UNIQUE keystone fires both directions** — rejects 2nd non-EXPIRED plan + accepts EXPIRED + ACTIVE coexistence; **all 8 multi-column lockstep mismatch directions fire** (applies_to_chk × 3 catching ALL_ASSESSMENTS with array AND SPECIFIC without array AND SPECIFIC with empty array; signed_chk × 2 catching IN_PROGRESS with timestamp AND COMPLETED without; sent_home_chk × 2 catching sent_home=true without timestamp AND sent_home=false with timestamp; dates_chk catching reversed dates); 4 result values + 3 delivery methods + 3 applies_to values all accepted in single inserts; partial-INDEX query paths return correct counts (1 active visit, 1 follow-up REFER, 1 allergen alert); STAFF visit happy path exercises the soft polymorphic ref; UNIQUE on dietary_profiles.student_id rejects duplicates; **plan delete CASCADEs through all 4 child tables in one statement** (goals + goal_progress + accommodations + services drop together). All 11 FK delete actions confirmed via`pg_constraint.confdeltype`. Splitter trap not tripped — ninth migration in a row across Cycles 4–10. IMMUTABLE per ADR-010, no UPDATE/DELETE method; admin-only`GET /health/access-log`paginated audit list).`HealthRecordService`(5 endpoints:`GET /health/students/:studentId`full record with inlined conditions + immunisations + VIEW_RECORD audit;`POST /health/students/:studentId`create record nurse/admin only;`PATCH /health/students/:studentId`field-by-field update;`GET /health/immunisation-compliance`admin school-wide vaccine OVERDUE rollup;`hasNurseScope(actor)`helper =`isSchoolAdmin OR holds hlt-001:write`is the canonical write gate).`ConditionService`(4 endpoints under hlt-001:read/write — list / create / patch / DELETE; emergency_medical_notes kept for evac awareness; physician + management_plan + immunisations all stripped); parent (GUARDIAN) row-scoped via `sis_student_guardians`keyed on actor.personId, receives different strip (full allergy details + immunisations + physician but no management_plan or emergency_medical_notes since those are staff procedural); Iteration issues caught: tenant exempt-path collision (fixed in tenant-resolver.middleware.ts); 4 TS6133 unused imports/parameters; 1 module name collision with the existing system /health controller resolved by naming the new module`HealthRecordsModule`in`health-records.module.ts`(the original`HealthModule`for the system health check stays untouched).`HealthAccessLogService`and`HealthRecordService`are exported from the module so the Step 6 + 7 services can call`recordAccess` for VIEW_MEDICATIONS / VIEW_VISITS / VIEW_IEP / VIEW_SCREENING / VIEW_DIETARY and the row-scope helpers (`assertCanReadStudentExternal`, `loadRecordIdForStudent`, `assertNurseScope`). **Step 6 lands `apps/api/src/health/`medication module** with 3 services + 3 controllers + 10 endpoints + the`hlth.medication.administered`Kafka emit.`MedicationService`(3 endpoints — list with inlined schedule + create + patch under hlt-001:read / hlt-002:write; service-layer 403 for teachers since medication info is not classroom-relevant — life-threatening allergies surface via the Step 5 health record stripped DTO; parent strip drops`prescribingPhysician`since parents already have the prescription on paper; reads write VIEW_MEDICATIONS audit).`MedicationScheduleService`(4 endpoints — list / create / patch / delete schedule slots; deleting a slot leaves historical administration rows intact via the deliberate`schedule_entry_id`soft ref from Step 2).`AdministrationService` (4 endpoints + the keystone medication dashboard query): **`POST /health/medications/:id/administer`** writes active dose (`was_missed=false, administered_at=now(), administered_by=actor.employeeId, missed_reason=NULL`); refuses callers without an `hr_employees`row (synthetic Platform Admin would fail by design — clinical record); validates`scheduleEntryId`belongs to the parent medication; refuses inactive medications; emits`hlth.medication.administered` after the INSERT for the future Cycle 3 NotificationConsumer to fan out parent notifications. **`POST /health/medications/:id/missed`** writes missed dose (`was_missed=true, administered_at=NULL, administered_by=NULL, missed_reason=`5-value enum) — both shapes pinned by the Step 2 multi-column`missed_chk` keystone. **`GET /health/medication-dashboard`** is admin/nurse-only (gated on hlt-002:read) — joins `hlth_medication_schedule + hlth_medications + hlth_student_health_records + sis_students + iam_person`for today's slots (where`m.is_active=true AND (s.day_of_week IS NULL OR s.day_of_week = EXTRACT(DOW FROM now()))`) then LEFT JOINs `hlth_medication_administrations`keyed on`(schedule_entry_id, today's date)`and resolves status —`PENDING`if no admin row,`MISSED`if`was_missed=true`, `ADMINISTERED`otherwise. Sorted by scheduled_time ASC then student last_name ASC for a top-to-bottom daily checklist. KafkaModule added to HealthRecordsModule.imports for the emit. **Live verification on`tenant_demo`2026-05-04 (15 scenarios + Kafka envelope all green):** M1 admin sees full medication with physician=Dr. Sarah Lee + 1 schedule slot; **M2 parent sees`prescribingPhysician=null`** — strip works; M3 teacher 403 service-layer; M4 student 403 at gate; M5 nurse administer with scheduleEntryId writes active dose; M6 verifies `was_missed=f, administered_at NOT NULL, missed_reason NULL`; M7 nurse missed STUDENT_REFUSED writes missed dose; verifies `was_missed=t, administered_at=NULL, missed_reason=STUDENT_REFUSED`; M8 BOGUS missed_reason 400 from class-validator with full enum list; M9 bogus scheduleEntryId 400 ("does not belong to this medication"); M10 teacher administer 403; **M11 dashboard returns 3 rows for today's 08:00 slot** showing the seeded missed-STUDENT_ABSENT + the new ADMINISTERED + the new MISSED-STUDENT_REFUSED with status resolution working correctly; M12 parent dashboard 403 (HLT-002:read not granted); M13 history shows 4 rows in correct chronological order; M14 access log shows 3 VIEW_MEDICATIONS audit rows from M1/M2/M13. **Wire envelope captured live** on `dev.hlth.medication.administered`with full ADR-057 shape:`event_type='hlth.medication.administered'`, `source_module='health'`, `tenant_id`populated, payload includes`administrationId, medicationId, medicationName='Albuterol Inhaler', studentId, studentName='Maya Chen', scheduleEntryId, administeredBy (employeeId), administeredByAccountId, doseGiven, parentNotified=true, administeredAt`. `MedicationService.loadStudentForMedication`exported from the module so the Step 7 modules can bridge medication ↔ student ids. Cycle 10 endpoint count after Step 6: **24** (14 from Step 5 + 10 from Step 6). **Step 7 lands`apps/api/src/health/`** with 4 services + 4 controllers + 1 Kafka consumer + 23 endpoints + 2 new Kafka emits — the largest backend step of Cycle 10. **Cycle 10 endpoint count after Step 7: 47.** Cycle 10 Kafka surface: 3 emit topics (`hlth.medication.administered`, `iep.accommodation.updated`, `hlth.nurse_visit.sent_home`) + 1 consumer (`IepAccommodationConsumer`). **`IepPlanService`** owns the full IEP/504 plan surface — plans + goals + goal_progress + services + accommodations (12 endpoints). Visibility: admin/nurse/counsellor see all; parent (GUARDIAN row scope via `sis_student_guardians`) sees full IEP detail with no PII strip (parents are full IEP team participants); teacher 403 service-layer with redirect-to-read-model message ("Teachers see accommodations via sis_student_active_accommodations"); student 403 at gate. Schema's partial UNIQUE on `(student_id) WHERE status<>'EXPIRED'`is caught and surfaced as a friendly 400. Plan UPDATE locks the row FOR UPDATE inside a tenant transaction. Every accommodation INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE + every plan UPDATE calls`emitAccommodationSnapshotByPlanId(planId)`which packages the FULL post-mutation accommodation snapshot for the student and emits`iep.accommodation.updated`. EXPIRED plans contribute an empty array — the consumer interprets that as "drop the read-model rows for this student". **`IepAccommodationConsumer`— THE ADR-030 KEYSTONE.** Subscribes to`dev.iep.accommodation.updated`under group`iep-accommodation-consumer`. Per inbound event, reconciles `sis_student_active_accommodations`in two phases inside one tenant transaction: (1) UPSERT every accommodation in the payload keyed on`source_iep_accommodation_id`(the schema's partial UNIQUE INDEX from the Step 4 migration is the canonical key —`ON CONFLICT (source_iep_accommodation_id) WHERE source_iep_accommodation_id IS NOT NULL DO UPDATE SET ...`); (2) DELETE any rows for this student whose `source_iep_accommodation_id IS NOT NULL`AND not in the payload's set (catches both source deletions AND plan EXPIRED transitions). Seed rows with NULL`source_iep_accommodation_id`are intentionally untouched so the Step 4 ADR-030 demo seed coexists with consumer-maintained rows. Standard claim-after-success idempotency via`processWithIdempotency` matches the Cycle 5 CoverageConsumer + Cycle 9 BehaviourNotificationConsumer pattern. **`NurseVisitService`** — live nurse-office surface (4 endpoints under hlt-003:read/write; admin / nurse only). Soft polymorphic `visited_person_id`resolved to STUDENT name (sis_students → platform_students → iam_person) or STAFF name (hr_employees → iam_person) via two LEFT JOINs and COALESCE in the SELECT. Update path runs inside`executeInTenantTransaction`with`SELECT … FOR UPDATE`and stamps both lockstep columns in the same UPDATE so the schema's`signed_chk`and`sent_home_chk`multi-column CHECKs from Step 3 never fire mid-flight:`sentHome=true`adds`sent_home_at = now()`, `sentHome=false`clears it;`signOut=true`sets both`status='COMPLETED'`and`signed_out_at = now()`atomically. Emits`hlth.nurse_visit.sent_home` only on the false → true transition (not on every update where sent_home stays true) for the future Cycle 3 NotificationConsumer to fan out parent notifications. Per-STUDENT visit reads write a VIEW_VISITS audit row; STAFF visits skip the audit since staff visits are not student PHI. **`ScreeningService`** — per-student screening CRUD (4 endpoints under hlt-004:read/write/admin). Admin-only `GET /health/screenings/follow-up`hits the partial INDEX on`(school_id, follow_up_completed) WHERE follow_up_required=true AND follow_up_completed=false`. Reads write one VIEW_SCREENING audit row per distinct student in the result set. **`DietaryProfileService`** — per-student dietary CRUD + the `/allergen-alerts`POS / cafeteria surface (4 endpoints). UNIQUE on student_id rejects duplicates with friendly 400. Reads gated on`hlt-001:read`so parents AND teachers (own-class students) can see their child's allergen list for cafeteria + classroom safety; writes gated on`hlt-005:write`. **Live verification on `tenant_demo`2026-05-04 (26 scenarios + 2 wire envelopes all green):** I1-I5 IEP visibility — nurse + parent see full plan; teacher 403 service-layer with the redirect message; student 403; admin POST 2nd plan rejected with friendly partial-UNIQUE 400; **I6 ADR-030 KEYSTONE — nurse adds new ASSISTIVE_TECH ALL_ASSIGNMENTS accommodation; within 2 seconds the consumer reconciles`sis_student_active_accommodations`to 3 rows including the new one with`has_src=true`**; I8 SPECIFIC without array → 400 with friendly applies_to_chk message; **I9-I10 ADR-030 DELETE keystone — nurse DELETEs the accommodation; within 2s the consumer drops the matching read-model row (back to 2 rows)**; I11 goal progress with `recordedByName=Sarah Mitchell`; I12 teacher reads from the read model directly via psql confirming the ADR-030 contract. N1-N7 nurse visits — empty roster initially; sign-in flips to 1 IN_PROGRESS; sign out + parent_notified + sentHome=true atomic update via locked tenant tx; psql verifies (status=COMPLETED, signed_out_at NOT NULL, sent_home=true, sent_home_at NOT NULL); double sign-out 400. S1-S5 screenings — admin follow-up queue shows Maya VISION REFER from seed; nurse records new HEARING PASS for Ethan; BOGUS result rejected by class-validator; admin marks complete; follow-up queue now empty. D1-D7 dietary — nurse + parent + teacher all see Maya's allergens (teachers DO need allergen lists for own-class students per real-world classroom snack/party safety — doc updated to reflect this); allergen alerts surface = 1 (Maya/Peanuts); admin POST new profile for Aiden; UNIQUE catch on duplicate; allergen alerts now = 2. **Wire envelopes captured live** on `dev.iep.accommodation.updated`(event_type, source_module='health', payload with`planType=504`, `planStatus=ACTIVE`, accommodations array of 2 post-DELETE) and `dev.hlth.nurse_visit.sent_home`(visitedPersonType=STUDENT, nurseAccountId set, sentHomeAt populated).
