`submit()`after the leave row + balance commit calls`workflowEngine.submit({requestType:'LEAVE_REQUEST', referenceId:leaveId, referenceTable:'hr_leave_requests'}, actor)`wrapped in try/catch — schools without a`LEAVE_REQUEST`template log "No active workflow template configured" + continue (the leave row is committed and the direct admin path still works);`approve()`/`reject()`keep their`actor.isSchoolAdmin`gate then delegate to new public`approveInternal(id, reviewNotes, reviewerAccountId)`+`rejectInternal(...)`helpers that contain the existing balance-shift + status-flip + Kafka-emit logic verbatim plus call a new`loadByIdNoAuth(id)`private helper since the consumer doesn't have an actor object. **New`apps/api/src/hr/leave-approval.consumer.ts`** subscribes to `dev.approval.request.resolved`under group`leave-approval-consumer`; uses the standard `unwrapEnvelope`+`processWithIdempotency`claim-after-success pattern; filters by`payload.requestType === 'LEAVE_REQUEST'`(other request types silently dropped — picked up by future ChildLinkApprovalConsumer / RoomChangeApprovalConsumer); routes APPROVED →`LeaveService.approveInternal(referenceId, null, requesterId)`and REJECTED →`rejectInternal(...)`; catches the "already APPROVED/REJECTED/CANCELLED" 400 from `lockAndValidate`and logs + drops (the documented race where an admin used the direct PATCH override and the row is no longer PENDING — the consumer-group claim still fires on success-path exit so a redelivery is a no-op); other errors rethrow →`processWithIdempotency`leaves the event unclaimed → next redelivery retries. **HrModule** imports`WorkflowsModule`and registers the new consumer. **Backward compatibility three layers:** (1) the seed continues to work — Cycle 4's seeded LEAVE_REQUEST template provides the chain, the inner`hr.leave.approved`emit still fires inside`approveInternal`so Cycle 4–5 chain (LeaveNotificationConsumer republishes hr.leave.coverage_needed for CoverageConsumer) works unchanged; (2) direct admin override path`PATCH /leave-requests/:id/approve|reject`stays in place — when an admin races the engine, the consumer logs and drops; (3) no template configured falls back to direct pattern — engine throws "No active workflow template configured", LeaveService swallows + logs + continues. **LeaveNotificationConsumer (Cycle 4) remains in place** — the plan's note that "the engine emits a notification to the requester automatically" is **deferred**: our engine emits`approval.step.awaiting`and`approval.request.resolved`but doesn't yet bridge into`NotificationQueueService.enqueue()`; a future-cycle consumer (or Step 4 auto-task rule on these topics) would generalise it. For now the existing LeaveNotificationConsumer covers the leave-notification UX. **Audit trail caveat:** `hr_leave_requests.reviewed_by`is populated from the resolved payload's`requesterId`since the engine doesn't yet expose the final approver's account id on`approval.request.resolved`. Documented Phase 2 carry-over — future cycles extend the resolved payload. Admins using the direct PATCH path still get their own id recorded. **Live verification on `tenant_demo`** (end-to-end smoke, 9 scenarios all green): pre-state Rivera Sick balance pending=0 used=2 (post-seed); Rivera submits 1-day Sick leave for 2026-09-30 → leave PENDING + workflow engine creates approval request + Step 1 with approver=admin@ (DEPARTMENT_HEAD fallback to first school admin alphabetically); Sarah approves Step 1 via admin override → Step 2 activates with approver=Sarah Mitchell (ROLE='SCHOOL_ADMIN' resolves her); Sarah approves Step 2 → request resolves APPROVED + resolved_at populated; LeaveApprovalConsumer fires within ~1 second → leave row flips PENDING → APPROVED, balance moves pending=0/used=2 → pending=0/used=3, reviewed_at populated + reviewed_by set; existing Cycle 4-5 chain still works — `hr.leave.approved`consumed by LeaveNotificationConsumer republishes hr.leave.coverage_needed → CoverageConsumer creates 6 OPEN coverage rows (one per Rivera class). Smoke residue cleaned (1 leave + 6 coverage + 1 approval request CASCADE drops 2 steps + 0 comments + balance restored). Two iteration issues caught (test-data only):`psql -t -c "SET …; SELECT …"`returns the SET command tag concatenated to the UUID — switched to schema-qualified queries and dropped SET; leave type name lookup`WHERE name='Sick'`missed because the seed names it`'Sick Leave'`— fixed and re-ran. Out of scope this step (deferred): engine-emitted requester notifications (bridge from approval.step.awaiting → NotificationQueueService); final-approver id in resolved payload; migrating RoomChangeRequestService (Cycle 5) and ChildLinkRequestService (Cycle 6.1) onto the engine — both work fine on their hardcoded patterns and the engine is ready to absorb them when chosen. **Step 6 lands`apps/api/src/workflows/workflow-engine.service.ts`** **Step 6 lands `apps/api/src/workflows/workflow-engine.service.ts`+`apps/api/src/workflows/workflow.controller.ts`+`apps/api/src/workflows/workflows.module.ts`+`apps/api/src/workflows/dto/workflow.dto.ts`** — the keystone of M2 Approval Workflows. WorkflowEngineService is the **sole writer to `wsk_approval_requests`and`wsk_approval_steps`per ADR-012** — source modules submit via REST (or call`submit()`programmatically when Step 7's LeaveService migration lands) and listen on`approval.request.resolved`to apply the approved action. WorkflowsModule wired into AppModule.imports between TasksModule and the global guards. **7 endpoints under`/approvals`:** `POST /approvals`(submit; engine selects active workflow template by request_type, creates request + Step 1 with resolved approver in one tx, emits approval.step.awaiting),`GET /approvals`(admin sees all or`?mine=true`for own; non-admin sees`requester_id = me OR EXISTS approver_id = me on any step`; filters status + requestType), `GET /approvals/:id`(full detail with step history + comments),`POST /approvals/:id/steps/:stepId/approve`(locks step + request rows FOR UPDATE inside one tx, validates step is AWAITING + caller is approver or admin, marks approved + activates next step with new resolved approver OR resolves request as APPROVED if no more steps),`POST /approvals/:id/steps/:stepId/reject`(same lock pattern, marks step REJECTED + every still-AWAITING step SKIPPED + request REJECTED + emits approval.request.resolved),`POST /approvals/:id/comments`(append; isRequesterVisible flag distinguishes admin-internal-only from requester-visible),`POST /approvals/:id/withdraw` (requester pulls back PENDING request — does NOT emit approval.request.resolved since the requester pulled back). **Approver resolution (`resolveApprover`):** SPECIFIC_USER → direct lookup of `approver_ref`UUID; ROLE → maps token`'SCHOOL_ADMIN'`→ role name`'School Admin'`via the new exported`roleTokenToName()`helper (inverse of Cycle 3's`roleNameToToken`convention) and queries`platform.iam_role_assignment + roles + iam_scope + iam_scope_type` for the first ACTIVE assignment with role.name match on the school + platform scope chain (excludes requester so self-approval doesn't resolve); MANAGER + DEPARTMENT_HEAD both fall back to the first school admin (`'sch-001:admin' = ANY(eac.permission_codes)`) since proper hr_employees.supervisor_id / sis_departments.head_id traversal is **deferred per the plan** — fallback logged at LOG level. Sequential mode picks one approver per step (smallest UUID by `ORDER BY 1`). **Two new emits:** `approval.step.awaiting`fires on every fresh AWAITING step (future cycles wire a notification consumer or a TaskWorker auto-task rule onto this so approvers see the step in their Tasks app);`approval.request.resolved`fires once per terminal transition (APPROVED or REJECTED — WITHDRAWN deliberately does NOT emit). Kafka emits happen OUTSIDE the tx so a broker hiccup doesn't roll back the user's action. **Comment visibility model:** admins see all; approvers (current or past on any step) see all to collaborate; requester sees only`is_requester_visible = true`rows. **Live verification on`tenant_demo` (12 scenarios all pass):** Rivera submits LEAVE_REQUEST against a real Cycle 4 leave id → engine creates request + Step 1 with approver=admin@ (DEPARTMENT_HEAD falls back to first school admin alphabetically); Rivera GET own visible; David GET 404 (no scope); David POST approve 403 ("You are not the assigned approver for this step"); Sarah (admin) approves Step 1 via admin override → Step 2 activates with approver=Sarah Mitchell (ROLE='SCHOOL_ADMIN' resolves her); re-approve Step 1 400 ("Only AWAITING steps can be approved; this one is APPROVED"); Sarah adds admin-internal comment (`isRequesterVisible:false`); Rivera GET sees 0 comments (filtered); Sarah GET sees 1 comment (admin override); Sarah approves Step 2 → request resolves to APPROVED + resolved_at populated + both steps APPROVED; `dev.approval.request.resolved` envelope captured on the wire with full ADR-057 shape (`event_type='approval.request.resolved'`, `source_module='workflows'`, payload `{requestId, requestType:'LEAVE_REQUEST', referenceId, referenceTable:'hr_leave_requests', requesterId, status:'APPROVED'}`). **Two issues caught and fixed during smoke:** (1) stale node process holding port 4000 from prior smoke — `kill -9 $(lsof -ti :4000)`resolved; (2)`SELECT DISTINCT … ORDER BY eac.account_id`SQL bug — Postgres requires ORDER BY columns to appear in the SELECT list under DISTINCT, switched to positional`ORDER BY 1`. Smoke residue cleaned (CASCADE drops the smoke request's steps + comments). Out of scope this step (deferred per the plan): parallel approval steps (`is_parallel=true`ships forward-compatible from Step 2 but engine is sequential-only); proper MANAGER / DEPARTMENT_HEAD traversal (needs hr_employees.supervisor_id + sis_departments.head_id which aren't seeded — falls back to school admin with LOG line); escalation timeout worker (schema ready, cron deferred). **Step 5 lands`apps/api/src/tasks/task.service.ts`+`apps/api/src/tasks/acknowledgement.service.ts`+`apps/api/src/tasks/task.controller.ts`+`apps/api/src/tasks/acknowledgement.controller.ts`+`apps/api/src/tasks/dto/task.dto.ts`** **Step 5 lands `apps/api/src/tasks/task.service.ts`+`apps/api/src/tasks/acknowledgement.service.ts`+`apps/api/src/tasks/task.controller.ts`+`apps/api/src/tasks/acknowledgement.controller.ts`+`apps/api/src/tasks/dto/task.dto.ts`** — the request-path API on top of the Step 1 schema. **9 new endpoints (5 task + 4 ack):** `GET /tasks`(own list =`owner_id OR created_for_id = actor`, admin sees all; filters status/category/priority/dueAfter/dueBefore/includeCompleted; default sort due-NULLS-LAST then priority urgency; default limit 100 max 200), `GET /tasks/assigned`(delegation inbox:`created_for_id = actor AND owner_id != actor`), `GET /tasks/:id`(row-scope 404 on no access),`POST /tasks`(MANUAL source; assigneeAccountId field lands the row on someone else's list with`created_for_id = caller`— admin-only this cycle, non-admin delegation rejected with 403; ACKNOWLEDGEMENT-category rejected for non-admins since the worker owns that flow; emits`task.created`), `PATCH /tasks/:id`(status transitions + retitle + reschedule with multi-column`completed_chk`lockstep —`COALESCE(completed_at, now())`on DONE/CANCELLED so re-DONE doesn't bump timestamp, NULL on TODO/IN_PROGRESS for re-open path; emits`task.completed`only on first DONE transition),`GET /acknowledgements`(default own pending where`subject_id = actor.personId AND status='PENDING'`; admin `?all=true`for tenant-wide history),`GET /acknowledgements/:id`(row-scope 404),`POST /acknowledgements/:id/acknowledge`(flips PENDING → ACKNOWLEDGED + DONE-cascades every linked`tsk_tasks`row in same tx + emits`student.acknowledgement.completed`), `POST /acknowledgements/:id/dispute`(flips to ACKNOWLEDGED_WITH_DISPUTE with required`reason`1-2000 chars + same DONE-cascade). **Convention adopted clarifying the schema:**`owner_id`= the person whose to-do list the task lands on (the assignee in everyday terms);`created_for_id`= nullable, set when one user creates a task on behalf of another. AUTO worker writes`owner_id = assignee, created_for_id = NULL`; MANUAL self-task `owner_id = me, created_for_id = NULL`; MANUAL delegation `owner_id = assignee, created_for_id = me`(admin-only). The plan's auth note suggests the inverse but the schema and to-do-list mental model are clearer with this convention. **Acknowledge/dispute tx model:**`executeInTenantTransaction`with`SELECT … FOR UPDATE`on the ack row so two parallel calls serialise; validates subject_id match + PENDING status; UPDATEs ack + cascade-UPDATEs all linked`tsk_tasks`rows where status NOT IN (DONE, CANCELLED) to status='DONE' + completed_at=now() in the same UPDATE so the multi-column`completed_chk`is satisfied; Kafka emit outside the tx so a broker hiccup doesn't roll back the user's action. **Live verification on`tenant_demo`(23 scenarios all pass):** S1–S14 cover the 5 task endpoints + permission denials + delegation pathway with admin override; A1–A9 cover the 4 ack endpoints + the DONE-cascade verification + the row-scope 404 + the empty-reason 400 from class-validator + the ?all=true admin-only override. **Wire envelopes captured live with full ADR-057 shape:**`dev.task.completed`with`source_module:'tasks'`+ payload {taskId, ownerId, title, taskCategory, source:'MANUAL', sourceRefId:null, completedAt};`dev.student.acknowledgement.completed`with payload {acknowledgementId, subjectId, status:'ACKNOWLEDGED_WITH_DISPUTE', sourceType:'DISCIPLINE_RECORD', sourceRefId, disputeReason}. **Bug caught during smoke:** the first ack-smoke run keyed`subject_id`to the principal's`iam_person.id`instead of Maya's because the SQL JOIN reused the same`pu`row for both`subject_id`and`created_by`; Maya's GET returned 0 acks even though the rows existed. Re-seeded the test acks with the correct `subject_id`lookup; the service's row-scope check correctly returned 0/404 — the bug was test-data only, not a service-layer issue. Smoke residue cleaned (3 manual tasks + 4 ack-tasks + 2 acks deleted) — tenant returns to post-Step-3 seed state (5 sample tasks, 0 acks). Out of scope this step (deferred): teachers delegating to students they teach (admin-only this cycle, future iteration can use the`sis_class_teachers`row-scope from Cycle 2); bulk admin operations on`ops-001:admin`; task tags (deferred at schema level); a `task.completed`consumer to wire the event into the Cycle 3 notification pipeline (similar pattern to Cycle 5's`sch.coverage.assigned`documented carry-over). **Step 4 lands`apps/api/src/tasks/task.worker.ts`+`apps/api/src/tasks/template-render.ts`+`apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.module.ts`** **Step 4 lands `apps/api/src/tasks/task.worker.ts`+`apps/api/src/tasks/template-render.ts`+`apps/api/src/tasks/tasks.module.ts`** wired into AppModule.imports after HouseholdsModule. The TaskWorker is the **sole writer to `tsk_tasks`per ADR-011** — domain modules emit Kafka events, the worker translates them into rows. **Bootstrap:** reads`tsk_auto_task_rules`across every active school via`platform.schools`+`platform_tenant_routing`+`executeInExplicitSchema`, takes the union of distinct `trigger_event_type`values, env-prefixes via`prefixedTopic()`, and subscribes under group `task-worker`. Adding a new rule with a never-before-seen trigger type at runtime requires a worker restart — documented limitation. **Per-event flow:** `unwrapEnvelope`→`processWithIdempotency('task-worker', event)`claim-after-success → query active rules for this tenant + topic → AND-evaluate`tsk_auto_task_conditions`against payload (7-value operator enum EQUALS/NOT_EQUALS/IN/NOT_IN/GT/LT/EXISTS implemented in`evaluateCondition()`, JSON dot-paths for field_path) → resolve owners (`cls.assignment.posted`joins`sis_enrollments + sis_students + platform_students + platform_users`for the class;`target_role=SCHOOL_ADMIN`reads`platform.iam_effective_access_cache`with`'sch-001:admin' = ANY(permission_codes)`matching the`AbsenceRequestNotificationConsumer.loadSchoolAdminAccounts`precedent;`target_role=STUDENT`resolves a single account from`payload.studentId`; fallback reads `payload.recipientAccountId`) → execute actions in sort_order (`CREATE_ACKNOWLEDGEMENT`inserts`tsk_acknowledgements`per owner returning a Map<owner, ack_id> for the next CREATE_TASK to link via`acknowledgement_id`; `CREATE_TASK`runs per-(owner, source_ref_id) Redis SET NX on`tsk:auto:{subdomain}:{owner}:{source_ref_id}`30-day TTL then INSERTs with`source='AUTO'`+ the rule's priority/category + rendered title + description, emits`task.created`with`correlation_id = inbound event_id`; `SEND_NOTIFICATION`reserved for future cycles). **Dedup is dual-layer:** per-event_id consumer-group claim catches Kafka redelivery of the same event row; per-(owner, source_ref_id) Redis SET NX catches "different event_id but same logical action" (a teacher PATCHing`isPublished=true`on an already-published assignment re-emits with a new event_id but the source_ref_id matches). The schema-level partial INDEX on`(owner_id, source, source_ref_id) WHERE source != 'MANUAL'`is non-unique (partitioned tables can't constrain across partitions without including the partition key) — investigation support only. **Three new helper additions**:`unprefixTopic(wireTopic)`in`apps/api/src/kafka/event-envelope.ts`(inverse of`prefixedTopic`so the worker matches wire topics back to logical`trigger_event_type`); `cls.assignment.posted`Kafka emit in`apps/api/src/classroom/assignment.service.ts`create + update paths whenever`is_published=true`lands (with`assignment_title`+`class_name`+`due_date`placeholders for the worker to render);`isPublished: true`field added to the`cls.grade.published`payload in`apps/api/src/classroom/grade.service.ts` so the seeded condition (`payload.isPublished = true`) actually evaluates against a present field — the topic name implies it but the field needs to be on the wire for the condition evaluator. **Live verification on `tenant_demo`2026-05-03:** pre-created the 8 trigger topics with`kafka-topics.sh --create --if-not-exists`(one-time dev workaround for the documented Cycle 3+5 subscribe-before-publish race); booted API; worker logged`TaskWorker subscribed to 8 topic(s)`cleanly; teacher Rivera POSTed an assignment "Step 4 Live Smoke — Photosynthesis Lab" with`isPublished:true`against P1 Algebra; within ~3s the worker created 1 row in`tsk_tasks`for Maya (the only ACTIVE enrollee) with`title='Complete: Step 4 Live Smoke — Photosynthesis Lab'`(rendered from`title_template='Complete: {assignment_title}'`), `owner_id`=student@, `source='AUTO'`, `source_ref_id`=assignment id, `priority='NORMAL'`, `task_category='ACADEMIC'`, `status='TODO'`; PATCHing the same assignment again with `isPublished:true`triggered the worker's dedup-hit log line`[task-worker] dedup hit on tsk:auto:demo:<owner>:<source_ref_id> — skipping task creation`and the task count stayed at 1; cleanup deleted the smoke task + smoke assignment + matching Redis dedup key. Out of scope this step (deferred): the remaining 7 auto-task rules are scaffolded but not exercised end-to-end yet — the keystone assignment.posted path verifies the engine and the smaller paths reuse the same resolveOwners / template-render / dedup machinery; runtime rule-add without worker restart; warmup-emit sweep at startup to dodge the topic-subscribe race. **Step 3 lands`packages/database/src/seed-tasks.ts`** **Step 3 lands `packages/database/src/seed-tasks.ts`** (idempotent, gated on `tsk_auto_task_rules`row count for the demo school) wired into`package.json`as`seed:tasks`. Five sections seeded on `tenant_demo`(test tenant stays empty by convention): (A) **8 auto-task rules** all`is_system=true`covering`cls.assignment.posted`(NORMAL, 0h offset, ACADEMIC, CREATE_TASK only),`cls.grade.published`(LOW, 168h, ACADEMIC),`cls.grade.returned`(LOW, 168h, ACADEMIC),`hr.leave.approved`(HIGH, 24h, ADMINISTRATIVE for SCHOOL_ADMIN),`att.absence.requested`(NORMAL, 24h, ADMINISTRATIVE for SCHOOL_ADMIN),`msg.announcement.requires_acknowledgement`(NORMAL, 72h, ACKNOWLEDGEMENT with both CREATE_ACKNOWLEDGEMENT + CREATE_TASK actions),`sis.consent.requested`(HIGH, 168h, ACKNOWLEDGEMENT for GUARDIAN with both actions),`sys.profile.update_requested`(NORMAL, 168h, ADMINISTRATIVE) — total 10 actions across the 8 rules; (B) **2 auto-task conditions** both gating on`payload.isPublished = true`for the assignment-posted and grade-published rules; (C) **3 workflow templates** all`is_active=true`— Leave Request Approval (2 steps: DEPARTMENT_HEAD then ROLE SCHOOL_ADMIN, 48h timeout each), Absence Request Review (1 step: ROLE SCHOOL_ADMIN, 24h), Child Link Approval (1 step: ROLE SCHOOL_ADMIN, 72h); (D) **5 sample tasks** back-dated to 2026-04-15 so they land in the`tsk_tasks_2026_04`partition leaf — 3 ACADEMIC tasks for Maya keyed on her first 3 published`cls_assignments` rows (`source=AUTO`, due 2026-04-20), 1 PERSONAL task for Maya ("Study for Biology test", `source=MANUAL`, due 2026-04-18), 1 ADMINISTRATIVE task for David ("Update emergency contact information", `source=SYSTEM`, due 2026-04-25); (E) **1 historical approval-request audit row** wrapping Rivera's APPROVED sick leave (2026-03-09 → 2026-03-10 from Cycle 4 seed) — `wsk_approval_requests`row with`status=APPROVED`, `reference_id`= Rivera's`hr_leave_requests.id`, plus 2 `wsk_approval_steps` rows showing the historical audit trail (Step 1 VP Linda Park approved 2026-02-16 with comment "Coverage arranged with Park."; Step 2 Principal Sarah Mitchell approved 2026-02-17 with comment "Approved."). **`seed-iam.ts`updated:** Teacher / Student / Parent / Staff each gain`OPS-001:read+write`(the catalogue's existing entry for Internal Task Management). The plan refers to this as "SYS-001" but the function library at`permissions.json`has the entry under`OPS-001`("Internal Task Management" in the Internal Operations group); adding a new SYS-001 entry would conflict with the existing SYS-001 "Access Management" so the catalogue stays at 447 functions and Cycle 7 services use`@RequirePermission('ops-001:*')`. School Admin and Platform Admin already hold `OPS-001:admin`via the`everyFunction: ['read','write','admin']`grant. Cache rebuild reports 7 account-scope pairs — admin/principal 447 (was 447 unchanged), teacher 38 (was 36), student 19 (was 17), parent 19 (was 17), vp/counsellor 18 (was 16). **Live counts on`tenant_demo`after seed:** rules=8, conditions=2, actions=10, tasks=5, tasks-by-category={ACADEMIC=3, PERSONAL=1, ADMINISTRATIVE=1}, workflow-templates=3, workflow-steps=4, approval-requests=1, approval-steps=2. All 5 sample tasks confirmed routed to the`tsk_tasks_2026_04` leaf (`SELECT COUNT(\*) FROM ONLY tsk_tasks_2026_04`= 5). Idempotent re-run logs`tsk_auto_task_rules already populated for demo school — skipping`with no INSERTs. **Step 2 lands`packages/database/prisma/tenant/migrations/027_wsk_approval_workflows.sql`** **Step 2 lands `packages/database/prisma/tenant/migrations/027_wsk_approval_workflows.sql`** with 6 logical base tables for the M2 Approval Workflows module: `wsk_workflow_templates`(per-(school, request_type) chain definition with UNIQUE(school_id, request_type) so each school has one active template per request type, free-form request_type TEXT for school-authored types);`wsk_workflow_steps`(ordered approval steps with step_order > 0 CHECK, 4-value approver_type CHECK SPECIFIC_USER/ROLE/MANAGER/DEPARTMENT_HEAD, **multi-column approver_shape_chk** enforcing SPECIFIC_USER + ROLE require approver_ref while MANAGER + DEPARTMENT_HEAD require it null since those resolve dynamically at runtime, is_parallel column ships forward-compatible but the engine is sequential-only this cycle, timeout_hours > 0 CHECK when set, UNIQUE(template, step_order) so two steps in the same template can't share a position, ON DELETE CASCADE on template);`wsk_approval_requests`(status lifecycle PENDING → APPROVED/REJECTED/CANCELLED/WITHDRAWN with **multi-column resolved_chk** keeping resolved_at in lockstep with status — PENDING ⇒ NULL, every terminal status ⇒ NOT NULL — and **multi-column reference_shape_chk** enforcing reference_id + reference_table both-set-or-both-null for soft polymorphic refs to the originating domain row; partial INDEX(school, status, request_type) WHERE status NOT IN terminal-set for admin "active queue" hot path, INDEX(requester, created_at DESC) for "my requests", partial INDEX(reference_table, reference_id) WHERE reference_id IS NOT NULL for reverse-lookup; FK template_id is NO ACTION refusing to delete a template that has historical requests against it);`wsk_approval_steps`(step_order > 0 CHECK, status lifecycle AWAITING → APPROVED/REJECTED/SKIPPED with **multi-column actioned_chk** enforcing AWAITING ⇒ actioned_at NULL and APPROVED/REJECTED ⇒ actioned_at NOT NULL while SKIPPED has no constraint on actioned_at since a skipped step was never actioned; UNIQUE(request, step_order); partial INDEX(approver_id, status) WHERE status='AWAITING' for the approver pending-queue hot path; CASCADE on request);`wsk_approval_comments`(append-only thread with is_requester_visible flag distinguishing public from approver-internal-only notes; CASCADE on request);`wsk_workflow_escalations`(append-mostly audit with hours_overdue >= 0 CHECK and **multi-column resolved_chk** keeping resolved_at + resolved_by all-set-or-all-null together, partial INDEX(escalated_to_id, resolved_at) WHERE resolved_at IS NULL for escalation-recipient queue, NO ACTION FKs since the audit row outlives the request being audited — schema-only this cycle, escalation timeout worker deferred). 6 new intra-tenant DB-enforced FKs (1 CASCADE on wsk_workflow_steps.template_id; 1 NO ACTION on wsk_approval_requests.template_id; 2 CASCADE on wsk_approval_steps.request_id and wsk_approval_comments.request_id; 2 NO ACTION on wsk_workflow_escalations.request_id and step_id), 0 cross-schema FKs. **Tenant logical base table count after Step 2: 120** (was 114 after Step 1; +6 logical wsk\** tables, none partitioned). **Live verification on`tenant_demo`(single BEGIN…ROLLBACK with savepoints, 23 assertions all green):** happy-path 7-table multi-insert (1 template + 3 steps + 1 request + 1 step + 1 comment); step*order=0 + timeout_hours=0 rejected; approver_type_chk rejects BOGUS; approver_shape_chk rejects SPECIFIC_USER without approver_ref AND MANAGER with approver_ref both directions; both UNIQUE constraints reject duplicates; request status_chk + step status_chk reject BOGUS; resolved_chk rejects PENDING with resolved_at AND APPROVED without resolved_at both directions; reference_shape_chk rejects partial pair; actioned_chk rejects AWAITING with actioned_at AND APPROVED without actioned_at, accepts SKIPPED without actioned_at; CASCADE behavior verified on template + request deletes; NO ACTION FK fires when deleting template with historical requests; escalation hours_overdue<0 rejected; escalation resolved_chk rejects partial pair; happy-path escalation insert succeeds. Splitter`;`-in-string trap not tripped — first-attempt provision succeeded after a state-machine audit that properly handles block-comments + line-comments + single-quoted strings with `''`escape sequences (the naive single-line regex from earlier cycles produced false positives on multi-line COMMENT strings). Idempotent re-provision verified. **Step 1 lands`packages/database/prisma/tenant/migrations/026_tsk_tasks_and_auto_rules.sql`** Plan at `docs/campusos-cycle7-implementation-plan.html`; handoff at `HANDOFF-CYCLE7.md`. Step 1 lands `packages/database/prisma/tenant/migrations/026_tsk_tasks_and_auto_rules.sql`with 6 logical base tables for the M1 Task Management module:`tsk_acknowledgements`(per-(school, subject) PENDING / ACKNOWLEDGED / ACKNOWLEDGED_WITH_DISPUTE / EXPIRED with multi-column dispute_chk + ack_chk; soft polymorphic source_type + source_ref_id + source_table);`tsk_auto_task_rules`(per-(school, trigger_event_type) catalogue with priority + task_category enums + is_system flag distinguishing seed rules from school-authored; partial UNIQUE INDEX (school_id, trigger_event_type) WHERE is_system=true so schools can layer a custom override on the same event);`tsk_auto_task_conditions`(AND-ed conditions with 7-value operator enum EQUALS/NOT_EQUALS/IN/NOT_IN/GT/LT/EXISTS, JSONB value, ON DELETE CASCADE on rule);`tsk_auto_task_actions` (3-value action_type CREATE_TASK/CREATE_ACKNOWLEDGEMENT/SEND_NOTIFICATION + JSONB action_config + sort_order, CASCADE on rule); **`tsk_tasks` RANGE-partitioned by created_at monthly with 24 partitions** covering 2025-08 → 2027-08 (matching the msg_messages window from Cycle 3), composite PK (id, created_at) because partition column must appear in unique constraint, multi-column completed_chk enforcing TODO/IN_PROGRESS ⇒ completed_at NULL and DONE/CANCELLED ⇒ completed_at NOT NULL, FK acknowledgement_id → tsk_acknowledgements ON DELETE SET NULL replicates onto each of the 24 partitions plus the parent (25 pg_constraint rows for one logical FK matching the pay_ledger_entries.family_account_id precedent), partial INDEX(owner_id, source, source_ref_id) WHERE source != 'MANUAL' for auto-task dedup investigation (authoritative idempotency stays Redis SET NX in the future Step 4 Task Worker since partitioned-table UNIQUE constraints must include the partition column which would defeat dedup); **`tsk_tasks_archive`RANGE-partitioned by created_at annually** with 3 partitions covering 2025/2026/2027, same shape plus archived_at column, no FK back so a deleted ack leaves archived rows stable (the archiver job moving DONE/CANCELLED rows older than 30 days from tsk_tasks → tsk_tasks_archive is deferred to ops — schema-only this cycle). 4 new intra-tenant DB-enforced FKs (2 CASCADE on rules, 1 SET NULL on tsk_tasks → tsk_acknowledgements replicated 25× across the partitioned parent + 24 leaves), 0 cross-schema FKs. **Tenant logical base table count after Step 1: 114** (was 108 after Phase 2 polish; +6 logical tsk*\_ tables — the 24 monthly tsk_tasks partitions + 3 yearly tsk_tasks_archive partitions are not counted as logical base tables, matching the sis_attendance_records / msg_messages / msg_notification_log / msg_moderation_log / pay_ledger_entries precedent). **Live verification on`tenant_demo`(single BEGIN…ROLLBACK with savepoints, 12 assertions all green):** happy-path 5-table multi-insert; status_chk + completed_chk (both directions) + dispute_chk + ack_chk + operator_chk all fire; partition out-of-window rejection for 2024-12-31; partition routing inspection via`SELECT … FROM ONLY tsk_tasks_2026_04`confirms the row landed in the right leaf; CASCADE on tsk_auto_task_rules drops attached condition + action rows; SET NULL on tsk_acknowledgements clears tsk_tasks.acknowledgement_id while preserving the task row; partial UNIQUE rejects duplicate is_system=true rule and accepts is_system=false override on the same (school, event). Sanity counts: 24 monthly leaves + 3 yearly leaves + 25 FK constraint rows (24 partitions + 1 parent). **Splitter`;`-in-string trap not tripped this time** — Python audit script run before first provision attempt confirmed zero `;` inside any block comment or single-quoted string. First cycle since Cycle 4 to clear the splitter trap on first attempt. Idempotent re-provision verified (zero new applies on second run). (Steps 2–10 of Cycle 7 — Workflow schema, seed data, Task Worker Kafka consumer, Task NestJS module, Workflow Engine, Leave migration, Tasks UI, Approvals UI, vertical-slice integration test — remain to ship.)

**Phase 2 Parent Polish landed 2026-05-03 (5 commits, no review yet).** Five separate commits between `44dff03` and `c9d2de7` ship cross-cutting improvements identified during parent testing — none of them tied to a numbered Cycle. (1) `44dff03` — iPhone-style dashboard launchpad: app tiles shrink from aspect-square cards to a 56×56 rounded icon box with a label below; hover tints the icon-box from campus-50 to campus-100; badge moves to `-right-1 -top-1` with `ring-2 ring-white` so it sits on top of the icon-box edge. Grid widens 4 cols mobile / 6 cols desktop. (2) `9f8658c` — calendar event RSVPs + my-children filter + multi-school selector. Tenant migration `023_sch_calendar_event_rsvps.sql` adds the RSVP table (3-value GOING/TENTATIVE/NOT_GOING CHECK, UNIQUE(event, person), soft FK to platform.iam_person). New `CalendarRsvpService` with 3 endpoints under `sch-003:read` — `POST /calendar/:id/rsvp` upserts, `GET /calendar/:id/rsvps` (admin all, others own), `GET /calendar/:id/rsvp-summary` aggregate counts + my response. `CalendarService.list` accepts an optional `myKidsPersonIds` array; controller resolves the parent's own personId plus children's personIds (via `StudentService.listForGuardianPerson`) when guardian + `myKidsOnly=true`. UI: guardian-only `My children only` toggle + multi-school selector dropdown (only renders for 2+ schools — placeholder for cross-tenant aggregation). Event detail Modal grows an RSVP panel with chip buttons + aggregate counts. Phase 3 carry-over: full audience targeting on calendar events (filter is RSVP-only today). (3) `adc78d4` — Add Child workflow with admin approval. Tenant migration `024_sis_child_link_requests.sql` with mutually exclusive LINK_EXISTING / ADD_NEW shapes (multi-column `shape_chk`) + PENDING/APPROVED/REJECTED lifecycle (`reviewed_chk` keeps reviewer fields all-set or all-null). New `ChildLinkRequestService` with 6 endpoints under `/children` — search, link-request, add-request, list, approve, reject. `approve()` runs in `executeInTenantTransaction` with FOR UPDATE on the request row; ADD_NEW path atomically creates iam_person + platform_students + sis_students + sis_student_guardians + (when guardian has a household) platform_family_members CHILD entries. Emits `iam.child.linked`. UI: `/children` gains an Add child button with a 3-step Modal (SEARCH → PICK_RESULT → ADD_NEW); pending banner above the cards; new admin queue at `/children/link-requests` with status filter chips + per-row Approve/Reject Modal capturing reviewer notes. (4) `ae32299` — public enrollment search by location. Platform migration `20260503130749` adds `latitude` / `longitude` / `fullAddress` / `sharedBillingGroupId` (the last wired in commit 5) to `platform.schools`. Tenant migration `025_enr_period_allows_public_search.sql` adds `allows_public_search BOOLEAN DEFAULT true` to `enr_enrollment_periods` so existing OPEN periods auto-appear; invitation-only cycles flip false. New `EnrollmentSearchService` + `@Public()` `EnrollmentSearchController` at `GET /enrollment/search?lat=&lng=&radiusMiles=&gradeLevel=` (radius capped at 100 miles). Cross-tenant — phase 1 reads platform.schools + platform_tenant_routing then app-side Haversine filter; phase 2 enters each surviving tenant via the new `TenantPrismaService.executeInExplicitSchema(schemaName, fn)` helper. Two infra fixes the public endpoint surfaced: (a) `TenantGuard` is global so it ran on @Public() endpoints and threw on `getCurrentTenant()` — now reads `IS_PUBLIC_KEY` via `Reflector` and short-circuits, matching `AuthGuard`. (b) The middleware's exempt-path matcher used `req.path`, which Nest strips of the global `/api/v1` prefix before middleware runs — switched to `req.originalUrl` so the existing `/api/v1/health` entry actually matches (latent bug). Web: new `/find-schools` route at the app root (outside the auth-gated `(app)` layout) with lat/lng + "Use my location" button + radius slider + grade filter; login page gains a "Find a school accepting applications →" link below the account picker for unauth visitors. Verified live on `tenant_demo`: Springfield query finds Lincoln Elementary at 0.3 mi with the seeded Fall 2026 Admissions period; NYC query empty; grade=9 hits, grade=12 misses. (5) `c9d2de7` — multi-school billing structure. `family-account.service.ts` SELECT base joins `platform.schools` to surface `school_name` + `shared_billing_group_id` on every row; `FamilyAccountResponseDto` and the matching web `FamilyAccountDto` pick up the two new fields. Parent billing dashboard restructured to render one section per school, with sections grouped by `sharedBillingGroupId` when set (so two schools sharing a district billing module collapse into one combined section). Demo case (single tenant) shows one section with "Lincoln Elementary" header. Per-account UI extracted into `FamilyAccountSection.tsx` so the page is now a thin layout that maps over grouped accounts. Phase 3 carry-over: true cross-tenant aggregation (since tenancy is still per-request, this page only ever sees the current tenant's accounts). Schema impact: 2 new tenant tables (`sch_calendar_event_rsvps`, `sis_child_link_requests`) and 1 new tenant column on `enr_enrollment_periods` bring tenant base table count from 106 → **108**. 4 new platform columns on `platform.schools`. **HANDOFF-PHASE2-POLISH.md** carries the full per-feature detail; this list summarises what changed and where.

**Cycle 6 (Enrollment & Payments) is COMPLETE + APPROVED at `64993a8` (REVIEW-CYCLE6-CHATGPT — final verdict). Round 1 against `f7db6a5` returned REJECT pending 8 actionable fixes; Round 2 against `64993a8` returned APPROVED** after all 8 fixes landed. REVIEW-CYCLE6 fixes (2026-04-29): the reviewer surfaced 9 critical/blocking + 7 majors. Triage triaged 3 claims wrong (1 ResolvedActor.employeeId already on the interface; 2 Kafka consumer error-swallowing already fixed by REVIEW-CYCLE3 BLOCKING 1; 5 StudentService visibility already on actor.employeeId since Cycle 4 Step 0), 5 accepted DEVIATIONs (13 consumer envelope tenant validation already on Phase 2 from REVIEW-CYCLE5; 14 v11 platform endpoints forward-looking; 15 ledger immutability triggers ADR-010 explicit choice; 16 outbox pattern already on Phase 2 from REVIEW-CYCLE5; 11 the name-based sis*students link a documented Phase 2 design), and 8 valid bugs fixed in the closeout commit: (Fix 3 BLOCKING) `GET /classes/:id/attendance/:date` now resolves the actor + applies row scope (admin/class teacher → roster, student → own row, parent → linked children's, else 404) and lazy `PRESENT/PRE_POPULATED` writes only fire for managers — verified live with 6 scenarios on `tenant_demo`; (Fix 4 BLOCKING) `getStudentGradebook` filters classes to STAFF caller's `sis_class_teachers` rows so a teacher of one class can't see other classes' grades — verified live by stripping Rivera from 5 of 6 classes and watching the response drop from 4→1; (Fix 6 BLOCKING) `InvoiceService.cancel` writes a compensating `ADJUSTMENT` ledger entry equal to `-(total - sum(completed payments))` for non-DRAFT invoices so the family balance stays correct after cancel — verified live with Tech Fee SENT cancel dropping balance $400→$0; (Fix 7 BLOCKING) shared `INVOICE_AMOUNT_PAID_SQL` formula nets out completed refunds (`SUM(payments WHERE status IN (COMPLETED,REFUNDED)) - SUM(refunds WHERE status=COMPLETED, on payments for this invoice)`), `RefundService.issue` locks the parent invoice **before** the payment row to match `PaymentService.pay`'s lock order, and after the ledger write recomputes invoice status PAID ↔ PARTIAL ↔ SENT — verified live with $400 pay→PAID→$50 refund→PARTIAL→$50 pay→PAID; (Fix 8 BLOCKING) `generateFromSchedule` per-family invoice insert now runs inside `executeInTenantTransaction` with a per-(family, fee_schedule) `pg_advisory_xact_lock` and in-tx existence re-check — verified live with 5 parallel calls landing exactly 1 winner + 4 skipped; (Fix 9 MAJOR) `CapacitySummaryService.recompute` takes a per-(period, grade) advisory tx lock so concurrent transitions serialise — verified by build-artifact + happy-path; (Fix 10 MAJOR) `OfferService.issue` no longer emits `guardianPersonId: dto.familyResponse` (copy-paste bug that always emitted null on fresh ISSUED offers); now reads `guardian_person_id` from the locked application snapshot returned by the inner tx — verified live by capturing the wire envelope on `dev.enr.offer.issued` showing the correct guardian id; (Fix 11 MAJOR) `PaymentAccountWorker.createOrLinkAccount` takes a per-school advisory tx lock before computing `MAX(account_number)+1` so concurrent enrolments serialise on the FA-#### allocation — verified by build-artifact inspection; (Fix 12 MINOR) tenant-resolver JSDoc rewritten to accurately describe the actual middleware (header-first `X-Tenant-Subdomain`, then hostname). The `RefundService.issue` lock-order change (invoice → payment) now matches `PaymentService.pay`'s order. See `REVIEW-CYCLE6-CHATGPT.md` for the full triage table + verification trail. **Cycle 6 ships clean to Round 2 of the post-cycle architecture review.** **Step 12 lands `docs/cycle6-cat-script.md`** — the reproducible end-to-end vertical slice CAT, verified live against `tenant_demo` on 2026-04-29. The script wraps a 4-check schema preamble (106 tenant base tables / 10 enr* tables / 12 pay* tables / 0 cross-schema FKs, plus row-count verification of the 19 seeded tables) around the plan's 12 scenarios: S1 admin admissions pipeline (4 seeded applications across SUBMITTED→ENROLLED); S2 parent row-scope (David Chen sees only Maya); S3 parent submits new "CatStep12 Chen" Grade 9 application → guardian*person*id stamped from `actor.personId`, capacity_summary recomputes Grade 9 received 3→4; S4 admin transitions SUBMITTED→UNDER_REVIEW→ACCEPTED with `assertTransitionAllowed` + row lock + admin notes; S5 admin issues UNCONDITIONAL offer (response_deadline +14d); **S6 keystone — parent ACCEPTS offer**: offer flips to ACCEPTED + application to ENROLLED + capacity_summary Grade 9 offers_accepted 1→2 / available 109→108 + **`enr.student.enrolled` envelope captured live** on `dev.enr.student.enrolled` with full ADR-057 shape (`source_module:'enrollment'`, payload includes applicationId/offerId/schoolId/enrollmentPeriodId/studentFirstName='CatStep12'/studentLastName='Chen'/studentDateOfBirth/gradeLevel=9/admissionType=NEW_STUDENT/guardianPersonId=David Chen iam_person.id/guardianEmail/enrolledAt) + **PaymentAccountWorker reaction verified**: UPSERT idempotency hit (FA-1001 already exists for David), graceful skip of `pay_family_account_students` insert because `sis_students` row for CatStep12 doesn't exist yet (the documented Step 7 edge case for the future EnrollmentConfirmedWorker); S7 admin billing reads (1 family account, 2 invoices); S8 `generate-from-schedule` idempotency check on Tech Fee 2026 → `{created:0, skipped:1}` (UNIQUE-by-(family, fee_schedule_id, non-CANCELLED status) catches the seeded SENT row); S9 parent partial-pay $200 → invoice flips SENT→PARTIAL + balance 400→200 + PAYMENT ledger entry + **`pay.payment.received` envelope captured live** with full ADR-057 shape (payload includes paymentId/invoiceId/familyAccountId/amount/paymentMethod=CARD/invoiceStatus=PARTIAL/totalAmount/amountPaid/paidAt); S10 parent pays remaining $200 → invoice flips PARTIAL→PAID + balance 200→0 + second PAYMENT row + second `pay.payment.received` envelope + ledger SUM = $0 over 5 entries + overpay attempt 400 ("Invoice is already PAID"); **S11 admin partial-refund $50** → REFUND ledger entry restoring balance 0→50 + payment stays COMPLETED (partial-refund leaves it as-is, only full-amount flips to REFUNDED) + ledger SUM = $50 over 6 entries + **`pay.refund.issued` envelope captured live** with full ADR-057 shape (payload includes refundId/paymentId/familyAccountId/amount/refundCategory=GOODWILL/reason/status=COMPLETED/authorisedBy/completedAt + mock `re_dev*<uuid>`Stripe id); S12 6 permission denials covering all three tiers — gate-tier 403 (teacher /family-accounts no fin-001:read; student POST /pay no fin-001:write; parent POST /fee-categories no fin-001:admin; parent PATCH /applications/{Aiden's}/status no stu-003:admin), service-layer admin-only (parent /refunds "Only admins can list refunds"), service-layer self-service rule (parent CASH method "Self-service parent payments accept CARD or BANK_TRANSFER only"). **All 12 scenarios pass.** Cleanup section drops the new application + 1 refund + 2 payments + 3 ledger entries + resets Tech Fee invoice back to SENT + restores capacity_summary so the next run starts clean. Final balance read confirms`$400 cached:true`. **Known gaps surfaced by the CAT (Phase 2 punch list, not Cycle 6 scope):** PaymentAccountWorker's `pay_family_account_students`link is correctly skipped when`sis_students`doesn't exist yet — the future EnrollmentConfirmedWorker will materialise`sis_students`from`enr_applications`on enroll and re-emit`enr.student.enrolled`, at which point the worker will idempotently insert the link; receipt PDF download (the `pay_payments.receipt_s3_key`is nullable and currently NULL — Phase 3 ops); real Stripe API wiring (CARD auto-COMPLETE with mock`pi_dev\**`ids, refunds with mock`re*dev*\_`ids — Phase 3 ops). **Cycle 6 ships clean to the post-cycle architecture review.** **Step 11 lands the Parent Billing UI** at`apps/web/src/app/(app)/billing/page.tsx`+`apps/web/src/app/(app)/billing/pay/[invoiceId]/page.tsx`+`apps/web/src/app/(app)/billing/ledger/page.tsx`— 3 new parent-facing routes alongside the Step 10 admin surfaces. **No new hooks, no new types, no new backend endpoints** — sits entirely on the 23 hooks from Step 10 and the 24-endpoint payments surface from Step 7. The shared`Billing` launchpad tile (`apps/web/src/components/shell/apps.tsx`) now branches on `personType=GUARDIAN`to route guardians to`/billing`(parent dashboard) instead of`/billing/accounts`(admin landing); the gate stays`fin-001:write` so admins / staff / parents all see the tile, and the description copy switches based on persona ("Your balance, invoices, and payments" vs "Fees, invoices, family accounts, and payments"). The admin invoice detail page (`/billing/invoices/[id]`) gains a `Pay now →`button visible to anyone with`fin-001:write`whenever the invoice has a positive`balanceDue`AND status is not DRAFT/CANCELLED — clicks navigate to`/billing/pay/[invoiceId]`. **`/billing`** is the parent dashboard — auto-redirects admins (`fin-001:admin`) to `/billing/accounts`via a`useEffect`, gracefully handles non-guardian non-admin (e.g. teachers/students with `fin-001:write`on edge cases) with a "not available" empty state. For guardians, it loads`useFamilyAccounts()`(row-scoped backend returns only their own family account, typically 1 row), then for`account.id`reads`useFamilyAccountBalance`(Redis-cached, 30s stale) +`useInvoices({familyAccountId})`+`usePayments({familyAccountId})`. Renders a 3-stat header (Current balance colour-coded rose/emerald/normal / Outstanding invoices count / Children on account count) plus a children sentence ("For Maya Chen (Grade 9), Lily Chen (Grade 10)"), an Outstanding-invoices section that filters to `balanceDue > 0 AND status !== 'CANCELLED'`sorted by due-date ascending (each row shows title + due date + status pill + 3-stat (Total / Paid / Balance due) + a`Pay now →`link to`/billing/pay/{id}`when status is not DRAFT/CANCELLED), and a "Recent payments" section showing the 5 newest payments with method / amount / status + a`See all →`link to`/billing/payments`(which already works for parents because the global payments page is gated on`fin-001:write`and the backend is row-scoped). Header`View ledger`button to`/billing/ledger`. **`/billing/pay/[invoiceId]`** is the payment form — gated on `fin-001:write`, refuses to render the form when the invoice is DRAFT/CANCELLED or `balanceDue=0`("This invoice can't be paid" empty state). Loads`useInvoice(invoiceId)`; defaults the amount input to the full `balanceDue`via a`useEffect`that fires once on first load, with a`Pay full balance ($X)`quick-fill button next to the input. Method picker is a chip group constrained to CARD + BANK_TRANSFER for non-admins (matches the Step 7 backend`assertSelfServiceMethod`check that returns 403 on CASH/CHEQUE/WAIVER for non-admins) and the full 5-method menu for admins. CARD method shows a sky-tinted hint ("Card payments are processed in test mode. No real card is charged — the invoice will be marked paid immediately."), BANK_TRANSFER shows an amber hint ("payment is recorded immediately but funds usually arrive in 1–3 business days"). Optional notes textarea (500-char max). Submit calls`usePayInvoice(invoiceId).mutateAsync({amount, paymentMethod, notes})`; the Step 7 backend wraps the conflict check in one tx that locks the invoice FOR UPDATE, recomputes status PARTIAL/PAID, writes the PAYMENT ledger entry, emits `pay.payment.received`, and returns the new payment row. On success the page Toasts "Paid $X.XX by card" (or "Recorded $X.XX bank transfer payment" depending on method) and routes back to the invoice detail page. Client-side validation: amount must be > 0 AND ≤ balanceDue + 0.001 (matching the backend's overpay pre-check); submit button is disabled until valid. **`/billing/ledger`** is the parent ledger — gated on `fin-001:read`OR`fin-001:write`AND`personType=GUARDIAN`(admins are explicitly told to use`/billing/accounts/[id]`for the per-family ledger view; the page renders a "Not available" empty state for non-guardians). Loads`useFamilyAccounts()`to find the family account, then`useFamilyAccountBalance`+`useFamilyAccountLedger({limit: 100})`(newest-first per the backend default). The keystone trick is the **running-balance computation**: anchors at the current balance (the freshest read, Redis-cached) and walks the newest-first ledger backwards — for row N,`runningBalance`is the account state immediately AFTER that entry was posted; row N+1's running balance = row N's running balance − row N's signed amount. Single`useMemo`over`(ledger, balance, account.balance)`. Header card shows the current balance with a subtitle that reads "Outstanding — invoices waiting on payment." (positive) / "Credit on file — applied automatically to future invoices." (negative) / "Account is settled." (zero). Ledger table renders 5 columns — When / Type / Description / Amount (signed via `formatSignedCurrency`) / Balance (after this entry, via `formatCurrency`). Pagination footnote when 100 rows ship ("Showing the most recent 100 entries"). **Build clean** (`pnpm --filter @campusos/web build`): 3 new parent routes ship at `/billing`2.46 kB (114 kB First Load),`/billing/ledger`1.71 kB (114 kB),`/billing/pay/[invoiceId]`3.24 kB (115 kB). **Live verification on`tenant*demo`2026-04-29** (12 scenarios — 5 read-paths + 4 mutation paths + 3 permission denials, with cleanup): parent`GET /family-accounts`200 → 1 row FA-1001 David Chen balance=$400; parent`GET /invoices`200 → 2 rows; parent`GET /payments`200 → 1 row (the seeded $12000 PAID tuition); parent`GET /family-accounts/{id}/balance`200`{balance:400, cached:true}`; parent `GET /family-accounts/{id}/ledger?limit=10`200 → 3 rows newest-first; parent partial-pay $200 on Tech Fee 2026 → COMPLETED + invoice flips SENT→PARTIAL + balance 400→200 + ledger row PAYMENT −$200 + new`pi_dev*<uuid>`Stripe mock id; parent pays remaining $200 → COMPLETED + invoice flips PARTIAL→PAID + balance 200→0 + second PAYMENT row + second`pi*dev*<uuid>`; over-pay attempt 400 ("Invoice is already PAID"); parent CASH method 403 ("Self-service parent payments accept CARD or BANK_TRANSFER only" — backend's `assertSelfServiceMethod`); student `GET /family-accounts`403 (no`fin-001:read`/write); teacher `POST /invoices/{id}/pay`403 INSUFFICIENT_PERMISSIONS at the gate (no`fin-001:write`). **Cleanup** restores tenant to seed state via direct SQL (DELETE FROM `pay*payments`+`pay_ledger_entries`for the 2 smoke payment rows +`UPDATE pay_invoices SET status='SENT'`+`redis-cli DEL ledger:balance:{accountId}`); final balance read confirms `$400 cached:true`so the next CAT run starts clean. **Out of scope this step (deferred):** receipt PDF download from`/billing/payments`(the schema's`pay_payments.receipt_s3_key`column is nullable and currently NULL for every seeded payment — a real S3 wiring is a Phase 3 ops task once Stripe lands properly per ADR-003); a parent-facing payment-plan management UI (the`usePaymentPlan`/`useCreatePaymentPlan`hooks ship in Step 10 but no surface yet — a polish item that depends on a school actually offering payment plans, which the seed doesn't); per-invoice receipt download (no`receipt_s3_key`populated in dev mode);`?before=`pagination on the ledger view (the`limit=100`window covers 99% of family accounts in the first year of operation). **Step 10 lands the Billing Admin UI at`apps/web/src/app/(app)/billing/`** — 4 routes (5 pages with the invoice + family-account detail views) + 23 React Query hooks in `apps/web/src/hooks/use-billing.ts`+ a new`Billing`launchpad tile gated on`fin-001:write`(held by School Admin / Platform Admin / Parent — Step 11's parent dashboard will reuse the same tile against the same gate, since the row-scope at`FamilyAccountService.list`/`InvoiceService.list`/`PaymentService.list`already binds parents to their own family) using a new`BanknotesIcon`in`apps/web/src/components/shell/icons.tsx`. **`/billing/accounts`** is the default landing — directory of family accounts with a 3-stat header (Active accounts / Outstanding balance / Total accounts) + a single search input that filters by account number, holder name, holder email, or any linked student name; rows show account number / holder + email / linked students with grade / status pill / balance colour-coded (rose for owed, emerald for credit, gray for $0). **`/billing/accounts/[id]`** is the per-family detail page — header with account number + holder + status pill + 3-stat (current balance / students / auth policy) on top of the seeded balance from `useFamilyAccount`(overridden by the fresher`useFamilyAccountBalance`Redis-cached read when it lands); linked-students cards; Invoices table with click-through to invoice detail; Payments table with status pills + click-through to invoice; Ledger table rendered newest-first with type pills (CHARGE rose / PAYMENT emerald / REFUND violet / CREDIT sky / ADJUSTMENT gray) + signed amounts via`formatSignedCurrency` (positive rose, negative emerald). **`/billing/invoices`** is the global invoice list — 6+1 status filter chips (All / Draft / Sent / Partial / Paid / Overdue / Cancelled), an account dropdown, a `Generate from schedule`Modal admin-only that POSTs`/invoices/generate-from-schedule`(one DRAFT per linked family for the schedule's grade — backend is idempotent and returns`{created, skipped, invoiceIds}`); rows show invoice title (link to detail) / family / total / paid / balance / due date / status pill. **`/billing/invoices/[id]`** is the invoice detail — header with title + family link + status pill + admin Send-invoice (DRAFT only) and Cancel buttons (anything other than PAID/CANCELLED); 4-stat (Total / Paid / Balance due / Due date); line-items table with fee-schedule attribution shown when present; payment-history table below. The Send button calls `PATCH /invoices/:id/send`which the Step 7 backend wraps in a tx that locks the invoice FOR UPDATE, flips DRAFT→SENT under sent_chk, writes the CHARGE ledger entry, and emits`pay.invoice.created`; Cancel is `PATCH /invoices/:id/cancel` and refuses on PAID. **`/billing/payments`** is the global payment tracker — 4+1 status chips (All / Pending / Completed / Failed / Refunded), method dropdown, account dropdown; rows show paid-at / family link / invoice link / method / amount / status pill; per-row admin-only `Refund`button on COMPLETED rows opens a Modal that pre-fills the refund amount to the full payment amount and forces a 6-value`refundCategory`+ a`reason`(1000-char max) before POSTing`/payments/:id/refund` (Step 7 backend SUM-of-prior-refunds pre-flight rejects over-cap; partials leave the payment as COMPLETED, full refunds flip to REFUNDED). **`/billing/fees`** is the fee management surface — fee categories grid (compact cards with active pill) + admin New-category Modal that POSTs `/fee-categories`; fee schedules table with Schedule / Category / Year / Grade / Amount / Recurrence / Affected-families / Edit columns. The Affected-families counter is a client-side calculation: for each fee schedule, count active family accounts whose linked students match the schedule's `gradeLevel`(or count all active accounts when`gradeLevel=null`for "all grades"). Edit Modal mutates name/grade/amount/recurrence/isActive via`PATCH /fee-schedules/:id`; new schedules use the same Modal pattern with academic-year dropdown driven by the existing Step 8 `useAcademicYears` hook. New helper module **`apps/web/src/lib/billing-format.ts`** holds the constant arrays (`RECURRENCE_OPTIONS`, `INVOICE_STATUSES`, `PAYMENT_METHODS`, `PAYMENT_STATUSES`, `REFUND_CATEGORIES`) + per-enum label maps + per-status pill class maps for invoices/payments/family-accounts/refunds/ledger-entry-types + `formatCurrency`(Intl.NumberFormat USD with 2 decimal places) +`formatSignedCurrency`(positive prefixed`+`, negative prefixed `−`) + `formatDateOnly`/`formatDateTime`mirrors of`admissions-format.ts`so the two helper modules stay independent. The 23 hooks in`use-billing.ts`: `useFeeCategories`(5min staleTime) /`useCreateFeeCategory`; `useFeeSchedules`/`useFeeSchedule(id)`/`useCreateFeeSchedule`/`useUpdateFeeSchedule(id)`; `useFamilyAccounts`(refetch on focus) /`useFamilyAccount(id)`/`useFamilyAccountBalance(id)`(30s staleTime) /`useFamilyAccountLedger(id, args)`; `useInvoices(args)`(refetch on focus) /`useInvoice(id)`/`useCreateInvoice`/`useSendInvoice(id)`/`useCancelInvoice(id)`/`useGenerateFromSchedule`; `usePayments(args)`(refetch on focus) /`usePayment(id)`/`usePayInvoice(invoiceId)`; `useRefunds(args)`/`useIssueRefund(paymentId)`; `usePaymentPlan(id)`/`useCreatePaymentPlan(invoiceId)`. Mutation invalidations always cover the matching list query + the per-id detail query + the family-account list and detail (since balance + ledger update); `useSendInvoice`and`usePayInvoice`and`useIssueRefund`all invalidate`family-accounts`and`family-account`to keep the running balance fresh. Cycle 6 payments DTO surface added to`apps/web/src/lib/types.ts`: `Recurrence`; `FeeCategoryDto`+`CreateFeeCategoryPayload`; `FeeScheduleDto`+`CreateFeeSchedulePayload`+`UpdateFeeSchedulePayload`; `FamilyAccountStatus`/`PaymentAuthPolicy`/`FamilyAccountStudentDto`/`FamilyAccountDto`; `InvoiceStatus`/`InvoiceLineItemDto`/`InvoiceLineItemInputDto`/`InvoiceDto`/`CreateInvoicePayload`/`GenerateFromSchedulePayload`/`GenerateFromScheduleResponse`/`ListInvoicesArgs`; `PaymentMethod`/`PaymentStatus`/`PaymentDto`/`PayInvoicePayload`/`ListPaymentsArgs`; `EntryType`/`LedgerEntryDto`/`LedgerBalanceDto`/`ListLedgerArgs`; `RefundCategory`/`RefundStatus`/`RefundDto`/`IssueRefundPayload`/`ListRefundsArgs`; `PlanFrequency`/`PlanStatus`/`InstallmentStatus`/`PaymentPlanInstallmentDto`/`PaymentPlanDto`/`CreatePaymentPlanPayload`. **No new API endpoints in this step** — sits entirely on Step 7's 24-endpoint payments surface. Web build clean (`pnpm --filter @campusos/web build`): 6 billing routes ship at `/billing/accounts`4.37 kB (114 kB First Load),`/billing/accounts/[id]`4.67 kB (114 kB),`/billing/fees`4.28 kB (117 kB),`/billing/invoices`5.63 kB (115 kB),`/billing/invoices/[id]`5.06 kB (114 kB),`/billing/payments`5.86 kB (115 kB). **Live verification on`tenant_demo`2026-04-28** (13 read-only scenarios, no mutations — tenant remains at seed state): principal`GET /fee-categories`200 → 4 rows (Activity Fee / Registration Fee / Technology Fee / Tuition all active); principal`GET /fee-schedules`200 → 4 rows joined to academic year + category names; principal`GET /family-accounts`200 → 1 row FA-1001 David Chen balance=$400 ACTIVE 1 student=Maya Chen Grade 9; principal`GET /invoices`200 → 2 rows (Tech Fee 2026 SENT bal=$400 due 2026-05-28; Fall 2026 Tuition PAID bal=$0 due 2025-09-01); principal`GET /payments`200 → 1 row ($12000 CARD COMPLETED); principal`GET /family-accounts/{id}/balance`200`{balance:400, cached:true}`(Redis hit from the Step 7 smoke); principal`GET /family-accounts/{id}/ledger?limit=10`200 → 3 rows newest-first (CHARGE +$400 Tech Fee 2026-04-28, PAYMENT −$12000 2025-08-20, CHARGE +$12000 2025-08-15); parent (David Chen)`GET /family-accounts`200 → only FA-1001 (row-scope on`account_holder_id = actor.personId`); parent `GET /invoices`200 → only own family's 2 rows; teacher`GET /family-accounts`403 (no`fin-001:read`); student `GET /invoices`403; teacher`POST /fee-categories`403 (no`fin-001:admin`); parent `GET /refunds`403 ("Only admins can list refunds" — service-layer check). The mutation paths (create-category, create-schedule, generate-from-schedule, send-invoice, issue-refund) reuse the exact payload shapes verified end-to-end in Step 7's 30-scenario smoke; Step 10 is purely additive on the web side and the page wiring matches the controller contracts. **Out of scope this step (deferred):** payment-plan management UI (the hook + Modal landing here would need an installment-progression view with mark-overdue actions; punted to Step 11 since parents are the primary plan consumers); refund-history page (refund rows are visible inline on the family-account detail's ledger panel via the REFUND entry, but a dedicated`/billing/refunds`list would surface filter/search across all families — a polish item); invoice manual-create with custom line items (the API supports it via`POST /invoices`, but Step 10 ships only the `Generate from schedule`bulk path — the manual create form is reasonable for unusual one-off charges that would otherwise land via SQL or by hand); per-account close/suspend transitions (the schema's`status`column accepts`SUSPENDED`/`CLOSED`but there's no current admin UI for it — punted because the canonical reason to close an account is the student withdrawing from school, which Cycle 7+ will surface). **Step 9 lands the Parent Application & Enrollment UI at`apps/web/src/app/(app)/apply/`+`apps/web/src/app/(app)/offers/[id]/`** — 3 routes + a new parent `Apply`launchpad tile gated on`personType=GUARDIAN`AND`stu-003:write`(the existing admin`Admissions`tile from Step 8 stays gated on`stu-003:admin`, so parents and admins never collide on the same tile). Reuses Step 8's `AcademicCapIcon`, the existing enrollment hooks, and `admissions-format.ts`; no new helper modules. **`/apply`** is the parent landing — combines a "Your applications" tracker and a Start-new entry point in one page (the plan's separate `/applications`tracker route folded in here for fewer surfaces); fetches own apps via`useApplications({})`(row-scoped at`ApplicationService.list`to`guardian_person_id = actor.personId`); each row shows student name + grade + period + stream + status pill + the latest 3 non-confidential admin notes; rows with an ISSUED offer surface a Respond callout in sky-tinted card with `formatRelativeDeadline`countdown that links to`/offers/:id`; rows with an ACCEPTED offer show an emerald confirmation; if at least one OPEN period exists the header shows a Start-new-application button + a green emerald banner listing the open windows. **`/apply/new`** is the application form — single-page with sectioned cards (Admission window / Student details / Guardian contact / Screening responses), auto-selects the only OPEN period when there's exactly one, filters available streams to `isActive=true`, derives the grade dropdown options from the period's `intake_capacities`rows so parents only see grades the school is accepting, auto-populates`guardianEmail`from`useAuthStore.user.email`(editable), 3-chip admission-type group, screening responses are key/value rows the parent can add/remove (free-form`responseValue`per Step 6's`@Allow()`decorator on`ScreeningResponseInputDto.responseValue`); Submit POSTs `/applications`and routes back to`/apply` on success. **`/offers/[id]`** is the response page — header with student name + grade + status pill + offer-type label; sky-tinted Respond-by callout with `formatRelativeDeadline`for ISSUED offers; conditions panel for CONDITIONAL offers (lists`offerConditions`array, shows whether the school has marked`conditionsMet=true/false/null`and **the Accept button is disabled while`conditionsMet !== true`on a CONDITIONAL offer** so parents can't bypass the schema invariant client-side); 3 response buttons Accept / Decline / Defer; Defer reveals an academic-year selector (driven by`useAcademicYears`, filters out the current year so the target is always a future year per the schema's `deferral_target_year_id`semantics); on Accept the page re-fetches the offer (which flips to`status=ACCEPTED`, `familyResponse=ACCEPTED`, application=`ENROLLED`) and renders an inline emerald **Welcome confirmation banner** ("🎉 Welcome! {studentName} has been enrolled in Grade {N}. A tuition invoice will be generated shortly — check the Billing section once it arrives.") with quick links back to `/apply`and to`/children`. The `useRespondToOffer(id)`hook also invalidates`['notifications']`so the parent's bell badge picks up the email-style ack notification queued by`OfferService.respond`. **No new API endpoints land in this step** — sits entirely on Step 6's enrollment surface. Build clean (`pnpm --filter @campusos/web build`): **3 parent admissions routes ship** at `/apply`2.95 kB (114 kB First Load),`/apply/new`3.65 kB (114 kB),`/offers/[id]`4.4 kB (115 kB). **Live verification on`tenant_demo`2026-04-29** (5 reads + 1 end-to-end submit + 1 row-scope 404 + cleanup): parent`GET /enrollment-periods`200 → 1 OPEN Fall 2026 Admissions period with 2 streams + grade options [9, 10]; parent`GET /applications`200 → 1 row (Maya Chen ENROLLED with 1 note); parent`GET /offers`200 → 1 row (Maya UNCONDITIONAL ACCEPTED); parent`GET /academic-years`200 (Step 8 endpoint reused for the Defer year picker); parent`GET /offers/{Maya's}`200; parent`GET /offers/{Sophia's unrelated}`404 (row-scope at`OfferService.getById`); parent `POST /applications`for fictional "Step9Smoke Test" → 201 with`guardianPersonId`correctly stamped from David Chen's`iam_person.id`, `submittedAt`populated, screening row saved, status=SUBMITTED; parent's tracker now shows 2 rows; cleanup`DELETE FROM enr_applications WHERE student_first_name='Step9Smoke'`restores tenant to seed state. Mutation paths beyond submit (Accept / Decline / Defer) reuse the exact payload shapes Step 6 verified live — the`OfferService.respond`row-scope check at`enr_offers.application_id → enr_applications.guardian_person_id == actor.personId`is the access gate. Out of scope this step (deferred): save-as-draft (the API requires SUBMITTED on first POST per the schema's`submitted_chk`; a true DRAFT-status flow would need a separate PATCH path); document upload from the parent side (the `enr_application_documents`schema is ready, but the signed-S3-URL upload endpoint doesn't exist yet); per-school configurable screening-question schema (parents currently compose free-form`(questionKey, responseValue)`pairs). **Step 8 lands the Enrollment Admin UI at`apps/web/src/app/(app)/admissions/`** — 5 routes + 16 React Query hooks in `apps/web/src/hooks/use-enrollment.ts`+ a new`Admissions` launchpad tile gated on **`stu-003:admin`** (tightened from the plan's `stu-003:write`because parents already hold`stu-003:write`after Step 5; Step 9 will introduce a separate parent-facing`Apply`tile) using a new`AcademicCapIcon`in`apps/web/src/components/shell/icons.tsx`. **`/admissions/applications`** is the default landing — pipeline view grouping `ApplicationDto`rows by status across 6 columns SUBMITTED → UNDER_REVIEW → ACCEPTED → WAITLISTED → REJECTED → ENROLLED, filterable by enrollment period and grade; the period filter accepts a`?enrollmentPeriodId=` query param so deep-links from the period detail surface the right slice. **`/admissions/periods`** lists periods with status pills (UPCOMING/OPEN/CLOSED), Open/Close transition buttons, and a 6-stat capacity rollup totalling Places / Applications / Offers / Accepted / Waitlisted / Available across all grades; New-period modal collects academic year (driven by the new `useAcademicYears`hook), name, opens-at, closes-at, and an`allowsMidYearApplications` toggle. **`/admissions/periods/[id]`** is the period detail with the per-grade Capacity Dashboard inline — table rendering `capacitySummary`rows with green/amber/red Available colour coding (red when`available <= 0`, amber when `available < totalPlaces\*0.1`); admission stream list with active/inactive pills; Add-stream and Add-capacity modals (the capacity modal accepts an optional stream selector when streams exist for the period). **`/admissions/applications/[id]`** is the keystone review page — header card with avatar + DOB + admission type + status pill + 4 status-transition buttons (Move to review / Accept / Waitlist / Reject) + Issue-Offer button surfaced when status=ACCEPTED and no offer exists yet; the **OfferPanel** renders inline below the header when `useOffers().find(o.applicationId === id)`returns a row — for ISSUED offers shows offer-type/issued/deadline + a CONDITIONAL conditions amber panel with Mark-met/Mark-failed admin actions and Accept/Decline proxy buttons that call`PATCH /offers/:id/respond`(Accept disabled until conditions resolved, surfacing the schema's CONDITIONAL invariant); guardian + timeline cards; screening responses rendered as key/value rows with`formatScreeningValue`handling the JSONB free-form value; documents list; **NoteComposer** at the top of the notes timeline letting admin pick a 6-value`note_type`and toggle`isConfidential`(admin-only — confidential notes are hidden from parents per`ApplicationService.list`); plus an emerald banner under the OfferPanel when status=ENROLLED noting that `enr.student.enrolled` has fired so the PaymentAccountWorker will create the family account. **`/admissions/waitlist`** is ordered by grade then position with status filter chips defaulted to ACTIVE plus an All chip; per-row Offer-this-spot button on ACTIVE entries opens a Modal that PATCHes `/waitlist/:id/offer` with a date deadline, atomically promoting the waitlist entry to OFFERED + the application to ACCEPTED + creating the new offer row in one tx. New helper module **`apps/web/src/lib/admissions-format.ts`** holds status labels and pill class maps for periods/applications/offers/waitlist + `NOTE_TYPE_LABELS`+`formatStudentName`+`formatDateOnly`/`formatDateTime`+`formatRelativeDeadline`(renders`responseDeadline`as "in N days" / "in N weeks" / "expired YYYY-MM-DD") +`todayIso`/`addDaysIso`. The 16 hooks: `useAcademicYears`(5min staleTime),`useEnrollmentPeriods`/`useEnrollmentPeriod`, `useCreateEnrollmentPeriod`/`useUpdateEnrollmentPeriod(id)`, `useCreateAdmissionStream(periodId)`/`useCreateIntakeCapacity(periodId)`, `useApplications(args)`/`useApplication(id)`, `useCreateApplication`, `useUpdateApplicationStatus(id)`, `useAddApplicationNote(id)`, `useOffers`/`useOffer(id)`, `useIssueOffer(applicationId)`, `useSetOfferConditionsMet(id)`/`useRespondToOffer(id)`(the respond mutation also invalidates`['notifications']`), `useWaitlist(args)`/`useOfferFromWaitlist(id)`(invalidates waitlist + applications + offers + periods because all four denormalisations change atomically). Cycle 6 enrollment DTO surface added to`apps/web/src/lib/types.ts`: `AcademicYearDto`, `EnrollmentPeriodDto`+ nested`IntakeCapacityDto`/`AdmissionStreamDto`/`CapacitySummaryRowDto`, the period payloads, `ApplicationDto`+ nested`ScreeningResponseDto`/`ApplicationDocumentDto`/`ApplicationNoteDto`(plus all 7`ApplicationStatus`values, 5`AdminTransitionTarget`values, 3`AdmissionType`values, 6`ApplicationNoteType`values),`OfferDto`+`OfferType`/`OfferStatus`/`FamilyResponse`enums + the 3 offer payloads,`WaitlistEntryDto`+`WaitlistStatus`enum + the 2 waitlist payloads. **One small additive API endpoint lands alongside the UI:**`GET /academic-years`on a new`AcademicYearController`wired into`SisModule`and gated on`stu-001:read`— needed by the create-period dropdown which has to pick an`academicYearId`; reads `sis_academic_years`directly with`executeInTenantContext`and returns`{id, name, startDate, endDate, isCurrent}` rows ordered by start_date DESC.
