Mutation paths (create-period, status transitions, issue-offer, conditions-met verify, accept-by-proxy, add-note, offer-from-waitlist) reuse the exact payload shapes verified end-to-end in Step 6 + Step 7 smoke runs; Step 8 is purely additive on the web side and the page wiring matches the controller contracts. Out of scope this step (deferred): dedicated period editor (only status-toggle + add-stream / add-capacity modals; full PATCH name/dates editing is a future polish), application notes filter/search, document upload from the admin side (Step 5 seed planted documents directly; the upload UI is part of the parent-facing Step 9), waitlist position drag-reorder. **Step 7 lands`apps/api/src/payments/`** — the keystone Cycle 6 request-path module that closes the enrollment→payments event loop. 7 services + 6 controllers + **24 endpoints** + 3 Kafka emit topics (`pay.invoice.created`/`pay.payment.received`/`pay.refund.issued`) + the **`PaymentAccountWorker`** Kafka consumer subscribed to `dev.enr.student.enrolled`under group`payment-account-worker`. **`LedgerService`** is the foundation — internal-only `recordEntry(tx, ...)`called inside open tenant transactions by InvoiceService.send / PaymentService.pay / RefundService.issue so every ledger write is atomic with the lifecycle change that caused it; balance reads go through Redis at`ledger:balance:{accountId}`TTL=30s with cache invalidation inside the same write tx (three new helpers added to`RedisService`—`getLedgerBalance`/`setLedgerBalance`/`invalidateLedgerBalance`); `pay_ledger_entries` is IMMUTABLE per ADR-010 — no UPDATE/DELETE service methods. **`FeeScheduleService`** — admin-only fee category + schedule CRUD on `fin-001:admin`; reads on `fin-001:read`. **`FamilyAccountService`** — list / getById / listStudents / balance / ledger; row-scoped on `account_holder_id = actor.personId` for parents; admins see all. **`InvoiceService`** — create DRAFT / list / get / **send (locks invoice FOR UPDATE inside the tx, flips DRAFT→SENT under sent_chk, writes CHARGE ledger entry, emits `pay.invoice.created`)** / cancel (refuses on PAID — issue refund instead) / `generate-from-schedule`bulk (one DRAFT invoice per linked family for the schedule's grade; idempotent — re-running returns`created=0 skipped=N` for families that already have a non-CANCELLED invoice attributed to that fee_schedule_id). **`PaymentService`** — list / get / **pay (locks invoice FOR UPDATE in same tx, writes PAYMENT ledger entry with negative amount, recomputes invoice status PARTIAL/PAID from running total, emits `pay.payment.received`)** with row-scope (parent must be the account holder); refuses overpay against balance-due pre-check; Stripe stubbed in dev — CARD payments mark COMPLETED immediately with mock `pi_dev*<uuid>` intent id; self-service parent payments accept CARD or BANK_TRANSFER only. **`RefundService`** — admin-only list (service-layer 403 for non-admins) / **issue (locks payment FOR UPDATE, writes REFUND ledger entry positive amount restoring balance, marks payment REFUNDED if full-amount, emits `pay.refund.issued`)**; refund cap pre-check via SUM(prior refunds) prevents over-refund; partial refunds leave the payment as COMPLETED; Stripe stubbed with mock `re*dev*<uuid>`. **`PaymentPlanService`** — admin-only POST creates plan + auto-generates installment rows in one tx; due dates = startDate + n × (1 month \| 3 months); last installment absorbs sub-cent residue. **`PaymentAccountWorker`** subscribes to `dev.enr.student.enrolled`under group`payment-account-worker`; reuses the `unwrapEnvelope`+`processWithIdempotency`(claim-after-success per REVIEW-CYCLE2 BLOCKING 2) base helpers from Cycle 3; per inbound event UPSERTs`pay*family_accounts`keyed on (school, account_holder_id) with auto-allocated`FA-####`account_number from`MAX(account_number)+1`and idempotently inserts`pay_family_account_students`if the matching`sis_students`row already exists; gracefully skips when`guardian_person_id`is null (admin-submitted application — Step 6 case) or`sis_students`doesn't exist yet (the future EnrollmentConfirmedWorker will materialise it from`enr_applications` and re-emit). PaymentsModule wired between EnrollmentModule and NotificationsModule; imports TenantModule + IamModule + KafkaModule + NotificationsModule (for RedisService access). Build clean, all 24 routes mapped on boot, payment-account-worker subscription confirmed live. **`seed-iam.ts`updated mid-smoke:** Parent role gains FIN-001:write (parent now 15 perms, was 14). The Step 5 plan said "FIN-001 read to Parent" but the parent self-service Pay Now action demonstrably needs write at the gate — the row-scope check at`PaymentService.pay`(account_holder_id == actor.personId) keeps parents bound to their own family account. **Live verification on`tenant_demo` (30 scenarios, 2026-04-28):** principal lists 4 fee categories + 4 fee schedules with academic-year + category names joined; parent reads own FA-1001 with balance $400 + 1 student (Maya); balance Redis cache hits on second read (`cached:true`); ledger view shows 3 seeded entries (CHARGE +$12k, PAYMENT -$12k, CHARGE +$400) summing to $400; parent partial-pay $200 → invoice PARTIAL + balance $200; parent pays remaining $200 → PAID + balance $0; overpay rejected 400; **`pay.payment.received`envelopes (×2: PARTIAL+PAID) captured live on`dev.pay.payment.received`** with full ADR-057 shape and `source_module:'payments'`; admin refunds $200 → status COMPLETED + balance back to $200 + payment REFUNDED + `re_dev**`mock Stripe id; second-refund attempt 400 ("only COMPLETED payments are refundable"); parent ledger now 6 entries with the REFUND visible; parent service-layer 403 on`/refunds`; bulk `generate-from-schedule`on Tech Fee →`created=0 skipped=1`proving idempotency on existing invoices; **PaymentAccountWorker keystone test:** parent submits Lily Chen application → admin transitions to ACCEPTED → admin issues offer → parent ACCEPTs →`enr.student.enrolled`fires → worker logs`[payment-account-worker] reusing existing pay_family_accounts.id=… for guardianPersonId=…`(UPSERT idempotency hit because David already has FA-1001) and`no sis_students row for Lily Chen yet — skipping link, will be picked up on later re-emit`(graceful handling of the documented edge case where`sis_students` materialisation is deferred to a future EnrollmentConfirmedWorker); 5 permission denial paths all 403 (teacher /fee-schedules, parent POST /fee-schedules, student GET /invoices, parent POST /payment-plan, parent GET /refunds service-layer). **`tenant_demo`restored to seed state** post-smoke via direct SQL DELETE/UPDATE so downstream Step 8 (Enrollment admin UI) starts from the seeded shape. **Step 6 lands the first Cycle 6 NestJS request-path module at`apps/api/src/enrollment/`** with 5 services + 4 controllers + 17 endpoints + 5 Kafka emit topics (`enr.application.submitted`, `enr.application.status_changed`, `enr.offer.issued`, `enr.offer.responded`, **`enr.student.enrolled`** — the topic the future Step 7 `PaymentAccountWorker`will subscribe to).`EnrollmentPeriodService`(period CRUD + nested stream + capacity create; admin writes lock the row FOR UPDATE inside the tx; window CHECK re-validated after PATCH against the merged state).`ApplicationService`(parent submission with`guardian_person_id`stamped from`actor.personId`; admin status transitions with **`assertTransitionAllowed`function** rejecting illegal moves at the service layer — only`SUBMITTED→UNDER_REVIEW/ACCEPTED/REJECTED/WAITLISTED/WITHDRAWN`, `UNDER_REVIEW→ACCEPTED/REJECTED/WAITLISTED/WITHDRAWN`, `WAITLISTED→ACCEPTED/REJECTED/WITHDRAWN`, `ACCEPTED→WITHDRAWN`; ENROLLED is reserved for `OfferService.respond`accept path; admin patch locks row FOR UPDATE and recomputes capacity_summary in same tx; non-admin reads strip`is_confidential=true`notes from the response).`OfferService`(admin-only`POST /applications/:id/offer`; CONDITIONAL offers require `conditions_met=true` before parent ACCEPT; **`PATCH /offers/:id/respond`locks BOTH`enr_offers`AND`enr_applications`FOR UPDATE in the same tx — Cycle 5 review carry-over satisfied** — on ACCEPT, application transitions to ENROLLED + capacity_summary recomputes +`enr.student.enrolled`fires with full student/guardian/grade payload; on DECLINED, only the offer flips; on DEFERRED, family_response set but offer.status stays ISSUED so the partial INDEX on response_deadline keeps tracking the deadline).`WaitlistService`(admin-only list — service-layer 403 for non-admins;`PATCH /waitlist/:id/offer`locks waitlist + parent app FOR UPDATE in one tx, flips waitlist status to OFFERED + application to ACCEPTED + inserts new`enr_offers`row atomically).`CapacitySummaryService`(internal-only —`recompute(tx, periodId, grade)`aggregates`enr_intake_capacities`+`enr_applications`+`enr_offers`and UPSERTs the summary row; called inside open tenant transactions by ApplicationService and OfferService on every status flip; no DB triggers per the plan, service owns the formula). EnrollmentModule wired into`AppModule.imports`between`SchedulingModule`and`NotificationsModule`; imports `TenantModule + IamModule + KafkaModule`. Build clean (`pnpm --filter @campusos/api build`→`nest build`succeeds), all 17 routes mapped on boot. **Live verification on`tenant_demo`(16 scenarios across 4 personas, 2026-04-28):** principal sees 4 applications + 2 offers; parent (David Chen) sees only Maya's 1 application + 1 offer (row scope on`guardian_person_id`); teacher and student get 403 at gate (no `stu-003:read`); confidential REFERENCE_CHECK note on Sophia's application hidden from parent payload (`confidentialNotes=0`); admin transitions Aiden SUBMITTED→UNDER_REVIEW→ACCEPTED with reviewedAt + reviewedBy populated and an admin note created in the same tx; admin issues offer (UNCONDITIONAL, 14d deadline); second-offer attempt returns 400 ("Application already has an offer issued") proving the schema's UNIQUE on application_id is the safety net; admin accepts offer by proxy → offer.ACCEPTED + application.ENROLLED + family_responded_at populated under response_pair_chk; **`enr.student.enrolled`envelope captured live on`dev.enr.student.enrolled`** with full ADR-057 envelope shape — `event_type='enr.student.enrolled'`, `source_module='enrollment'`, `tenant_id`populated, payload includes applicationId/offerId/schoolId/enrollmentPeriodId/studentFirstName/studentLastName/studentDateOfBirth/gradeLevel/admissionType/guardianPersonId/guardianEmail/enrolledAt — exactly what Step 7's PaymentAccountWorker needs; due_date nullable for one-off / open-ended bills; INDEX(school, status) + INDEX(account, due_date));`pay_invoice_line_items`(CASCADE on invoice; nullable no-cascade FK to`pay_fee_schedules`so historical attribution survives schedule deactivation;`quantity NUMERIC(6,2) > 0`, `unit_price NUMERIC(10,2) >= 0`, `total NUMERIC(10,2) >= 0`— total denormalised so the formula stays in service code);`pay_payments`(no-cascade FKs to invoice + family account so payments preserve audit even when the invoice is cancelled;`amount NUMERIC(10,2) > 0`— refunds are a separate row not a negative payment; 5-value`entry_type`CHECK CHARGE/PAYMENT/REFUND/CREDIT/ADJUSTMENT; signed`amount NUMERIC(10,2)`— CHARGE positive, PAYMENT negative by convention, schema imposes no sign rule so corrections have full freedom; soft refs`school_id`→`platform.schools`and`account_holder_id`→`platform.iam_person`); `pay_family_account_students`(UNIQUE(family_account_id, student_id) link table; ON DELETE CASCADE on both family account and`sis_students`consistent with the rest of the sis_students child-table convention; 2-value `offer_type` CHECK UNCONDITIONAL/CONDITIONAL with multi-column `conditions_chk` enforcing UNCONDITIONAL ⇒ both columns NULL and CONDITIONAL ⇒ `cardinality(offer_conditions) > 0` (NOT `array_length(*, 1) > 0`—`array*length`returns NULL on empty arrays so empty-array CONDITIONAL offers slipped through; smoke caught it; switched to`cardinality()`and added an idempotent`ALTER TABLE … DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS … ; ADD CONSTRAINT …`tail block); 6-value`status`CHECK ISSUED/ACCEPTED/DECLINED/EXPIRED/WITHDRAWN/CONDITIONS_NOT_MET; nullable 3-value`family_response`ACCEPTED/DECLINED/DEFERRED with multi-column`response_pair_chk`keeping family_response + family_responded_at all-set or all-null together; multi-column`deferred_chk`requiring`deferral_target_year_id`when family_response=DEFERRED;`deadline_chk`response_deadline > issued_at; soft FK`deferral_target_year_id`to`sis_academic_years`; partial INDEX(response_deadline) WHERE status='ISSUED' for the future expiry sweep job); `enr_waitlist_entries`(UNIQUE(enrollment_period_id, application_id) one waitlist row per (period, application), CASCADE on both period and application; **Step 1 lands `packages/database/prisma/tenant/migrations/018_enr_enrollment_and_applications.sql` with 8 base tables: `enr_enrollment_periods` (3-value `status` CHECK UPCOMING/OPEN/CLOSED, `closes_at > opens_at` window CHECK, DB-enforced FK to `sis_academic_years`, UNIQUE(school*id, academic_year_id, name), `allows_mid_year_applications` flag, INDEX(school_id, status)); `enr_admission_streams` (FK ON DELETE CASCADE on the period, nullable `grade_level` for "any grade" streams, optional per-stream window with consistency CHECK, UNIQUE(period, name)); `enr_intake_capacities` (FK ON DELETE CASCADE on both period and stream, nullable `stream_id`, UNIQUE INDEX `(enrollment_period_id, COALESCE(stream_id, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'::uuid), grade_level)` — same COALESCE-sentinel trick as Cycle 5's `sch_periods` UNIQUE on `COALESCE(day_of_week, -1)` so a NULL-stream row and a specific-stream row coexist for the same (period, grade), 0 ≤ reserved_places ≤ total_places CHECK); `enr_capacity_summary` (UNIQUE(period, grade), 6 counter columns + `available INT` maintained by the future Step 6 CapacitySummaryService via UPSERT, composite non-negative CHECK on every counter); `enr_applications` (8-value `status` enum DRAFT/SUBMITTED/UNDER_REVIEW/ACCEPTED/REJECTED/WAITLISTED/WITHDRAWN/ENROLLED, 3-value `admission_type` CHECK NEW_STUDENT/TRANSFER/MID_YEAR_ADMISSION, soft nullable `guardian_person_id` to platform.iam_person — parent applying for the first time may not yet have an iam_person row, the OfferService back-fills on accept; multi-column `submitted_chk` keeps submitted_at in lockstep with status — DRAFT ⇔ submitted_at NULL, non-DRAFT ⇔ submitted_at NOT NULL — so a buggy service can't land a half-state row; multi-column `reviewed_chk` keeps reviewed_at + reviewed_by all-set or all-null together); `enr_application_screening_responses` (JSONB `response_value` so schools configure their own question shapes without schema migrations, UNIQUE(application_id, question_key)); `enr_application_documents` (signed-S3-URL pattern matching Cycle 4's `hr_employee_documents`, file_size_bytes >= 0 CHECK); `enr_application_notes` (6-value `note_type` CHECK INTERVIEW_NOTES/ASSESSMENT_RESULT/STAFF_OBSERVATION/REFERENCE_CHECK/VISIT_NOTES/GENERAL, `is_confidential` flag — Step 9's parent-portal application status tracker filters confidential rows out, admin pipeline shows everything). 10 intra-tenant DB-enforced FKs across the 8 tables (1 to `sis_academic_years` + 9 within `enr*\*`), 0 cross-schema FKs. **Cycle 5 (Scheduling & Calendar) is COMPLETE + APPROVED at `1650497` (REVIEW-CYCLE5-CHATGPT — final verdict). Round 1 against `e214bdc` returned REJECT pending 2 BLOCKING + 2 actionable MAJOR fixes; Round 2 against `1650497` returned APPROVED** after all 4 fixes landed. Round 2 fixes: BLOCKING 1 — `RoomBookingService.create` now wraps conflict check + INSERT in one `executeInTenantTransaction` and takes `pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('sch_room_bookings:'||roomId))` at the top of the tx; verified live with 5 parallel POSTs landing exactly 1× 201 + 4× 409. BLOCKING 2 — `RoomChangeRequestService.approve/reject` now `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` the row inside the same tx that runs the UPDATE, with `WHERE status='PENDING'` belt-and-braces on the UPDATE; verified live with 5 parallel approves landing exactly 1× 200 + 4× 400. MAJOR 3 — `DayOverrideService.create` now wraps pre-check + INSERT in one tx and catches SQLSTATE 23505 / Prisma P2002 to rethrow the friendly `ConflictException` even on a race; verified live with 5 parallel POSTs landing exactly 1× 201 + 4× 409. MAJOR 4 — `sch_timetable_slots.teacher_id` migration COMMENT rewritten from "Soft FK" to "DB-enforced FK to hr_employees(id) — both sides are tenant-scoped per the Cycle 4 Step 0 staff identity convention" to match the `REFERENCES hr_employees(id)` declaration in the schema; applied to live `tenant_demo` via `COMMENT ON COLUMN`. The two accepted MAJOR deviations (consumer envelope tenant validation; `sch.coverage.needed` best-effort emit) stay on the Phase 2 punch list. CI hardening alongside: workflow gained `permissions: contents: read` per CodeQL's least-privilege guidance. **Step 1 lands `packages/database/prisma/tenant/migrations/015_sch_bell_schedules_and_rooms.sql`** with 3 base tables: `sch_bell_schedules` (5-value `schedule_type` CHECK STANDARD/EARLY_DISMISSAL/ASSEMBLY/EXAM/CUSTOM, `is_default` flag with a partial UNIQUE INDEX on `(school_id) WHERE is_default = true` so each school has at most one default schedule, UNIQUE(school_id, name)); `sch_periods` (FK to `sch_bell_schedules ON DELETE CASCADE`, nullable `day_of_week SMALLINT` 0..6 (NULL = rotation-driven for the future Cycle 5b `sch_rotation_cycles` linkage), `start_time TIME < end_time TIME` CHECK, 5-value `period_type` CHECK LESSON/BREAK/LUNCH/REGISTRATION/ASSEMBLY, UNIQUE(bell_schedule_id, COALESCE(day_of_week, -1), start_time)); `sch_rooms` (7-value `room_type` CHECK CLASSROOM/LAB/GYM/HALL/LIBRARY/OFFICE/OUTDOOR, optional `capacity INT >= 0` CHECK, `has_projector` + `has_av` boolean amenity flags, optional DISPLAY-ONLY soft `fac_space_id` ref to a future `fac_spaces` table (M52 Facilities — unenforced, nullable, forward-compat), UNIQUE(school_id, name)). 1 intra-tenant DB-enforced FK (sch_periods → sch_bell_schedules CASCADE), 0 cross-schema FKs. Tenant logical base table count after Step 1: 77 (was 74). Live verification on `tenant_demo`: 11 negative-path assertions all fired the expected ERROR (schedule_type / period_type / room_type CHECK violations, period times-reversed, day_of_week=9, capacity=-1, both UNIQUE(school_id, name) constraints, the partial-default UNIQUE, the period (schedule, day, start_time) UNIQUE, FK rejection on a bogus bell_schedule_id), happy-path multi-insert across all 3 tables succeeds (1 schedule + 3 periods + 2 rooms), ON DELETE CASCADE on `sch_bell_schedules` drops all 3 child periods cleanly. **Splitter `;`-in-string trap struck again** — the first re-provision failed because the `sch_periods.day_of_week` COMMENT contained a literal `;` mid-sentence; the splitter cuts on every `;` regardless of quoting context, so the COMMENT was rewritten with em-dashes and the migration applied cleanly on the second pass.

**Step 2 lands `016_sch_timetable_and_bookings.sql`** with 3 base tables on top of `CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS btree_gist` (the extension lives at database scope so it's idempotent across re-provisions and across multiple tenants on the same database): `sch_timetable_slots` (DB-enforced FKs to `sis_classes`, `sch_periods`, `sch_rooms`, and nullable `hr_employees` for TBD-teacher slots; `effective_from`/`effective_to` date range with NULL-end-date meaning open-ended; UNIQUE(class_id, period_id, effective_from); two `EXCLUDE USING gist` constraints — `sch_timetable_slots_teacher_no_overlap` and `sch_timetable_slots_room_no_overlap` — both shaped `(actor WITH =, period_id WITH =, daterange(effective_from, COALESCE(effective_to, 'infinity'::date), '[)') WITH &&)`). The half-open `[)` daterange means a slot ending on a date and another slot starting on the same date do **not** overlap — the natural rotation pattern. Open-ended slots coalesce NULL → infinity so they block every future date. `teacher_id` is nullable and the `=` operator class treats NULL-vs-NULL as not-equal, so two TBD-teacher slots on the same period and overlapping range do **not** conflict (the room EXCLUSION still catches the room collision). Constraint violations raise SQLSTATE 23P01 — Step 5's `TimetableService` will translate that to `409 Conflict`. `sch_room_bookings` (CONFIRMED/CANCELLED status with multi-column lifecycle CHECK that CANCELLED ⇒ cancelled_at NOT NULL, window CHECK end_at > start_at, partial INDEX on (room_id, start_at, end_at) WHERE CONFIRMED for the conflict-check hot path). `sch_room_change_requests` (PENDING/APPROVED/REJECTED/AUTO_APPROVED with multi-column reviewed_chk that PENDING ⇔ reviewed_at NULL; nullable `requested_room_id` for the "any available" case; AUTO_APPROVED reserved for a future auto-approval sweep). 8 intra-tenant FKs (4 on sch_timetable_slots, 1 on sch_room_bookings, 3 on sch_room_change_requests), 0 cross-schema FKs. Tenant logical base table count after Step 2: 80 (was 77). Live verification on `tenant_demo` (with seeded classes + employees from prior cycles): both EXCLUSION constraints reject overlapping slots cleanly with SQLSTATE 23P01; non-overlapping reuse on `effective_from = old.effective_to + 1d` is accepted; NULL-vs-NULL teacher does not conflict; dates_chk + UNIQUE(class_id, period_id, effective_from) + all 4 FKs on slots (period/room/class/teacher) reject orphan refs (the FKs on room_id and class_id were verified separately on a clean state since the open-ended Rivera slot's teacher EXCLUSION preempted the original ordering); bookings + change_requests status / window / multi-column lifecycle CHECKs all fire on bogus inputs.

**Step 3 lands `017_sch_calendar_and_coverage.sql`** with 4 base tables: `sch_calendar_events` (8-value `event_type` CHECK HOLIDAY/PROFESSIONAL_DEVELOPMENT/EARLY_DISMISSAL/ASSEMBLY/EXAM_PERIOD/PARENT_EVENT/FIELD_TRIP/CUSTOM, optional `bell_schedule_id` FK override that pins the date range to a non-default schedule, `affects_attendance` flag that flips off Cycle 1's attendance pre-population, `is_published` draft flag, dates_chk that `end_date >= start_date`, 2-branch time_consistency_chk that keeps `all_day` in lockstep with `start_time`/`end_time` — `all_day=true` requires both NULL, `all_day=false` requires both NOT NULL with `start_time < end_time`); `sch_calendar_day_overrides` (UNIQUE(school_id, override_date), nullable `bell_schedule_id` ref, `is_school_day` flag for snow-day closures — CalendarService resolution order is override first, then matching event, then default schedule); `sch_coverage_requests` (4-value `status` CHECK OPEN/ASSIGNED/COVERED/CANCELLED, UNIQUE(timetable_slot_id, coverage_date) so the future CoverageConsumer's redelivery cannot create duplicate rows, soft `leave_request_id` ref to `hr_leave_requests` so the seed can plant rows out of order, 3-branch `assignment_chk` that OPEN ⇔ assigned_substitute_id NULL AND assigned_at NULL, ASSIGNED/COVERED ⇒ both NOT NULL, CANCELLED unconstrained because a request can be cancelled either before or after assignment and both audit shapes are legitimate); `sch_substitution_timetable` (UNIQUE(original_slot_id, effective_date), DB-enforced FKs to `sch_timetable_slots`, `hr_employees`, `sch_rooms`, and `sch_coverage_requests` with ON DELETE CASCADE — a substitution row without its parent coverage request is meaningless). 9 intra-tenant FKs (3 on `sch_calendar_events` + day_overrides bell_schedule refs + 3 on `sch_coverage_requests` slot/teacher/sub + 4 on `sch_substitution_timetable` slot/sub/room/request), 0 cross-schema FKs. Total scheduling tables: 10. Tenant logical base table count after Step 3: 84 (was 80). Live verification on `tenant_demo` (25 assertions in one BEGIN…ROLLBACK transaction with savepoints, bootstrapping a temp bell schedule + period + room + slot tied to Rivera + an existing seeded class): every CHECK fires (event_type / dates / 5 time_consistency paths / coverage status / 3 assignment_chk paths), every UNIQUE rejects duplicates (override per-date / coverage per-(slot,date) / substitution per-(slot,date)), every FK rejects orphans (calendar→bell_schedule, override→bell_schedule, coverage→slot, coverage→teacher, substitution→request, substitution→slot), happy-path multi-insert across all 4 tables succeeds, OPEN→ASSIGNED transition with `assigned_substitute_id` + `assigned_at` accepted, ON DELETE CASCADE on `sch_coverage_requests` drops the matching `sch_substitution_timetable` row cleanly. Tests 14/15/16 attribute their rejection to `assignment_chk` rather than `status_chk` because the assignment_chk's OR-clause is the load-bearing rule for the whole lifecycle — when no branch matches, it's the constraint that fires; same row-rejection outcome and same observation Step 2 documented for `sch_room_change_requests`. **Splitter `;`-in-string trap not tripped this time** — spot-checked all CHECK predicates, default expressions, and COMMENT strings; the only `--` mid-sentence in the block-comment header sits safely inside `/* */`.

**Step 4 lands `packages/database/src/seed-scheduling.ts`** (idempotent, gated on `sch_bell_schedules` row count) — seeds the demo tenant with 2 bell schedules ("Standard Day" default with 11 periods (8 LESSON / 1 LUNCH / 2 BREAK, 08:00–15:50, all `day_of_week=NULL` so each period applies to every weekday) + "Early Dismissal" with 8 periods (6 LESSON / 1 LUNCH / 1 BREAK, 08:00–12:30)); 10 rooms (Room 101–106 CLASSROOM cap 30 with projector / Science Lab cap 25 with projector+AV / Gymnasium cap 100 with AV / Library cap 50 with projector / Main Hall cap 200 with projector+AV — each with `building` and `floor` populated); 6 timetable slots mapping Rivera's Cycle 1 classes onto Standard Day Periods 1–6 (Algebra→101, English→102, Biology→103, World History→104, Geometry→105, Chemistry→106) with `effective_from='2025-08-15'` and open-ended `effective_to`; 5 calendar events (Spring Break HOLIDAY 04-14→04-18 affects_attendance=true / PD Day PROFESSIONAL_DEVELOPMENT 03-15 affects_attendance=true / Parent-Teacher Conference Evening PARENT_EVENT 05-01 18:00–20:00 non-all-day / End of Year Assembly ASSEMBLY 06-06 / Senior Prom CUSTOM 05-23 draft — 4 published, 1 draft, all authored by Mitchell); 1 day override (2026-02-07 snow day, is_school_day=false, no bell_schedule_id); 1 ASSIGNED coverage request covering Rivera's Period 1 Algebra on 2026-03-09 (Day 1 of Rivera's Cycle 4 APPROVED sick leave) with VP Park as `assigned_substitute_id` and `leave_request_id` joined to the Cycle 4 hr_leave_requests row; 1 substitution_timetable row materialising Park's assignment in Room 101 on 2026-03-09; 1 room booking (Main Hall 2026-05-01 18:00–20:00 status=CONFIRMED) by Mitchell, lining up cleanly with the matching calendar event. **`seed-iam.ts` updated:** Teacher gains SCH-004:read + SCH-005:read+write (31 → 34); Staff gains SCH-001:read + SCH-004:read + SCH-005:read+write (10 → 14). After `tsx src/build-cache.ts`: cache holds 7 account-scope pairs — admin/principal 444, teacher 34, vp/counsellor 14, student 15, parent 11. **Bug caught and fixed mid-run:** the calendar events INSERT initially passed nullable `start_time`/`end_time` as TEXT and the coverage requests INSERT passed `leave_request_id` as TEXT — both columns are typed (TIME and UUID), so Postgres refused to coerce; explicit `$N::time` and `$5::uuid` parameter casts on the affected calls resolved both. Idempotent re-run gates cleanly on the `sch_bell_schedules` count. Tenant base table count unchanged at 84 (data only — no DDL).

**Step 5 lands the first scheduling request-path module at `apps/api/src/scheduling/`** with 5 services + 5 controllers + 22 endpoints. `BellScheduleService` (6 endpoints: `GET /bell-schedules`, `GET /bell-schedules/:id`, `POST /bell-schedules` admin, `PATCH /bell-schedules/:id` admin, `POST /bell-schedules/:id/periods` admin (full upsert — DELETE all + INSERT each, atomic in one transaction so a UNIQUE / CHECK violation aborts the whole replacement instead of landing half-applied), `POST /bell-schedules/:id/set-default` admin (atomically clears any other default for the same school in the same tx so the partial UNIQUE INDEX never rejects the flip)). `TimetableService` (7 endpoints: `GET /timetable` with `classId` / `teacherId` / `roomId` / `onDate` filters, `GET /timetable/teacher/:employeeId`, `GET /timetable/class/:classId`, `GET /timetable/room/:roomId`, `POST /timetable/slots` admin, `PATCH /timetable/slots/:id` admin, `DELETE /timetable/slots/:id` admin) — every read joins through `sis_classes` + `sis_courses` for `classSectionCode` + `courseName`, `sch_periods` for `periodName` + `dayOfWeek` + `startTime` + `endTime`, `sch_rooms` for `roomName`, and LEFT JOINs `hr_employees` + `platform.iam_person` for `teacherName`. **The translateConflict helper is the load-bearing trick**: `INSERT` / `UPDATE` is wrapped in a try/catch; SQLSTATE 23P01 paired with the constraint name (`sch_timetable_slots_teacher_no_overlap` vs `sch_timetable_slots_room_no_overlap`) is decoded into a friendly `409 Conflict` with the conflicting actor's name (e.g. "Teacher James Rivera is already scheduled for Period 1 during the requested date range" or "Room Room 101 is already scheduled for Period 1 during the requested date range" — name lookups via separate `sch_rooms` / `sch_periods` / `hr_employees` reads on the failure path); 23503 (FK violation) becomes 400; 23505 on `sch_timetable_slots_class_period_from_uq` becomes a different 409. Slot writes emit `sch.timetable.updated` via `KafkaProducerService.emit({sourceModule:'scheduling'})`. `RoomService` (4 endpoints: `GET /rooms` with optional `availabilityDate` + `availabilityPeriodId` annotation that returns `available: boolean | null` per row by intersecting against active timetable slots that match the period for the supplied date, `GET /rooms/:id`, `POST /rooms` admin, `PATCH /rooms/:id` admin). `RoomBookingService` (4 endpoints: `GET /room-bookings` with `roomId` / `status` / `fromDate` / `toDate` filters, `GET /room-bookings/:id`, `POST /room-bookings` write — `assertNoConflicts` checks both existing CONFIRMED bookings whose time range overlaps **and** active timetable slots whose period clock-time on the booking's ISO weekday overlaps the booking window (the schema does NOT enforce booking-vs-slot conflict — it's an app-layer gate; per Step 2's out-of-scope decision), `PATCH /room-bookings/:id/cancel` write — owner or admin only). `RoomChangeRequestService` (5 endpoints: `GET /room-change-requests` write — non-admins see only their own rows via row-scope filter on `requested_by = actor.employeeId`, admins see all; `GET /room-change-requests/:id`, `POST /room-change-requests` write — refuses if the slot's `teacher_id` is set and not the actor (admins bypass), reads `current_room_id` from the slot rather than trusting the client; `PATCH /room-change-requests/:id/approve` admin (requires `approvedRoomId` if the original request had `requestedRoomId=null`); `PATCH /room-change-requests/:id/reject` admin). All 5 services use `tenant.schoolId` from `getCurrentTenant()` for the school-scoped fields rather than reading from `school_config` (more direct, since the tenant context is always populated). The `actor.employeeId` populated by `ActorContextService.resolveActor(...)` is the row-scope key for change requests + bookings + room change request submitters; admins bypass the row check. `SchedulingModule` imports `TenantModule` + `IamModule` + `KafkaModule` and lives at index 5 in `AppModule.imports` between `HrModule` and `NotificationsModule`. Build clean (`pnpm --filter @campusos/api build` → `nest build` succeeds). All 22 routes mapped on boot. **Live verification on `tenant_demo` (10 scenarios):** principal `GET /bell-schedules` returns 2 schedules with periods sorted by sort_order; principal `GET /timetable` returns 6 slots with full join shape; principal `GET /timetable/teacher/{Rivera}` returns Rivera's 6 Periods 1–6 in Rooms 101–106; student `GET /bell-schedules` blocked 403 (no `sch-001:read`); parent same 403; teacher `POST /timetable/slots` blocked 403 (no `sch-001:admin`); admin `POST /timetable/slots` against Rivera + Period 1 + a different room (with effectiveFrom 2025-08-15) returns **`409 Conflict — "Teacher James Rivera is already scheduled for Period 1 during the requested date range"`** (teacher EXCLUSION); admin `POST /timetable/slots` with `teacher_id=null` against Room 101 + Period 1 returns **`409 Conflict — "Room Room 101 is already scheduled for Period 1 during the requested date range"`** (room EXCLUSION); admin `POST /timetable/slots` for Period 7 (free LESSON) + Library + null teacher succeeds 201; teacher `POST /room-bookings` for Library 2030-09-15 09:00–10:00 returns 201; second booking for the same window returns `409 — "Room is already booked for an overlapping window (booking …)"`; student `POST /room-bookings` blocked 403 (no `sch-005:write`); teacher cancels own booking → `status=CANCELLED`; teacher submits room change request, teacher tries to approve own → 403, admin approves → `status=APPROVED` with `reviewedAt` populated, second admin approve attempt → 400 ("Request is in status APPROVED; only PENDING requests can be approved"); `set-default` round trip flips Early Dismissal → default, then back to Standard Day → default cleanly via the partial-UNIQUE-aware atomic helper; `GET /rooms?availabilityDate=2026-05-15&availabilityPeriodId={P1}` returns 10 rooms with Room 101 marked `available=false` (Rivera's Algebra slot covers it) and the other 9 marked `available=true`. **All smoke residue cleaned up** (`DELETE FROM sch_room_change_requests` + `sch_room_bookings` for the smoke rows) so `tenant_demo` is back to seed state for downstream Steps 6–10.

**Step 6 lands the calendar / coverage NestJS side at `apps/api/src/scheduling/`** — 4 services + 3 controllers + 16 endpoints + 1 Kafka consumer (`CoverageConsumer`) + 2 Kafka emits (`sch.coverage.needed`, `sch.coverage.assigned`). `CalendarService` (5 endpoints under `sch-003:read` / `sch-003:write` admin gates: `GET /calendar` with `fromDate` / `toDate` / `eventType` / `includeDrafts` filters — non-admins only ever see published; admins with `includeDrafts=true` see drafts too. `GET /calendar/:id` (drafts hidden from non-admins by 404). `POST /calendar` admin — schedule-aware time-shape assertion (`allDay=true` clears times; `allDay=false` requires `startTime < endTime`). `PATCH /calendar/:id` admin — when `allDay` toggles to `true` the UPDATE clears `start_time` + `end_time` in the same statement so the schema's time-consistency CHECK never fires mid-flight. `DELETE /calendar/:id` admin. `GET /calendar/day/:date` — the resolution endpoint that the calendar UI hits for a single day; reads override → published events with `bell_schedule_id` → school's `is_default=true` schedule → none, returns `{date, resolvedFrom: 'OVERRIDE'|'EVENT'|'DEFAULT'|'NONE', isSchoolDay, bellScheduleId, bellScheduleName, overrideId, overrideReason, eventIds[]}`). `DayOverrideService` (3 endpoints: `GET /calendar/overrides`, `POST /calendar/overrides` `sch-003:admin`, `DELETE /calendar/overrides/:date` `sch-003:admin`; UNIQUE(school_id, override_date) handled at the service layer with a 409 instead of letting the schema raise — better UX). `CoverageService` (4 endpoints under `sch-004:read` / `sch-004:write`: `GET /coverage` daily board with `fromDate` / `toDate` / `status` filters — non-admin staff see only rows where they are the absent or assigned employee; `GET /coverage/:id`; `PATCH /coverage/:id/assign` admin — locks the row with `FOR UPDATE`, flips OPEN→ASSIGNED, populates `assigned_substitute_id` + `assigned_at`, INSERTs the matching `sch_substitution_timetable` row in the same transaction, then emits `sch.coverage.assigned` with the full join shape outside the tx so the substitute receives an IN_APP notification through the Cycle 3 pipeline; `PATCH /coverage/:id/cancel` admin — drops any matching substitution row in the same tx so the substitute's day-view stops showing the cover, then flips status=CANCELLED). `SubstitutionService` (2 read-only endpoints: `GET /substitutions?fromDate=&toDate=` and `GET /substitutions/teacher/:employeeId?fromDate=&toDate=`; substitution rows are written exclusively by `CoverageService.assign` — there's no direct INSERT path). **`CoverageConsumer` (the long-awaited piece — Cycle 4's `hr.leave.coverage_needed` finally has a consumer)**: subscribes to `dev.hr.leave.coverage_needed` under group `coverage-consumer` using the same `unwrapEnvelope` + `processWithIdempotency` pattern as Cycle 3's notification consumers. Per inbound event, enumerates every weekday (Mon–Fri) date in `[startDate, endDate]`, joins through `sch_timetable_slots → sch_periods` for each `affectedClasses[].classId`, filters slots whose date range covers each date AND whose period `day_of_week` is NULL or matches the date's ISO weekday, then INSERTs one `sch_coverage_requests` row per matching `(slot, date)` pair with status=OPEN. `UNIQUE(timetable_slot_id, coverage_date)` is the schema-side belt-and-braces dedup — Kafka redelivery raises 23505 and the consumer swallows it. The deterministic event_id from REVIEW-CYCLE4 MAJOR 3 is the primary dedup gate (`processWithIdempotency` claims after success, so transient DB failures are retried). When new rows land, emits `sch.coverage.needed` with `{leaveRequestId, employeeId, startDate, endDate, createdCoverageIds[]}` so the Step 8 admin UI can react. **Build clean** (`pnpm --filter @campusos/api build`). All 16 new routes mapped on boot. The consumer subscribes successfully on boot (`Subscribed: groupId=coverage-consumer topics=dev.hr.leave.coverage_needed`). **Live verification on `tenant_demo` (10 scenarios):** parent `GET /calendar` returns 4 published events; admin with `includeDrafts=true` sees all 5 (Senior Prom DRAFT included); student passing `includeDrafts=true` still gets only 4 (gate-tier permission gates plus service-tier double-check); `GET /calendar/day/2026-02-07` resolves to `{resolvedFrom: 'OVERRIDE', isSchoolDay: false, overrideReason: 'Winter storm closure'}`; `GET /calendar/day/2026-04-15` (within Spring Break) resolves to `{resolvedFrom: 'DEFAULT', isSchoolDay: true, bellScheduleName: 'Standard Day', eventIds: [<spring break>]}` because the seeded Spring Break event has no `bell_schedule_id` override; `GET /calendar/day/2026-09-15` (regular weekday) resolves to `{resolvedFrom: 'DEFAULT', bellScheduleName: 'Standard Day', events: 0}`; `GET /coverage` returns the seeded ASSIGNED row Park-covers-Rivera-2026-03-09; `GET /substitutions` returns the matching substitution row. Permission denials all return 403 (parent calendar admin POST, student day-override POST, teacher coverage assign). Calendar event create-as-draft → parent doesn't see it → admin patches `isPublished=true` → parent sees it → cleanup delete returns 200. Day override create-resolve-delete round trip clean. **End-to-end CoverageConsumer flow verified live**: Rivera submits a 1-day sick leave for 2026-09-23 (Wednesday) → admin approves → within 1 second the consumer chain (`hr.leave.approved` → `LeaveNotificationConsumer.emitCoverageNeeded` → `hr.leave.coverage_needed` → `CoverageConsumer.handle`) creates 6 OPEN coverage rows (one per Rivera class — Algebra/English/Biology/World History/Geometry/Chemistry — Periods 1–6), one per class for that single date; admin assigns Park to Period 1 Algebra → status=ASSIGNED + `assignedSubstituteName=Linda Park` + `assignedAt` populated → matching `sch_substitution_timetable` row materialises in Room 101; admin cancels the assigned row → status=CANCELLED + the substitution row is dropped (the substitute's day-view stops showing the cover); the other 5 OPEN rows untouched. **All smoke residue cleaned up** (DELETE FROM `sch_coverage_requests` + `hr_leave_requests` for the smoke 2026-09-23 + restored Rivera's Sick balance) so `tenant_demo` is back to seed state.

**Step 10 lands `docs/cycle5-cat-script.md`** — the reproducible end-to-end vertical slice CAT, verified live against `tenant_demo` on 2026-04-28. The script wraps a 4-check schema preamble (84 tenant base tables / 10 sch\_ scheduling tables / 0 cross-schema FKs / 2 btree_gist EXCLUSIONs, plus row-count verification of the 9 seed tables) around the plan's 10 scenarios: S1 admin sees Rivera's 6 timetable slots; S2 teacher sees own week via `/timetable/teacher/:employeeId`; S3 parent David Chen sees Maya's 4 enrolled-class slots via the new Step 9 `/timetable/student/:studentId` row-scoped endpoint; S4 admin attempt to schedule Rivera twice for Period 1 → `409 Conflict — "Teacher James Rivera is already scheduled for Period 1 during the requested date range"` (EXCLUSION 23P01 → translateConflict); S5 seeded ASSIGNED coverage row + matching substitution row for Park-covers-Rivera-2026-03-09 verified; **S6 live leave→coverage**: Rivera submits 1-day Sick leave for 2026-09-30 (Wed) → admin approves → within 3 seconds the consumer chain (`hr.leave.approved` → `LeaveNotificationConsumer.emitCoverageNeeded` → `hr.leave.coverage_needed` → `CoverageConsumer.handle`) creates 6 OPEN coverage rows (one per Rivera Period 1–6); **S7 assign substitute**: admin assigns Park to Period 1 Algebra → `status=ASSIGNED` + matching `sch_substitution_timetable` row materialises in Room 101 → ADR-057 envelope on the wire on `dev.sch.coverage.assigned` with `source_module=scheduling` and full join shape inline (coverageRequestId, timetableSlotId, coverageDate, substituteId, substituteName, absentTeacherId, absentTeacherName, classSectionCode, courseName, periodName, roomId, roomName, assignedAt); S8 calendar list returns the 5 seeded events (4 published + 1 draft Senior Prom) + 1 day-override row, `/calendar/day/2026-02-07` resolves to `{resolvedFrom: 'OVERRIDE', isSchoolDay: false, overrideReason: 'Winter storm closure'}`; S9 teacher books Library 2026-10-15 09:00–10:00 → 201, second overlapping booking → `409 — "Room is already booked for an overlapping window (booking …)"`, booking Room 101 during Period 1 → `409 — "Room is in use by the timetable during the requested window: 1 / Period 1"` (the app-layer assertNoConflicts gate); S10 6 permission denials (parent /timetable 403, student POST /room-bookings 403, teacher PATCH /coverage/:id/assign 403 admin-only at service layer, parent POST /calendar 403, parent /timetable/student/{unrelated} 404 row-scope, student POST /bell-schedules 403). **All 10 scenarios pass.** Cleanup section drops the 1 leave + 6 coverage rows + 1 substitution + 1 booking and resets Rivera's Sick balance back to seed defaults (`used=2.0, pending=0.0`) so the next run starts clean. **Known gap surfaced by the CAT (Phase 2 punch list, not Cycle 5 scope):** `sch.coverage.assigned` is emitted to Kafka with the full join shape inline, but no consumer yet wires the substitute IN_APP notification through the Cycle 3 pipeline — the substitute's bell badge does NOT increment when assigned. The Step 6 handoff already documented this as a future patch; the CAT confirms the emit lands cleanly so the wiring is a small downstream consumer addition rather than a re-architecture. **Cycle 5 ships clean to the post-cycle architecture review.**

**Step 9 lands the non-admin schedule views at `apps/web/src/app/(app)/my-schedule/` + `apps/web/src/app/(app)/children/[id]/schedule/`** — every persona can now see the schedule that affects them. One small backend extension was needed: parents and students don't hold `sch-001:read` so they can't hit `/timetable` directly. New endpoint `GET /timetable/student/:studentId` lands on `TimetableService` gated on `stu-001:read` (held by every relevant persona) with row-scope enforced by `StudentService.assertCanViewStudent` (admin / parent of / assigned-class teacher / the student themself — the same row-scope contract Cycle 1 already uses for `/students/:id/...` reads). Query joins `sis_enrollments → sch_timetable_slots` for the student's ACTIVE enrollments and reuses the existing `SELECT_SLOT_BASE` so the response shape matches the other timetable endpoints. `SchedulingModule` now imports `SisModule` to get `StudentService`. Total scheduling endpoints: 39. Web side: a new `useTimetableForStudent(studentId)` hook + two new components and two new routes. **`apps/web/src/components/scheduling/TimetableWeekView.tsx`** extracts the periods × Mon-Fri grid rendering from `/schedule/timetable` into a reusable component (reads the school's default bell schedule via `useBellSchedules`); accepts `slots` + state passthrough + `showTeacher` / `showRoom` toggles + an optional `substitutions` array that builds a `Map<${slotId}::${YYYY-MM-DD}, SubstitutionDto>` so cells whose date matches a substitution render with an amber border + struck-through absent teacher + substitute's name to the right. The grid header annotates each weekday column with `MM-DD` so the user can see which week is rendered. **`apps/web/src/components/scheduling/RoomAvailabilityChecker.tsx`** is the Step 9 plan's "quick check availability widget" mounted on `/schedule/room-bookings`: pick a date + period from the school's default bell schedule → calls `useRooms({availabilityDate, availabilityPeriodId})` → renders a tiny pill grid with green/amber dots per active room (powered by Step 5's `available: boolean | null` annotation, query enabled-when-period-picked). **`/my-schedule`** is a single shared route. STAFF branch: `useMyEmployee → useTimetableForTeacher(employeeId)` for the grid + `useSubstitutionsForTeacher(employeeId, {fromDate=today, toDate=+14d})` so the next-two-weeks of upcoming substitution coverage shows up as a list below the grid (and the matching cells in the grid get the amber-highlight treatment). STUDENT branch: `useMyStudent → useTimetableForStudent(studentId)`; teacher names render in each cell. Other personas get an empty state pointing parents at `/children`. **`/children/[id]/schedule`** is the parent route — `useStudent` for the header + `useTimetableForStudent(studentId)` for the grid; the new endpoint's row-scope rejects parents not linked via `sis_student_guardians`, surfaced as an empty state. **Navigation entry points wired:** `/classes` Teacher and Student persona views gain a `My Schedule` button routing to `/my-schedule`; `/children` ChildCard gains a `Schedule` button alongside Attendance / Grades / Report-absence. **Sidebar tile active-state fix (Steps 7+8 carry-over):** `AppDef` gains an optional `routePrefix` field; the Schedule tile sets `routePrefix='/schedule'` so navigating into `/schedule/coverage`, `/schedule/bell-schedules`, `/schedule/rooms`, `/schedule/coverage/history`, etc. all keep the Schedule tile highlighted. Sidebar match resolves to `app.routePrefix ?? app.href`. **Build clean** (API + web): `/my-schedule` 3.73 kB, `/children/[id]/schedule` 3.87 kB First Load JS. **Live verification on `tenant_demo` on 2026-04-28** (5 scenarios): parent (David Chen) `GET /timetable/student/{Maya}` 200 → 4 slots (Maya is enrolled in Periods 1–4 of Rivera's 6 classes per the seed) with full join shape; student (Maya) `GET /timetable/student/{self}` 200; teacher (Rivera) `GET /timetable/student/{Maya}` 200 (Rivera is the assigned teacher of Maya's enrolled classes); teacher `GET /timetable/teacher/{Rivera}` 200 → 6 slots; parent `GET /timetable/student/{unrelated student}` 404 (row-scope rejects). **Stale-API-instance trap caught during smoke:** `pnpm --filter @campusos/api start` doesn't auto-rebuild — first smoke run hit a stale `node dist/main` instance from before the rebuild and returned 404s on the new endpoint. Killing the PID + rebooting from the freshly built `dist/` resolved it. Worth flagging for downstream cycles. **Out of scope this step (deferred):** week-nav on `/my-schedule` (currently anchored on today's week); a date-range picker for the substitution list (currently fixed at next 14 days). (Step 10 of Cycle 5 — the end-to-end CAT — remains to ship; see `docs/campusos-cycle5-implementation-plan.html` and `HANDOFF-CYCLE5.md` for the plan.)

**Step 8 lands the calendar / coverage web surface at `apps/web/src/app/(app)/calendar/` + `apps/web/src/app/(app)/schedule/coverage/`** — 3 routes + 15 more React Query hooks (30 total scheduling hooks) + a second `Calendar` launchpad tile gated on `sch-003:read` (every persona since Step 4) routing to `/calendar`. Reuses the `CalendarIcon` from Step 7. No new backend or schema; Step 8 sits on the 16 endpoints from Step 6 (CalendarService + DayOverrideService + CoverageService + SubstitutionService) and triggers `sch.coverage.assigned` indirectly via the Assign Modal. **`/calendar`** is a Mon-leading 7×6 month-view grid (±6 weeks of events fetched so leading/trailing days from adjacent months render their badges); each cell shows the day number (today gets a campus-700 dot), an inline gray "Closed" pill when a `sch_calendar_day_overrides` row marks `isSchoolDay=false`, and one chip per overlapping calendar event colour-coded by `eventType` via `calendarEventChipClasses` (HOLIDAY rose / PD violet / EARLY_DISMISSAL amber / ASSEMBLY sky / EXAM_PERIOD orange / PARENT_EVENT emerald / FIELD_TRIP teal / CUSTOM gray). Drafts render with a dashed border + 70% opacity (admins only when `Show drafts` is checked). Click any chip → Modal with full event detail + author + bell-schedule override + Affects-attendance pill; admins see Publish-now (PATCH `isPublished:true`) + Delete. Admins also get a `New event` button that opens a multi-field Modal (title, eventType picker, start/end dates, all-day toggle that reveals start/end time inputs only when off, optional description, Affects-attendance toggle) with both `Save draft` (POST `isPublished:false`) and `Publish now` (POST `isPublished:true`) submit paths. **`/schedule/coverage`** is the day-of board: `<input type="date">` defaulted to today + a Today button + a small counter ("3 open · 1 assigned · 0 cancelled"); body groups rows into Open (red-tinted) / Assigned (amber) / Cancelled (gray) sections. Per row shows period + section + course + absent teacher + room + assigned sub + status pill; admins see Assign / Reassign / Cancel buttons. Assign Modal pre-loads `useEmployees` + `useRooms`, filters out the absent teacher and inactive employees from the substitute dropdown, accepts an optional room override (default = original slot's room) + notes, and PATCHes `/coverage/:id/assign` — the Step 6 backend writes the OPEN→ASSIGNED transition + matching `sch_substitution_timetable` row in one tx and emits `sch.coverage.assigned` so the substitute receives an IN_APP notification through the Cycle 3 pipeline (the hook also invalidates `['notifications']` so the substitute's bell badge updates on the next 30s poll). Cancel Modal accepts optional notes and PATCHes `/coverage/:id/cancel` (backend drops the substitution row in the same tx). **`/schedule/coverage/history`** is the reporting view: 5-control filter bar (from / to dates default to ±30 days around today, status dropdown, absent-teacher and substitute name fields — name filters are client-side substring matches against the joined names; status is server-side); rows grouped by `coverageDate` newest-first with `assignedAt` rendered as a localised timestamp. **15 new hooks** added to `use-scheduling.ts`: `useCalendarEvents(args)` / `useCalendarEvent(id)` / `useCalendarDayResolution(date)` / `useCreateCalendarEvent` / `useUpdateCalendarEvent(id)` / `useDeleteCalendarEvent` for events; `useDayOverrides(args)` / `useCreateDayOverride` / `useDeleteDayOverride` for overrides; `useCoverageRequests(args)` (refetch on focus) / `useCoverageRequest(id)` / `useAssignCoverage(id)` (also invalidates `['notifications']`) / `useCancelCoverage(id)` for coverage; `useSubstitutions(args)` / `useSubstitutionsForTeacher(employeeId, args)` for the future Step 9 teacher schedule view. `scheduling-format.ts` extended with `CALENDAR_EVENT_TYPES` const + `calendarEventTypeLabel` + `calendarEventChipClasses` + `coverageStatusLabel` + `coverageStatusPillClasses` + `todayIso()` + `addDaysIso(date, n)`. `apps/web/src/lib/types.ts` extended with `CalendarEventDto` / `CalendarEventType` / `CreateCalendarEventPayload` / `UpdateCalendarEventPayload` / `ListCalendarEventsArgs` / `DayOverrideDto` / `CreateDayOverridePayload` / `ListDayOverridesArgs` / `DayResolutionSource` / `CalendarDayResolutionDto` / `CoverageStatus` / `CoverageRequestDto` / `AssignCoveragePayload` / `CancelCoveragePayload` / `ListCoverageArgs` / `SubstitutionDto` / `ListSubstitutionsArgs`. **Build clean** (`pnpm --filter @campusos/web build`): `/calendar` 5.94 kB, `/schedule/coverage` 4.37 kB, `/schedule/coverage/history` 1.81 kB First Load JS. **Live smoke against `tenant_demo` on 2026-04-28** (8 scenarios): principal `GET /calendar?fromDate=2026-04-01&toDate=2026-06-30` 200 → 4 published events; principal `GET /coverage?fromDate=2026-03-09&toDate=2026-03-09` 200 → 1 ASSIGNED row Park-covers-Rivera-Period-1-Algebra; principal `GET /calendar/overrides` 200 → 1 Winter storm closure; principal `GET /substitutions?fromDate=2026-03-01&toDate=2026-03-31` 200 → matching substitution row; parent `GET /calendar` 200 (sch-003:read granted at Step 4); parent `GET /coverage` 403 (no sch-004:read); teacher `GET /coverage` 200 (sch-004:read granted at Step 4); parent `POST /calendar` 403 (sch-003:admin required). The end-to-end Assign / Cancel path was already verified live in Step 6's smoke; Step 8 just wires the same payload shape into Modal UI. **Out of scope this step (deferred to follow-ups):** week-view fallback for `/calendar` (month-only ships); a dedicated day-override admin Modal (the hooks are wired but no UI yet — the seed's 1 row is enough to demonstrate the resolution chain); ICS / iCal export; the same Sidebar tile active-state UX gap from Step 7 applies to `/schedule/coverage` since the `Schedule` tile's href is `/schedule/timetable` and routes are siblings rather than nested. Step 9 will revisit navigation holistically. (Steps 9–10 of Cycle 5 — the teacher / parent / student schedule views, the room availability checker widget, and the CAT — remain to ship; see `docs/campusos-cycle5-implementation-plan.html` and `HANDOFF-CYCLE5.md` for the plan.)

**Step 7 lands the first scheduling web surface at `apps/web/src/app/(app)/schedule/`** — 4 routes (5 pages) + 15 React Query hooks in `apps/web/src/hooks/use-scheduling.ts` + a single new `Schedule` launchpad tile gated on `sch-001:read` (every staff persona since Step 4) routing to `/schedule/timetable`, plus a new `CalendarIcon` in `apps/web/src/components/shell/icons.tsx`. No new backend or schema; Step 7 is purely additive on the web side, sitting on the 22 endpoints from Step 5. **`/schedule/bell-schedules`** lists each bell schedule with its `Default` pill + period count; admins can `Set default` any non-default schedule (POST `/bell-schedules/:id/set-default`) or open a Modal to `New schedule` (POST `/bell-schedules` with name + scheduleType + optional `isDefault`). **`/schedule/bell-schedules/[id]`** is the period-level editor — hydrates a draft from the schedule's existing periods, renders an inline table (#, name, start, end, type, day-of-week, remove) where each row uses `<input type="time">` + `<select>` for `period_type`, tracks `dirty` so `Save` is disabled unless something changed, validates `HH:MM` shape + `start < end` per row before POSTing the full set to `/bell-schedules/:id/periods` (the backend's bulk DELETE-all + INSERT-each in one tx makes partial saves impossible). Non-admins see read-only rendering. **`/schedule/timetable`** renders the week-view grid: rows = the periods of the school's default bell schedule, columns = Mon–Fri; slots with `dayOfWeek=null` render in every weekday column (the seed pattern). Each cell renders a campus-100 chip with `classSectionCode`, `courseName`, teacher name (or "TBD"), and room. Filter chips at the top (All / By teacher / By class / By room) drive `useTimetable({…})` switching to the matching server-side filter — the schema's two EXCLUSION constraints from Step 2 mean overlaps are impossible. **`/schedule/rooms`** lists rooms as a card grid with type / capacity / building+floor / Projector+AV pills; admins can filter inactive + open a Modal to `New room`. Each card carries a `View weekly schedule →` link to `/schedule/timetable?roomId=…` (URL handoff to the timetable filter). **`/schedule/room-bookings`** is a day-grouped booking calendar — filters by `from` date / room / status (default `CONFIRMED only`), grouped by local date of `startAt`. Anyone with `sch-005:write` can `New booking` (Modal with room dropdown, purpose, `<input type="datetime-local">` start/end, converted to `Date.toISOString()` before POST); the Step 5 `assertNoConflicts` gate (existing CONFIRMED bookings + active timetable slots whose period clock-time on the booking's ISO weekday overlaps the booking window) returns a 409 the page surfaces via Toast directly. Cancel button is offered on owned bookings + admins; PATCH `/room-bookings/:id/cancel`. **Helpers** live in `apps/web/src/lib/scheduling-format.ts` (`scheduleTypeLabel`, `periodTypeLabel`, `roomTypeLabel`, `dayOfWeekLabel`, `formatTime`, `bookingStatusLabel`, `changeRequestStatusLabel`, plus `ROOM_TYPES` / `PERIOD_TYPES` / `WEEKDAY_LABELS` const arrays). `apps/web/src/lib/types.ts` extended with the full Cycle 5 DTO surface (BellScheduleDto / PeriodDto / RoomDto / TimetableSlotDto / RoomBookingDto / RoomChangeRequestDto + payloads + filter args). The 15 hooks (`useBellSchedules`, `useBellSchedule`, `useCreateBellSchedule`, `useUpdateBellSchedule(id)`, `useUpsertPeriods(scheduleId)`, `useSetDefaultBellSchedule`, `useTimetable(args)`, `useTimetableForTeacher`, `useTimetableForClass`, `useTimetableForRoom`, `useCreateTimetableSlot`, `useUpdateTimetableSlot(id)`, `useDeleteTimetableSlot`, `useRooms(args)`, `useRoom`, `useCreateRoom`, `useUpdateRoom(id)`, `useRoomBookings(args)`, `useCreateRoomBooking`, `useCancelRoomBooking`, `useRoomChangeRequests(args)`, `useCreateRoomChangeRequest`, `useApproveRoomChangeRequest(id)`, `useRejectRoomChangeRequest(id)`) match the Step 5 endpoint shape verbatim, with mutation invalidations covering the affected query keys (`useUpsertPeriods` also invalidates `['scheduling', 'timetable']` because period-time changes shift grid columns). **Build clean** (`pnpm --filter @campusos/web build`): `/schedule/bell-schedules` 2.66 kB, `/schedule/bell-schedules/[id]` 2.92 kB, `/schedule/timetable` 3.05 kB, `/schedule/rooms` 2.99 kB, `/schedule/room-bookings` 3.31 kB First Load JS. **Live smoke against `tenant_demo` on 2026-04-28** (8 scenarios): principal `GET /bell-schedules` 200 → 2 seeded schedules; principal `GET /timetable` 200 → 6 Rivera slots with full join shape; principal `GET /rooms` 200 → 10 rooms; principal `GET /room-bookings` 200 → 1 seeded Main Hall booking; parent `GET /bell-schedules` 403; parent `GET /timetable` 403; teacher `GET /bell-schedules` 200 (sch-001:read granted in Step 4); teacher `GET /room-bookings` 200 (sch-005:read granted in Step 4). Mutation paths (create schedule / upsert periods / create room / create booking) are already verified end-to-end in Step 5's smoke and the page wiring uses the same payload shapes. **Out of scope this step (deferred to Step 8/9):** the admin slot CRUD modal on the timetable grid (hooks are ready — `useCreateTimetableSlot` / `useUpdateTimetableSlot` / `useDeleteTimetableSlot` — but no UI), the room change request page (hooks ready), and the Step 9 availability checker widget (the `useRooms({ availabilityDate, availabilityPeriodId })` hook is already wired). Sidebar `Schedule` tile active-state has a small UX gap — clicking into `/schedule/bell-schedules` doesn't keep the tile highlighted because the tile's href is `/schedule/timetable` and the Sidebar matches by href prefix. Punted to Step 9 where the navigation pattern can be revisited holistically. (Steps 8–10 of Cycle 5 — the calendar / coverage UI, the teacher / parent / student views, and the CAT — remain to ship; see `docs/campusos-cycle5-implementation-plan.html` and `HANDOFF-CYCLE5.md` for the plan.)

**Cycle 4 (HR & Workforce Core) is COMPLETE + APPROVED at `76ddf03` (REVIEW-CYCLE4-CHATGPT — final verdict). Round 1 against `efbcb44` returned REJECT pending 1 BLOCKING + 3 MAJOR; Round 2 against `76ddf03` returned APPROVED** after all four fixes (`bda8a16`) plus the CI-fix commit (TS2347 in seed-hr + Prettier drift, `76ddf03`) landed. Cycle 4 ships clean. **REVIEW-CYCLE4 fixes:** (1) BLOCKING — `LeaveService.{approve,reject,cancel}` now `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` the request row inside the same transaction that writes the balance + status, so concurrent admin approves serialise on the row lock and the second one re-reads `status='APPROVED'` and 400s. Verified live with 5 parallel approve curls — 1 won, 4 got 400, balance moved cleanly. (2) MAJOR — `ActorContextService.resolveEmployeeId` now resolves `employment_status IN ('ACTIVE','ON_LEAVE')`; TERMINATED + SUSPENDED stay excluded. ON_LEAVE employees keep portal access (own profile, balances, requests) — TERMINATED still 404s on `/employees/me`. (3) MAJOR — `TrainingComplianceService.getDashboard` accepts `actor.isSchoolAdmin` OR a tenant-scope-chain `hr-004:admin` check via `permissionCheckService.hasAnyPermissionInTenant`. The `apps.tsx` tile already gates on the same union, so UI + API agree. (4) MAJOR — `hr.leave.coverage_needed` republishes now use a deterministic event_id derived from `sha1(inbound_event_id + ':hr.leave.coverage_needed.v1')` formatted as a v5-shaped UUID, plus `correlationId = inbound.event_id`. A Kafka redelivery republish carries the exact same event_id, so Cycle 5's future consumer idempotency catches the dup before doing the work twice. Optional `eventId` field added to `EnvelopeOptions` + `EmitOptions` to enable this pattern for future republishers. Verified live — Python independently computed the expected hash and matched the wire envelope; v5 marker correctly set.

Step 0 retired the Cycle 2 DEVIATION 4 mapping; Steps 1–4 added 17 hr\_\* tables (74 tenant tables total); Step 5 seeded 4 employees + 5 positions + 4 certs + 4 compliance rows + 8 onboarding tasks; Steps 6–7 shipped 23 HR endpoints + 5 Kafka emits + the LeaveNotificationConsumer; Steps 8–9 shipped 7 HR web routes + 3 launchpad tiles (Staff / Leave / Compliance) + 16 hooks. **Step 10 lands `docs/cycle4-cat-script.md`** — the reproducible end-to-end vertical slice CAT, verified live against `tenant_demo` on 2026-04-28. The script wraps a 4-scenario bridge-verification block (Step 0 carry-over: 4 hr_employees rows, 0 orphans across the 4 bridged columns, 74 tenant base tables, 0 cross-schema FKs) around the 9 plan scenarios: directory list (4 employees), employee profile + certifications + balances, submit + Kafka envelope verified on the wire, admin approve + balance update from used 2 → 3 / pending 1 → 0, `hr.leave.coverage_needed` republish with all 6 of Rivera's classes inline (Algebra 1 → Chemistry — for Cycle 5 Scheduling to consume), 3 IN_APP notifications enqueued and SENT (2 admins on requested + Rivera on approved), compliance dashboard amber row for Rivera's Teaching Licence at 60 days, 6 permission denial paths covering both gate-tier (parent/student no HR codes) and admin-tier (teacher cannot approve / verify cert / read dashboard) checks. **All 16 checks pass.** No new code in the closeout commit; the seed inconsistency caught during the Step 7 smoke (Rivera PD pending=0 vs PENDING request for 1d) was fixed in Step 7's commit (`70b6cf3`), so the fresh provision running this CAT produces the correct shape from the start. **Cycle 4 ships clean to the post-cycle architecture review.**

**Step 9 closes the HR UI.** Two new launchpad tiles via the single-source `apps.tsx` catalogue: a **Leave** tile under `hr-003:read` (every staff persona) using the existing `AttendanceIcon`, and a **Compliance** tile under `sch-001:admin` OR `hr-004:admin` using the existing `CheckCircleIcon`. Four new pages: `/leave` (employee view — balance cards with `used + pending` two-tone progress bar / `available` headline; request history with status pills; per-row Cancel button on PENDING requests with a `window.confirm` guard, wired to `useCancelLeaveRequest`); `/leave/new` (request form — leave-type dropdown defaulted to the first active type, `?startDate`/`?endDate` `<input type="date">`, `daysRequested` auto-calc from the date range with half-day support and a manual override, optional reason, live balance hint for the selected type, amber warning when daysRequested exceeds available, submit via `useSubmitLeaveRequest` then `router.push('/leave')` + toast); `/leave/approvals` (admin queue gated on `sch-001:admin` — non-admins see an empty-state explaining admin-only; lists every PENDING via `useLeaveRequests({status:'PENDING'})`; per-row Approve / Reject buttons open a Modal with optional review-notes textarea; Approve modal uses primary CTA, Reject uses red CTA; Modal mutations via `useApproveLeaveRequest(req.id)` / `useRejectLeaveRequest(req.id)` then close + toast); `/compliance` (admin dashboard — 3-stat header card row Active employees / With gaps / Compliant %, three filter chips (All / Has gaps / Fully compliant), per-employee row with `compliantCount / amberCount / redCount` summary pills + a `View profile` link to `/staff/:id` + a Details toggle that expands the per-requirement rows with green/amber/red urgency pills inline). Build clean: `/leave` 5.66 kB, `/leave/new` 5.74 kB, `/leave/approvals` 6.06 kB, `/compliance` 5.65 kB First Load JS.

**Step 8 lands the first HR web surface.** A new "Staff" tile appears on the home launchpad and in the sidebar for any user with `hr-001:read` (driven by the single-source `apps/web/src/components/shell/apps.tsx::getAppsForUser`, using the existing `PeopleIcon`). Three pages: `/staff` (searchable directory list — avatar + full name + primary position title + employee number + email; admin-only `Include inactive` checkbox; client-side search wired to the API's `?search=` and `?includeInactive=` query string); `/staff/[id]` (tabbed profile — Info / Certifications / Leave / Documents; Info tab is always visible to anyone with `hr-001:read`; the other three tabs render only for the owning employee or for school admins, gated by comparing `useMyEmployee().data.id` to the route id and checking `sch-001:admin`); and `/staff/me` (client-side redirect that calls `useMyEmployee` then `router.replace('/staff/:id')`, with a clean "no employee record" empty state for non-staff personas). Certifications tab shows a 4-stat summary (Requirements / Compliant / Expiring / Non-compliant) plus a per-cert list with green/amber/red urgency pills derived from `daysUntilExpiry` + `verificationStatus`, and a per-requirement breakdown joined to the seeded compliance rows. Leave tab shows balances (own profile only — admin viewing someone else gets a hint message) and the request history (admin sees all rows for the target employee). Documents tab is a list view; uploads land in Step 9. **`apps/web/src/lib/types.ts` extended** with the full HR DTO surface (EmployeeDto, EmployeePositionDto, PositionDto, EmployeeDocumentDto, CertificationDto, LeaveTypeDto, LeaveBalanceDto, LeaveRequestDto, ComplianceRowDto, EmployeeComplianceDto, ComplianceDashboardDto + payload types). **`apps/web/src/hooks/use-hr.ts` (new)** exposes 16 hooks: `useEmployees` (refetch on focus), `useEmployee`, `useMyEmployee`, `usePositions` (5 min stale), `useEmployeeDocuments`, `useEmployeeCertifications`, `useExpiringCertifications` (60 s poll), `useEmployeeCompliance`, `useComplianceDashboard` (60 s poll), `useLeaveTypes` (5 min stale), `useMyLeaveBalances`, `useLeaveRequests`, plus 5 mutations (`useSubmitLeaveRequest`, `useApproveLeaveRequest`, `useRejectLeaveRequest`, `useCancelLeaveRequest`, `useVerifyCertification`) — every mutation invalidates the affected query keys. Each hook accepts an `enabled` boolean so persona gating doesn't fire 403 calls. Build clean: `pnpm --filter @campusos/web build` ships `/staff` 6.18 kB, `/staff/[id]` 6.49 kB, `/staff/me` 2.34 kB First Load JS.

**Step 7 lands the leave / cert / compliance API surface plus the first HR Kafka consumer.** `LeaveService` exposes 7 endpoints (`GET /leave-types`, `GET /leave/me/balances`, `GET /leave-requests`, `GET /leave-requests/:id`, `POST /leave-requests`, `PATCH /leave-requests/:id/{approve,reject,cancel}`) and emits 4 Kafka events on every transition (`hr.leave.requested`, `hr.leave.approved`, `hr.leave.rejected`, `hr.leave.cancelled`) with `sourceModule:'hr'`. Submit / approve / reject / cancel each run in `executeInTenantTransaction` — balance UPDATEs respect the non-negative CHECKs from migration 012, so a cancel that would underflow `pending` or `used` fails loudly with a 23514 instead of silently corrupting totals. Per-employee balance reads return one row per leave type (zeros for types with no balance row yet) and surface a derived `available = accrued - used - pending`. `CertificationService` exposes 4 endpoints (`GET /employees/:id/certifications`, `POST /employees/:id/certifications`, `GET /certifications/:id`, `PATCH /certifications/:id/verify`, plus admin `GET /certifications/expiring-soon`) with the same own-or-admin row-scope guard as documents and a `daysUntilExpiry` field computed from today; `verify` is admin-only and emits `hr.certification.verified`. `TrainingComplianceService` exposes 2 endpoints (`GET /employees/:id/compliance` and admin `GET /compliance/dashboard`) with a derived `urgency` tier (green / amber / red) computed from `is_compliant` + `days_until_due`. The dashboard always includes every ACTIVE employee even when they have zero compliance rows (LEFT-side roster). `LeaveNotificationConsumer` (consumer group `leave-notification-consumer`) subscribes to all 4 leave topics, notifies admins on `requested`, notifies the submitter on `approved` / `rejected`, and on `approved` republishes `hr.leave.coverage_needed` with the affected class ids resolved via `sis_class_teachers` for the Cycle 5 Scheduling consumer. **HR endpoint count is now 23** (12 from Step 6 + 11 from Step 7). Build clean, all routes mapped on boot, leave-notification-consumer subscribes successfully (after pre-creating the 6 dev `dev.hr.*` topics — the same auto-creation race that affects Cycle 3's audience-fan-out worker on a fresh broker). **Live verification on `tenant_demo` (22 scenarios):** 5 leave types listed; teacher's seeded balances show non-zero used/pending; admin queue surfaces the 1 PENDING PD request; teacher submits a 1.5-day Sick request → `pending` jumps to 1.5; admin approves → `pending` drops to 0 and `used` rises 2 → 3.5; `hr.leave.requested` notifies the 2 admin accounts (Mitchell + Platform Admin) and `hr.leave.approved` notifies Rivera, all 3 rows SENT to Redis ZADD inbox; **`hr.leave.coverage_needed` published to `dev.hr.leave.coverage_needed` with all 6 of Rivera's classes inline** (Algebra 1 / English 9 / Biology / World History / Geometry / Chemistry); teacher cancels the seeded PD request → `pending` correctly returns to 0; admin POST cert + admin PATCH verify works end-to-end; teacher denied cert verify with 403 (`hr-004:write` admin-only); parent denied with 403 INSUFFICIENT_PERMISSIONS at the gate; `/certifications/expiring-soon` returns Rivera's Teaching Licence with the dynamic 60-day expiry from Step 5's seed. **Bug caught and fixed by the smoke:** Step 5 seed had Rivera's PD balance row at `pending=0` while also seeding a PENDING PD request for 1 day — the schema's `pending_chk >= 0` correctly rejected the cancel underflow, exposing the seed inconsistency. `seed-hr.ts` updated to set Rivera PD `pending=1.0` to match the seeded request; the live demo tenant patched in-place so the smoke could complete. Personal Leave's stale `used=1, pending=1` (no matching seeded request) reset to zeros for the same reason.

**Step 6 lands the first HR API surface at `apps/api/src/hr/`** with three services and 12 endpoints. `EmployeeService` handles the staff directory list (`GET /employees`, joined to `iam_person` for first/last name + `platform_users` for email + the most recent `hr_employee_positions` row for the primary position title), single-record reads (`GET /employees/:id` and `GET /employees/me` which resolves the calling user via `actor.employeeId` and 404s if the actor is parent/student/Platform Admin), admin-only create (validates the supplied `personId` + `accountId` against the platform schema, links them to a freshly minted `hr_employees` row inside one `executeInTenantTransaction`, optionally seeds a primary `hr_employee_positions` assignment from `initialPositionId`), and admin-only patch (employee_number / employment_type / employment_status / termination_date — dynamic SET-clause builder so callers can update one field or many). `PositionService` is admin-only for writes (`POST` / `PATCH` gated on `hr-001:admin`), reads on `hr-001:read`; the list query returns each position with `activeAssignments` (count of `hr_employee_positions` rows where `effective_to IS NULL`) and the joined `sis_departments.name` for the soft `department_id` ref. `EmployeeDocumentService` enforces a row-scope guard (`assertCanAccess` — admin OR `actor.employeeId === employeeId`, otherwise 403); list returns non-archived rows joined to `hr_document_types` for the type label; create validates the document type is active + the employee exists; archive flips `is_archived=true` (soft delete) — hard delete is intentionally not exposed. The document column `uploaded_by` is set from `actor.accountId` so the audit trail records the platform user, not the employee record. **DTOs use `class-validator`** with `IsIn` arrays for the employment_type and employment_status enums (keeps the values in sync with the SQL CHECK constraints). The position list controller uses `class-transformer` `@Transform` to coerce `?includeInactive=true` from the query string into a boolean. `HrModule` imports `TenantModule` + `IamModule` and lives at index 4 in `AppModule.imports` between `ClassroomModule` and `NotificationsModule`. Build clean, all 12 routes mapped on boot. Live verification on `tenant_demo`: principal@ sees 4 employees in `/employees` (Mitchell/Rivera/Park/Hayes ordered by last name, each with employee_number + primary position title); teacher@ `/employees/me` resolves Rivera with `positions=1` and `primaryPositionTitle='Teacher'`; principal@ `/positions` returns 5 (incl. the unassigned Administrative Assistant with `activeAssignments=0`); parent@ blocked at the permission gate on `/employees` and `/employees/me` with `hr-001:read` required; student@ blocked similarly on `/positions`; counsellor@ trying to read Rivera's documents gets a clean 403 from the row-scope check (own documents OR admin only); teacher@ POST `/positions` returns 403 (`hr-001:admin` required); principal@ creates a "Smoke Position" then PATCHes `isActive=false` round-trip; principal@ POST `/employees` with bogus UUIDs returns 400 from the validators (proves admin path is reachable to admins).

**Step 5 layers data on top of the schema:** `seed-hr.ts` extended with idempotent layers (each gates on its own row count) — 5 positions (Teacher, Principal, VP, Counsellor, Administrative Assistant) + 4 position assignments mapping the 4 employees to their roles, 5 leave types (Sick / Personal / Bereavement / PD / Unpaid) + 20 per-(employee × type) balances for the current academic year (Rivera has used some sick + personal so the CAT shows non-zero used/pending), 2 sample leave requests for Rivera (1 APPROVED sick leave from March, 1 PENDING PD day for next month), 4 certifications (Rivera Texas Teaching Licence VERIFIED + First Aid + Safeguarding L1; Mitchell DBS Enhanced VERIFIED — **Rivera's licence is seeded with `expiry_date = today + 60 days` so it dynamically drives the CAT compliance dashboard amber row**), 4 training requirements (3 school-wide ANNUAL/BIENNIAL + 1 position-specific Teaching Licence Renewal), 4 pre-computed compliance rows (Rivera 2 compliant + 1 not-compliant linked to the expiring licence; Mitchell 1 not-compliant Safeguarding), 4 document types (Contract / Background Check / W-4 / Teaching Licence Copy with retention_days populated), and 1 "New Teacher Onboarding" template + 1 NOT_STARTED checklist for Rivera + 8 tasks (DOCUMENT × 4, TRAINING × 2, SYSTEM_ACCESS × 1, ORIENTATION × 1) covering onboarding from contract through classroom orientation. **`seed-iam.ts` updated:** Teacher role gains HR-001:read + HR-003:read+write + HR-004:read (4 perms, 27 → 31). Staff role same 4 grants (6 → 10). The role-assignment block was rewritten from "if any assignments exist, skip" to "per-user lookup-or-create" so the new `vp@` (Linda Park) and `counsellor@` (Marcus Hayes) staff accounts now get the Staff role + scope assignment without dropping existing assignments. After `tsx src/build-cache.ts`: cache holds 7 account-scope pairs (was 5) — admin/principal 444, teacher 31, student 15, parent 11, vp/counsellor 10. Tenant base table count unchanged at 74 (data only — no DDL).

**Step 4 lands `014_hr_onboarding.sql`** with 3 base tables: `hr_onboarding_templates` (school-scoped catalogue, optional `position_id` for position-specific templates, UNIQUE(school_id, name)), `hr_onboarding_checklists` (per-(employee, template) instance with status NOT_STARTED → IN_PROGRESS → COMPLETED enforced by a multi-column CHECK that started_at and completed_at stay in sync with status, UNIQUE(employee_id, template_id) so a hire can hold at most one checklist per template), `hr_onboarding_tasks` (5 category values DOCUMENT/TRAINING/SYSTEM_ACCESS/ORIENTATION/OTHER, 4 status values PENDING/IN_PROGRESS/COMPLETED/SKIPPED, `due_days_from_start INT >= 0` CHECK, multi-column CHECK that `completed_at` is set if and only if status is COMPLETED or SKIPPED). 3 intra-tenant FKs (1 to `hr_employees ON DELETE CASCADE`, 1 to `hr_onboarding_templates`, 1 to `hr_onboarding_checklists ON DELETE CASCADE`). Tenant logical base table count: 74 (was 71). Live verification on `tenant_demo`: all 8 CHECK violations rejected (status enums, status/started-at sync, category enum, due_days negative, completed-at sync), happy-path "New Teacher Onboarding" template with 3 tasks instantiated for Rivera + one task moved to COMPLETED + checklist moved to IN_PROGRESS, UNIQUE(employee_id, template_id) rejects duplicate, ON DELETE CASCADE drops all 3 tasks when the parent checklist is deleted.
