- **Cycle 11: Counselling & Student Support — COMPLETE pending architecture review.** All 10 steps done; vertical-slice CAT at `docs/cycle11-cat-script.md` verified live on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-05 (8-check schema preamble + 10 plan scenarios all green; both ADR-057 wire envelopes captured live — `svc.referral.created` from S1 + `svc.tier.changed` from S6 with reason=CREATED + oldTier=null). Final cycle totals: **14 svc\_\* base tables** + 1 cross-cycle FK backfill on Cycle 9's `svc_behavior_plans.caseload_id` (155 → **169** tenant base tables); **32 intra-tenant FKs**; **0 cross-schema FKs**; **48 endpoints** across 10 services; **2 Kafka emit topics**; **1 dedicated FERPA permission gate** (`student_counseling_record:read`); **8 web routes + 47 React Query hooks**; IAM catalogue 447 → **450**. Tagged `cycle11-complete` after the closeout commit + CI green. Plan at `docs/campusos-cycle11-implementation-plan.html`; handoff at `HANDOFF-CYCLE11.md`. Cycle 11 completes the M27 Student Services module Cycle 9 began — 16 core tables across 4 domains: caseloads + referrals (Step 1, 4 tables), sessions + FERPA notes + MTSS/RTI tiers + interventions + team meetings (Step 2, 8 tables), coordinated care + mandatory reporting + Cycle 9 BIP `caseload_id` FK backfill (Step 3, 2 tables + 1 FK backfill). The 6 wellbeing check-in tables defer to Cycle 11.1. Cycle 11 is the second-highest sensitivity domain after Health — `svc_session_notes` is FERPA-protected counselling content gated on a dedicated `student_counseling_record:read` permission that teachers and parents NEVER hold; `svc_mandatory_reports` core fields (description, report_type, reported_to_authority, report_date) are immutable once past FILED; `svc_coordinated_care_notes` is gated on the **intersection** of `hlt-001:read` AND `cou-007:read` — neither permission alone unlocks it. Step 1 ships `036_svc_caseloads_referrals.sql` with 4 logical base tables: `svc_caseloads` (one row per (counsellor, student) assignment per academic year; **two partial UNIQUE keystones** — `(student_id, academic_year_id) WHERE status='ACTIVE' AND is_primary_counselor=true` for one primary per student-year, and `(counselor_id, student_id, academic_year_id) WHERE status='ACTIVE'` for no duplicate active assignments; the Cycle 9 `svc_behavior_plans.caseload_id` soft ref will become a real FK in Step 3); `svc_referral_types` (per-school catalogue with 4-value default_priority + parent-notification flag); `svc_referrals` (7-state lifecycle SUBMITTED → TRIAGED → ACCEPTED → IN_PROGRESS → COMPLETED + DECLINED + CANCELLED; nullable assigned_counselor_id with SET NULL so a counsellor leaving returns the referral to the unassigned triage state); `svc_referral_activity` (IMMUTABLE per ADR-010, 6-value activity_type CHECK, CASCADE on parent referral mirroring Cycle 8 tkt_ticket_activity + Cycle 10 hlth_health_access_log). 8 new intra-tenant FKs (5 NO ACTION, 2 CASCADE, 1 SET NULL); 0 cross-schema FKs. Tenant logical base table count: 155 → **159**. **Live verification on `tenant_demo` 2026-05-04 (single BEGIN…ROLLBACK with savepoints, 23 assertions all green):** happy-path INSERT across all 4 tables; concern_chk + status_chk + priority_chk × 2 + activity_type_chk all reject BOGUS; **partial UNIQUE primary keystone fires both directions** (rejects 2nd primary for same (student, year) AND accepts non-primary consultant on same (student, year)); **partial UNIQUE active_assignment keystone** rejects 2nd row for (counsellor, student, year) even with is_primary_counselor=false; **CLOSED status releases the primary slot** (UPDATE primary to CLOSED then INSERT new primary succeeds); lifecycle UPDATE SUBMITTED → TRIAGED → ACCEPTED with assigned_counselor_id populated; FK rejection on bogus counselor_id / academic_year_id / referred_by / referral_type_id; UNIQUE(school_id, name) on referral_types rejects duplicate; **NO ACTION on referral_types delete with referencing referral fires** (admin must deactivate via is_active=false); all 6 activity_type values accepted in one block; **CASCADE on referral delete drops 9 audit rows in one statement** (IMMUTABLE-but-CASCADE-on-parent shape verified). All 8 FK delete actions confirmed via `pg_constraint.confdeltype`. Splitter `;`-in-string trap not tripped — tenth migration in a row to clear the trap on first attempt (Cycles 4–11 unbroken streak). Idempotent re-provision verified (zero new applies on second run). Both `tenant_demo` and `tenant_test` provisioned cleanly. (Steps 2–10 — sessions + FERPA notes + MTSS schema, coordinated care + mandatory reporting + Cycle 9 caseload_id FK backfill, seed + COU-001..007 + student_counseling_record:read permissions, NestJS modules with FERPA gate + irreversible note locking + intersection-gated coordinated care + immutable mandatory reports, UI surface, vertical-slice CAT — remain to ship.)

Cycle 9 endpoint count: **28** (no new endpoints in Steps 6–10).

**Wave 2 Phase 2 punch list (carry-over from Wave 1).** These are the deferred items consolidated across the post-cycle reviews. None are blocking; they should be addressed before the platform onboards real schools at scale, sequenced naturally as related cycles surface them:

1. **Permission model rename / split** (Cycle 8 follow-up 1) — `IT-001` is functioning as the de-facto helpdesk function across IT/Facilities/HR-support tickets but the name is IT-only. Decision deferred until the M65 Facilities cycle surfaces real cross-functional admin overlap.
2. **Category uniqueness** (Cycle 8 follow-up 5) — `tkt_categories` UNIQUE(school_id, name) is global across the tree. Switch to per-parent uniqueness when real-world catalogue collisions appear.
3. **Consumer tenant routing validation** (carried since Cycle 3) — `unwrapEnvelope` builds `schemaName = 'tenant_' + subdomain` from the `tenant-subdomain` header without validating against `platform_tenant_routing`. Tighten before multi-tenant production.
4. **Outbox pattern for finance / emergency / workflow-critical events** (carried since Cycle 5) — `KafkaProducerService.emit()` is best-effort and never throws. The payments + workflow + ticket paths need transactional outbox semantics before real money flows.
5. **`X-Tenant-Subdomain` header in production** — `TenantResolverMiddleware` accepts it in every environment. Restrict to dev/test once production frontends rely on subdomain hostname routing.
6. **Platform-scope auth path** — `PermissionGuard` denies platform-scope routes that lack a tenant context. Needs a platform-scope authorization mode before v11 CRM / OPS / cross-tenant ops modules ship.
7. **Custom-rule TaskWorker fallback validation** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE7) — `TaskWorker.resolveOwners` flexible `recipientAccountId` / `accountId` fallback is fine for the 8 seeded system rules, but should validate the assignee against the calling tenant's projections once schools can author custom rules.
8. **DLQ row dashboard / alert** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE3) — `platform.platform_dlq_messages` is created and used by the consumer retry/park logic, but a parked poison message currently sits silently. Ops Phase 2 wiring.
9. **Counsellor role split** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE9 MAJOR 4) — `BEH-002:read+write` is granted to the generic Staff role, which the seed describes as covering VPs / counsellors / admin assistants. Service treats `beh-002:write` as counsellor scope (mechanically correct via `isSchoolAdmin OR holds beh-002:write`) but the role mapping is broader than the domain language. Behaviour Intervention Plans are sensitive student-support records; "admin assistant" should not automatically imply BIP authoring rights. Introduce a distinct Counsellor role (or narrow the assignment via a new function code) before the platform onboards real schools.
10. **Scheduled-dose duplicate logging** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE10 MAJOR 1) — `AdministrationService.administer()` and `logMissed()` insert into `hlth_medication_administrations` without a UNIQUE / advisory lock on `(medication_id, schedule_entry_id, date)`, so two nurses logging the same scheduled dose at the same minute would land 2 rows. Reviewer accepts this as non-blocking for the demo phase; lands before pilot. Fix pattern: copy Cycle 6 `InvoiceService.generateFromSchedule` advisory-tx-lock + in-tx existence check, OR add a partial UNIQUE INDEX on `(schedule_entry_id, administered_at::date) WHERE was_missed=false` for active doses.
11. **Nurse / Health Office split** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE10 MAJOR 2) — `Staff` role currently grants all 5 HLT codes read+write. Fine for demo where one persona covers all health-staff functions; before real school onboarding the Staff role should split into Nurse (full HLT) / Health Office (read-only) / Counsellor (HLT-001 + IEP only). Joins #9 above as a single role-redesign task before pilot.
12. **IEP parent visibility — locked product decision** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE10 MAJOR 3) — `IepPlanService.buildVisibility` GUARDIAN branch returns full plan detail (parents are full IEP team participants per IDEA / 504 statute); the Step 9 parent UI on `/children/[id]/health` deliberately omits IEP from the summary card to keep that surface focused on day-to-day health awareness. Reviewer accepts as intentional. Decision documented in `REVIEW-CYCLE10-CHATGPT.md`; lock and re-confirm before pilot.
13. **Teacher review-moderation scope** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE12 MAJOR 5) — `ReviewService.hasModeratorScope` treats any non-student actor with `lib-003:write` as a moderator who can hide/unhide reviews. Teachers hold lib-003:write to author reading lists, so they currently inherit moderator power over the entire student review corpus. Real schools likely want a separate `LIB-005:moderate` permission or constrain teacher moderation to class-scoped reviews. Joins #9 + #11 above as part of the broader Counsellor / Nurse / Librarian role-split work before pilot.
14. **STAFF reading-log breadth** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE12 MAJOR 6) — `ReadingLogService.list({ studentId })` allows any STAFF + ADMIN actor with `lib-003:read` to read any student's reading log. The seed grants lib-003:read to admin tier only today, but as the role model evolves a generic Staff member could inherit broad reading-history access. Pre-pilot should narrow to "librarian scope OR teacher of an enrolled class" depending on the UI surface.
15. **Hold duplicate race** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE12 MAJOR 7) — `HoldService.placeHold` does a check-then-insert without an advisory lock or partial UNIQUE on `(catalogue_item_id, patron_id) WHERE status IN ('PENDING','READY')`. Two concurrent submissions could both pass the pre-check. Race window is tiny and the worst case is "patron has 2 PENDING holds for the same item until the librarian cancels one" — non-blocking but worth a tenant migration adding the partial UNIQUE before pilot.
16. **Athletic Director role split** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE13 MAJOR 6) — Cycle 13 Step 4 grants `ATH-001..005` read+write to the generic Staff role (covering the AD), so a non-AD staff member with `ATH-005:read` can view medical-clearance details (physician name, contact, document S3 key, expiry) and a non-AD staff member with `ATH-003:read` / `ATH-004:read` can read coaching assignments / injuries beyond what the InjuryService row-scope now restricts. Reviewer accepts the broad grant for the demo phase but flags that a dedicated AD role should hold the `ATH-*` codes alone before real schools onboard. Joins #9 + #11 + #13 as part of the broader role-split work before pilot. Also carries the deferred AD-only result-correction workflow that reverses + recomputes `ath_season_records` inside one tenant tx (REVIEW-CYCLE13 MAJOR 4 — `patchResult` currently refuses outcome changes outright pending this surface).
17. **Result correction reverse-and-recompute workflow** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE13 MAJOR 4) — `ResultService.patchResult` currently refuses outcome changes with a friendly 400 because flipping an outcome (WIN/LOSS/DRAW/FORFEIT) without recomputing `ath_season_records` would leave the per-roster record stale. Future AD-only correction endpoint should lock the result row + parent game + season record inside one tenant tx, decrement the existing outcome's counters, write the new outcome, increment the new outcome's counters, and re-emit `ath.game.result.entered` so any future fan-out consumer sees the corrected snapshot. Score / `score_by_period` / notes corrections continue to flow through `patchResult` in the meantime because they do not affect the aggregate.
18. **`msg_thread_stats` recompute on edit/delete** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE14 MAJOR 5) — `ThreadStatsConsumer` increments `message_count` and updates `last_message_preview` on `msg.message.posted` only. Edits to the most recent message and soft-deletes do not propagate, so the inbox preview can drift on the sender's own most recent message until the next `posted` event lands. Pre-pilot fix: emit `msg.message.edited` + `msg.message.deleted` from `MessageService.edit()` + `softDelete()`; extend `ThreadStatsConsumer` to recompute `last_message_at` / `last_message_preview` / `message_count` from `msg_messages` (`MAX(created_at)` + `LEFT(body,100) WHERE deleted_at IS NULL ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1` + `COUNT WHERE deleted_at IS NULL`) instead of the simple increment.
19. **Audience-targeted emergency alert list filtering** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE14 MAJOR 7) — `EmergencyAlertService.list()` returns alert headers to every authenticated user with `com-003:read` and inlines `myDelivery=null` for non-recipients. Today's school-wide audience default makes every active school user a recipient by construction so the leak is theoretical, but when audience targeting lands (Phase 2 — routes via M61 Transportation, year groups, classes, custom lists), the non-admin list path must filter to alerts where the caller has a `msg_emergency_alert_deliveries` row.
20. **`mtg_meeting_participants.source` marker** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE15 MAJOR 6) — `SlotService.cancel()` deletes the parent's participant row when no other slot is booked, but the schema cannot distinguish "auto-created by booking" from "manually added" rows. Today's seed and CAT path do not exercise the manual-then-booked-then-cancelled corner, but a real-school operator could land in it. Pre-pilot fix: add `source TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'MANUAL'` 2-value CHECK column (MANUAL / SLOT_BOOKING) on `mtg_meeting_participants`; `SlotService.book` inserts with `source='SLOT_BOOKING'`; `cancel()` only removes participant rows where source matches.
21. **CAT live-output capture for sensitive cycles** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE15 MAJOR 8) — review-class observation. The Cycle 15 CAT script (`docs/cycle15-cat-script.md`) reads as a reproducible script rather than a live verification record with captured pass/fail observations per scenario. Future sensitive-cycle CATs (Cycle 16 Enrollment is next) should inline live shell-output snippets per assertion so a reviewer can see exactly what landed when the script was last executed against `tenant_demo`. Documentation pattern improvement only — no code change.
22. **Enrolment Officer (EO) role split** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE16 Round 2 accepted follow-up) — `ApplicationStageService.advance`, `ApplicationScoringService.assertEoOrAdmin`, `OnboardingService.completeTask`, the `assertCanReadApplication` row-scope helper, and offer-accept onboarding generation all treat `actor.personType === 'STAFF'` as the EO authority because the IAM seed grants `STU-003:read+write` to the generic Staff role (which the seed describes as covering VPs / counsellors / admin assistants / EO). Real schools likely want a distinct EO role that does not implicitly include counselling / nursing / VP duties. Joins items 9 (Counsellor split), 11 (Nurse / Health Office), 13 (Librarian moderator scope), 14 (STAFF reading-log breadth), 16 (Athletic Director split), 25 (student-life Staff scope) as part of the broader role-redesign work before pilot.
23. **Anonymised student-visible service-hour ranking** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE17 Round 2 Phase 2 follow-up) — Cycle 17's `GET /clubs/service-programmes/:id/leaderboard` is currently restricted to admin / staff per MAJOR 6's privacy fix. Schools may want a student-safe per-class anonymised ranking ("you're in the top 10%" / "your class average is X hours") that does not name peers. Phase 2 should add a new endpoint (e.g. `/clubs/service-programmes/:id/my-rank`) returning aggregate position + cohort summary without exposing other students' names or detailed totals.
24. **`ext_service_hour_approvals.approved_by` schema cleanup** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE17 Round 2 Phase 2 follow-up) — `ServiceHourService.log()` writes a placeholder `hr_employees` row id at insert time because the schema requires `approved_by NOT NULL`. Round 1 mitigated this by suppressing `approvedByName` in the DTO when status=PENDING, but the placeholder row still sits in the table. Cleaner long-term: a tenant migration that either drops the NOT NULL until review or splits into `assigned_reviewer_id` (settable at log) + `reviewed_by` (settable on review). Schema-level migration only — service code is already structured to accept either model.
25. **Student-life Staff scope** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE17 Round 2 Phase 2 follow-up) — generic Staff currently owns advisor / chaperone / leaderboard / approval workflows. Same role-split concern as items 9 + 11 + 13 + 14 + 16 + 22; before pilot, distinct Activities Coordinator / Service Hour Reviewer roles may be appropriate.
26. **Group event public RSVP eligibility** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE18 MAJOR 7) — `GroupEventService.assertCanRead`/`rsvp` allow any caller who can see a public group event to RSVP. "Public" should distinguish visibility from RSVP eligibility. Pre-pilot fix: add `grp_events.allow_public_rsvp BOOLEAN DEFAULT false`; gate `rsvp()` to members + (`allow_public_rsvp=true` for non-members). Schema migration + service tweak.
27. **Group event RSVP reconciliation on patch** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE18 MAJOR 8) — `GroupEventService.patch` allows reducing `maxAttendees` below current `GOING` count or flipping `requiresRsvp` off without reconciling existing RSVPs. Pre-pilot fix: either reject destructive changes when active RSVPs exist (the conservative path) or run a reconciliation workflow that flips over-cap GOINGs to a new `WAITLISTED` state.
28. **Group emit outbox** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE18 MAJOR 9) — `grp.announcement.posted` and `grp.event.created` are best-effort emits. Joins item 4 (outbox priority list) — once group announcements wire into the Cycle 3 NotificationQueueService for member fan-out, group emits become workflow-critical and need the outbox.
29. **`grp_members.person_id` naming** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE18 MAJOR 10) — column is named `person_id` but stores `platform_users.id`. ADR-055 separation hazard. Pre-pilot future migration renames to `account_id` (additive column + backfill + drop-old) or at minimum annotates via `COMMENT ON COLUMN`. No runtime bug today; same naming convention applied historically on Cycle 11 + Cycle 12 patron columns.
30. **Vehicle / driver credential detail row-scope tightening** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE19 MAJOR 9) — see Phase 2 backlog above. Joins items 9 / 11 / 13 / 14 / 16 / 22 / 25 / 26 in the broader role-split work for a dedicated TC role split before pilot.
31. **Run-start should match the inspection driver to route driver** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE19 MAJOR 10) — driver accountability refinement. Recommendation-class today since inspection is vehicle-level; introduce either a "delegated certification" flag on the inspection or require `inspection.driver_id === route.driver_id` on `RunLogService.start`.
32. **Food Service Manager (FSM) role split** (carried from REVIEW-CYCLE20 MAJOR 9) — generic Staff currently grants all FDS-001..004 read+write tiers as a stand-in for the FSM persona. Pre-pilot, a dedicated Food Service Manager role moves these write permissions out of generic Staff. Joins items 9 / 11 / 13 / 14 / 16 / 22 / 25 / 26 / 30 in the broader role-split work.

## UI Design Principles

These are foundational decisions for the web app. New views must follow them; existing views are migrated as they are touched.

- **Less is more.** No screen should feel cluttered. When in doubt, remove information rather than add it. The previous persona-specific dashboards (TeacherDashboard, ParentDashboard, StudentDashboard, AdminDashboard) packed stats, tables, and queues onto the home page; they have been deleted in favour of a clean launchpad.
- **Home page is a launchpad, not a dashboard.** `/dashboard` is a Google-style centered page: `CampusOS` logo, persona-aware greeting (`Good morning/afternoon/evening, {Name}`), a search input ("What would you like to do today?"), and a grid of App tiles. No stats, no tables, no lists. Each App owns its own detail views.
- **Apps are the primary navigation unit.** The current apps are **Classes** (or **My Classes** for students, **My Children** for parents), **Messages**, and **Announcements**. The sidebar lists exactly the same apps as the home grid — the two surfaces stay in sync via `apps/web/src/components/shell/apps.tsx::getAppsForUser(user)`. Adding a new app is one edit: add it to the catalogue and it shows up in both surfaces with the right persona gating.
- **Persona-aware app grid.** Driven by IAM permissions and `personType`, not by role names:
  - `sch-001:admin` OR `personType=STAFF` → Classes tile pointing at `/classes`
  - `personType=STUDENT` → My Classes tile (same `/classes` route, student variant)
  - `personType=GUARDIAN` → My Children tile pointing at `/children`
  - Anyone with `com-001:read` → Messages tile
  - Anyone with `com-002:read` → Announcements tile
- **iOS-style unread badges.** Red circle, white number, capped at "99+", in the top-right of an app tile and trailing-aligned on a sidebar row. Wired through `apps/web/src/hooks/use-app-badges.ts`:
  - **Messages** badge = sum of `ThreadDto.unreadCount` from `useThreads()` (15s poll).
  - **Announcements** badge = count of `useAnnouncements()` rows where `isPublished && !isRead` (30s poll).
  - The hooks are gated on permissions via an optional `enabled` argument so a STUDENT without `com-001:read` doesn't 403 on `/threads`. Both queries already poll, so badges refresh without extra plumbing.
  - The top-bar `NotificationBell` is the unified cross-app notifier and is independent of these per-app badges.
- **Logo is sans-serif.** The "CampusOS" wordmark uses the Tailwind `font-sans` stack (DM Sans / Inter / system) with `font-semibold` and `tracking-tight`. Never `font-display` (DM Serif Display) for the wordmark. The default text wordmark is a placeholder until a school uploads its own logo. `font-display` is still allowed for in-page article titles like an announcement detail headline; it must not be used for the brand mark.
- **App pages are minimal lists, not dashboards.** `/classes` and `/children` are flat grids of cards (one card per class / child) with one or two primary actions per row. Recent activity, queues, and aggregate stats belong in their own future apps (Attendance, Absences, etc.), not bolted onto a launchpad or a list page.

## Architecture

- **840 tables** across 38 modules, governed by 76 ADRs (Architecture Decision Records)
- Schema-per-tenant multi-tenancy (PostgreSQL `search_path` switching)
- Modular monolith (NestJS) with planned extraction of 6 services
- Event-driven via Kafka
- Phased delivery plan (see "Delivery Plan (Revised)" above): build core (Cycles 0–3) → test & refine → expand (Cycles 4–8)

## Tech Stack

- **Backend:** NestJS 10 (TypeScript strict), Node.js 22 (CI + production image)
- **Frontend:** Next.js 14 (App Router, Tailwind CSS, React Query, Zustand)
- **Database:** PostgreSQL 16 (Prisma ORM, schema-per-tenant)
- **Cache:** Redis 7 (ioredis)
- **Events:** Apache Kafka (KafkaJS)
- **Auth:** External IdP via OIDC (Keycloak for dev). CampusOS never stores passwords.
- **Monorepo:** pnpm + Turborepo

## Project Structure

```
apps/api/                → NestJS backend (modular monolith)
apps/api/src/auth/       → AuthGuard (JWT), PermissionGuard, @Public, @RequirePermission
apps/api/src/tenant/     → TenantResolverMiddleware, TenantGuard, TenantPrismaService, AsyncLocalStorage
apps/api/src/iam/        → Roles, permissions, assignments, effective access cache, ActorContextService (resolves caller persona + isSchoolAdmin + employeeId per tenant — Cycle 4 Step 0 added the tenant-scoped hr_employees lookup keyed on iam_person.id)
apps/api/src/platform/   → M0 Platform Core
apps/api/src/sis/        → M20 SIS Core (Cycle 1 Step 5): students, classes, families, guardians; /classes/my includes todayAttendance summary (Step 8); /students/me added in Cycle 2 Step 9 to bootstrap a STUDENT persona's own studentId; AcademicYearController (Cycle 6 Step 8) exposing GET /academic-years (read-only catalogue lookup ordered by start_date DESC, gated on stu-001:read) — backs the create-period dropdown in the Admissions admin UI.
apps/api/src/attendance/ → ATT-001..005 (Cycle 1 Step 6): attendance + absence requests + Kafka emits
apps/api/src/payments/   → M84 Family Billing (Cycle 6 Step 7). Seven services + six controllers + 24 endpoints + one Kafka consumer (PaymentAccountWorker). LedgerService (internal-only `recordEntry(tx, ...)` called inside open tenant transactions; Redis-cached balance at `ledger:balance:{accountId}` TTL=30s with cache invalidation in same write tx; pay_ledger_entries IMMUTABLE per ADR-010 — no UPDATE/DELETE service methods). FeeScheduleService (admin-only fee category + schedule CRUD on fin-001:admin; reads on fin-001:read). FamilyAccountService (list / getById / listStudents / balance / ledger; row-scoped on account_holder_id = actor.personId for parents). InvoiceService (create DRAFT / list / get / send locks invoice FOR UPDATE + writes CHARGE ledger entry + emits pay.invoice.created / cancel refuses on PAID / generate-from-schedule bulk idempotent on existing fee_schedule_id attribution). PaymentService (list / get / pay locks invoice FOR UPDATE + writes PAYMENT ledger entry + recomputes invoice status PARTIAL/PAID + emits pay.payment.received; Stripe stubbed — CARD auto-COMPLETE with mock pi_dev_<uuid>; refuses overpay vs balance-due pre-check). RefundService (list admin-only at service layer / issue locks payment FOR UPDATE + writes REFUND ledger entry + marks payment REFUNDED if full-amount + emits pay.refund.issued; refund cap pre-check; Stripe stubbed re_dev_<uuid>). PaymentPlanService (admin-only POST creates plan + auto-generates installment rows in one tx; due dates = startDate + n × frequency; last installment absorbs sub-cent residue). PaymentAccountWorker (Kafka consumer on enr.student.enrolled under group payment-account-worker; reuses unwrapEnvelope + processWithIdempotency claim-after-success; UPSERTs pay_family_accounts on (school, account_holder_id) + auto-allocates FA-#### account_number from MAX+1 + idempotently links sis_students; gracefully skips when guardian_person_id is null or sis_students doesn't exist yet). PaymentsModule imports TenantModule + IamModule + KafkaModule + NotificationsModule (for RedisService).
apps/api/src/enrollment/ → M81 Enrollment (Cycle 6 Step 6). Five services + four controllers + 17 endpoints under stu-003:read/write/admin. EnrollmentPeriodService (period CRUD + nested stream + capacity create; admin writes lock the row FOR UPDATE; window CHECK re-validated on PATCH against merged state). ApplicationService (parent submission stamps guardian_person_id from actor.personId; admin status transitions with assertTransitionAllowed function rejecting illegal moves at the service layer; admin patch locks row FOR UPDATE and recomputes capacity_summary in same tx; non-admin reads strip is_confidential=true notes). OfferService (admin issue; CONDITIONAL conditions verify; respond locks BOTH enr_offers AND enr_applications FOR UPDATE in same tx — Cycle 5 review carry-over satisfied; on ACCEPT, application transitions to ENROLLED + capacity_summary recomputes + enr.student.enrolled fires). WaitlistService (admin-only list; offer-from-waitlist locks waitlist + parent app FOR UPDATE in one tx). CapacitySummaryService (internal-only UPSERT helper called inside open tenant transactions on every status flip; aggregates intake_capacities + applications + offers and recomputes the per-(period, grade) row). Emits enr.application.submitted, enr.application.status_changed, enr.offer.issued, enr.offer.responded, enr.student.enrolled (the last is the Step 7 PaymentAccountWorker subscription topic). EnrollmentModule imports TenantModule + IamModule + KafkaModule and lives between SchedulingModule and NotificationsModule.
apps/api/src/scheduling/ → M22 Academic Scheduling (Cycle 5 Steps 5 + 6). Step 5: BellScheduleService (6 endpoints — list / get-with-periods / create / patch / bulk upsert periods / set-default; admin gates on sch-001:admin; set-default atomically clears any other default in the same tx so the partial UNIQUE INDEX(school_id) WHERE is_default never rejects the flip). TimetableService (7 endpoints under sch-001:read with sch-001:admin writes; reads return the full join shape — sis_classes/sis_courses/sch_periods/sch_rooms/hr_employees/iam_person; the translateConflict helper decodes SQLSTATE 23P01 into a friendly 409 with the conflicting actor name, looking up sch_rooms.name / sch_periods.name / hr_employees+iam_person on the failure path; emits sch.timetable.updated on every write). RoomService (4 endpoints under sch-005:read/admin; list endpoint accepts availabilityDate + availabilityPeriodId and annotates each row with available:boolean|null by intersecting against active timetable slots that match the period for that date). RoomBookingService (4 endpoints under sch-005:read/write; assertNoConflicts checks both existing CONFIRMED bookings whose time range overlaps AND active timetable slots whose period clock-time on the booking's ISO weekday overlaps the booking window — schema does not enforce booking-vs-slot conflict, this is the app-layer gate per the Step 2 out-of-scope decision). RoomChangeRequestService (5 endpoints under sch-005:read/write; non-admins see only their own rows via row-scope filter on requested_by = actor.employeeId; submit refuses if the slot's teacher_id is set and not the actor; approve/reject admin-only with reviewed_chk multi-column CHECK enforced by the schema). Step 6: CalendarService (5 endpoints under sch-003:read/write — list / getById / GET /calendar/day/:date day-resolution with order override → event-with-bell_schedule_id → default → none / create / patch / delete; non-admins see only published, admins with includeDrafts=true see drafts; PATCH that toggles allDay clears times in the same UPDATE so the time-consistency CHECK never fires mid-flight). DayOverrideService (3 endpoints under sch-003:read/admin — list / create / delete-by-date; pre-checks for an existing row to surface a friendly 409 instead of letting the schema raise). CoverageService (4 endpoints under sch-004:read/write — list / getById with row-scope (admin OR absent OR assigned) / assign locks the row FOR UPDATE then writes both the status flip and the matching sch_substitution_timetable row in one tx + emits sch.coverage.assigned outside the tx / cancel drops the substitution row in the same tx then flips status=CANCELLED). SubstitutionService (2 read-only endpoints — substitution rows are written exclusively by CoverageService.assign). CoverageConsumer (Kafka consumer on hr.leave.coverage_needed under group coverage-consumer; reuses unwrapEnvelope + processWithIdempotency from notification-consumer-base; per inbound event enumerates weekdays Mon–Fri in [startDate, endDate], joins through sch_timetable_slots → sch_periods for each affectedClasses[].classId, filters slots whose date range covers the date AND whose period day_of_week is NULL or matches the date's ISO weekday, INSERTs sch_coverage_requests with status=OPEN — UNIQUE(timetable_slot_id, coverage_date) is the schema-side belt-and-braces dedup; emits sch.coverage.needed once with createdCoverageIds[] when new rows land). SchedulingModule imports TenantModule + IamModule + KafkaModule + (Cycle 5 Step 9) SisModule. Step 9 adds TimetableService.listForStudent(studentId, actor) which routes through StudentService.assertCanViewStudent for the row-scope check and joins sis_enrollments → sch_timetable_slots for the student's ACTIVE enrollments. New endpoint GET /timetable/student/:studentId gated on stu-001:read (every persona) — the row-scope at the service layer is the actual access gate, since parents and students don't hold sch-001:read.
apps/api/src/hr/         → M80 HR/Workforce (Cycle 4 Steps 6 + 7). Step 6: EmployeeService, PositionService, EmployeeDocumentService (12 endpoints under hr-001:read / write / admin; row-scope guard on documents). Step 7: LeaveService (7 endpoints + 4 Kafka emits — hr.leave.requested/approved/rejected/cancelled — under hr-003:read/write; balance updates inside executeInTenantTransaction so the non-negative CHECKs from migration 012 fail loudly on underflow), CertificationService (4 endpoints under hr-004:read/write; admin-only verify with hr.certification.verified emit + admin /certifications/expiring-soon sweep returning the partial-index window), TrainingComplianceService (per-employee + admin school-wide dashboard; derived green/amber/red urgency from is_compliant + days_until_due), LeaveNotificationConsumer (group leave-notification-consumer; subscribes to all 4 leave topics; notifies admins on requested + submitter on approved/rejected; on approved also republishes hr.leave.coverage_needed with affected class ids resolved via sis_class_teachers for the Cycle 5 Scheduling consumer). DTOs use class-validator with IsIn arrays kept in sync with SQL CHECK constraints. Imports KafkaModule + NotificationsModule for the Step 7 wiring.
apps/api/src/classroom/  → M21 Classroom (Cycle 2): assignment + category CRUD (Step 4) + submissions, grading (single/batch/publish), gradebook reads, and progress notes (Step 5) + GradebookSnapshotWorker (Step 6 — first Kafka consumer; subscribes to cls.grade.{published,unpublished} under group `gradebook-snapshot-worker`, debounces 30s per (class,student), recomputes cls_gradebook_snapshots). Step 9 adds GET /students/:studentId/classes/:classId/grades (per-(student, class) assignment breakdown — published-only for non-managers; reuses GradebookService.assertCanViewStudent + AssignmentService.assertCanReadClass for row scope). 4 services + 1 worker + 6 controllers; emits cls.submission.submitted, cls.grade.published, cls.grade.unpublished, cls.progress_note.published.
apps/api/src/kafka/      → KafkaProducerService (best-effort emit; ADR-057 envelope wraps every payload — Cycle 3 Step 0; takes EmitOptions {topic, key, payload, sourceModule, ...}) + KafkaConsumerService (best-effort consumer registry, Cycle 2 Step 6) + IdempotencyService (claim against platform.platform_event_consumer_idempotency) + event-envelope.ts (EventEnvelope interface, envelopeFromOptions, prefixedTopic helper for KAFKA_TOPIC_ENV — Cycle 3 Step 0)
apps/api/src/notifications/ → M40 notification pipeline (Cycle 3 Step 5; Step 8 read-side). RedisService (best-effort ioredis: SET NX idempotency, ZADD on `notif:inapp:{accountId}`, HINCRBY/HDEL/HVALS/HGETALL on `inbox:{accountId}` for per-thread unread; the HDEL/HVALS/HGETALL helpers were added in Step 6 for UnreadCountService; the Step 8 helpers `listInAppNotifications` (ZREVRANGE), `countInAppSince` (ZCOUNT exclusive), and `get/setNotificationLastReadAt` (`notif:lastread:{accountId}` epoch-ms key, no TTL) back the bell). NotificationQueueService.enqueue() (Redis SET NX → preference + quiet-hours check → INSERT into msg_notification_queue PENDING). NotificationDeliveryWorker (10s poll across all platform.schools; SELECT FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED LIMIT 25; IN_APP delivery via Redis ZADD + msg_notification_log row; EMAIL/PUSH/SMS stubbed; up-to-5 retries with 30s backoff before status=FAILED). NotificationInboxService + NotificationInboxController (Step 8 — 3 endpoints under com-001:read OR com-002:read: GET /notifications/inbox returns `{ unreadCount, items, lastReadAt }` from the Redis sorted set, GET /notifications/history paginates msg_notification_queue with keyset cursor on COALESCE(sent_at, created_at) and an optional exact `type` filter, POST /notifications/mark-all-read bumps the lastread key to Date.now()). 5 Kafka consumers under `consumers/`: AttendanceNotificationConsumer (att.student.marked_{tardy,absent} → guardians via sis_student_guardians), GradeNotificationConsumer (cls.grade.published → student + guardians), ProgressNoteNotificationConsumer (cls.progress_note.published → guardians if is_parent_visible + student if is_student_visible), AbsenceRequestNotificationConsumer (att.absence.requested → school admins via iam_effective_access_cache; att.absence.reviewed → submitter), MessageNotificationConsumer (msg.message.posted → non-sender thread participants + Redis unread bump; producer ships in Step 6). Cycle 8 added TicketNotificationConsumer (4 tkt.ticket.* topics with internal-comment staff-only routing + self-resolve suppression). Cycle 9 Step 6 adds **BehaviourNotificationConsumer** under group `behaviour-notification-consumer` subscribing to all 4 dev.beh.* topics — `beh.incident.reported` → school admins via `iam_effective_access_cache` lookup for sch-001:admin holders; `beh.action.parent_notification_required` → iterates `payload.guardianAccountIds` (ActionService pre-resolved portal-enabled guardians via sis_student_guardians + portal_access=true); `beh.bip.feedback_requested` → `payload.recipientAccountId` (FeedbackService pre-resolved teacher's platform_users.id) with hr_employees→platform_users fallback; `beh.incident.resolved` → original reporter bridged from `payload.reportedById` (hr_employees.id) via hr_employees.person_id → iam_person.id → platform_users.id, with self-resolve suppression when `payload.resolvedByAccountId === reporterAccountId` (mirrors Cycle 8 follow-up 2 ticket pattern). Per-topic deep_link populated for the bell (`/behaviour/<incidentId>` for incident_reported + incident_resolved; `/children/<studentId>/behaviour` for action_assigned; `/behavior-plans/feedback` for bip_feedback_requested). Shared base helper `unwrapEnvelope()` + `processWithIdempotency()` (claim-after-success, REVIEW-CYCLE2 BLOCKING 2).
apps/api/src/announcements/ → M40 announcements (Cycle 3 Step 7). AnnouncementService (list / get / create / patch / mark-read / stats; manager scope = School Admin OR personType=STAFF, reader scope = msg_announcement_audiences row required; published is one-way), AudienceFanOutWorker (Kafka consumer on msg.announcement.published, group `audience-fan-out-worker`; resolves audience by audience_type — ALL_SCHOOL/CLASS/YEAR_GROUP/ROLE — into msg_announcement_audiences with delivery_status='DELIVERED', then enqueues `announcement.published` per recipient via NotificationQueueService; reuses unwrapEnvelope + processWithIdempotency for claim-after-success). 6 endpoints under com-002:read / com-002:write: GET /announcements, GET /announcements/:id, POST /announcements (draft or publish-now), PATCH /announcements/:id (edit draft, flip to published), POST /announcements/:id/read (idempotent — also flips audience row PENDING→DELIVERED), GET /announcements/:id/stats (author-or-admin only — total audience + read count + read % + delivery breakdown). Emits msg.announcement.published.
apps/api/src/messaging/  → M40 messaging core (Cycle 3 Step 6; Step 9 adds the compose-helper endpoints). ThreadService (create / list / read / archive — validates participants vs msg_thread_types.allowed_participant_roles via iam_role_assignment + roles + iam_scope, refuses pairs with msg_user_blocks rows, writes msg_admin_access_log when an admin reads a non-participant thread for FERPA audit; Step 9 added listThreadTypes + listRecipients and surfaces lastMessagePreview + lastSenderName on `list` via LATERAL JOIN on msg_messages — null on getById), MessageService (post + emit msg.message.posted + Redis HASH bump + content-moderation gate; 15-min author-only edit window; soft-delete that blanks the body in rowToDto for non-author readers), UnreadCountService (Redis-backed: increment / clearThread / getBadgeCount / getByThread — never queries msg_messages), ContentModerationService (loads active msg_moderation_policies in one tenant query; whole-word case-insensitive keyword match — Scunthorpe-safe; "most restrictive wins" = BLOCKED > ESCALATED > FLAGGED > CLEAN; writes msg_moderation_log on every non-CLEAN verdict). 12 endpoints (10 from Step 6 + 2 from Step 9) all under com-001:read / com-001:write: GET/POST /threads, GET /threads/types (Step 9), GET /threads/recipients (Step 9), GET /threads/:id, POST /threads/:id/read, PATCH /threads/:id/archive, GET/POST /threads/:threadId/messages, PATCH/DELETE /messages/:id, GET /notifications/unread-count (com-001:read OR com-002:read so the badge renders for any persona that holds either communications read tier).
apps/api/src/behavior-plans/ → M27 Student Services Behaviour Intervention Plans (Cycle 9 Step 5). BehaviorPlansModule with 3 services + 3 controllers + 14 endpoints under beh-002:read / write / admin. BehaviorPlanService (lifecycle: hasCounsellorScope() helper = isSchoolAdmin OR holds beh-002:write; buildVisibility() routes counsellors to no filter, STAFF teachers to sis_class_teachers + sis_enrollments row scope, others to AND FALSE; PATCH /:id/{activate,expire} use locked-row tx; activate pre-flights partial UNIQUE on (student_id, plan_type) WHERE status='ACTIVE' carrying conflicting plan id in 400; expire is terminal — partial UNIQUE on the ACTIVE filter releases automatically; generic PATCH refuses status=ACTIVE crossings + EXPIRED reads). GoalService (CRUD per plan; PATCH auto-bumps last_assessed_at = CURRENT_DATE on every progress transition away from NOT_STARTED; DELETE refused on EXPIRED parents). FeedbackService (POST /:id/feedback-requests pre-flights partial UNIQUE on (plan_id, teacher_id) WHERE submitted_at IS NULL + emits beh.bip.feedback_requested with recipientAccountId resolved at emit time for Cycle 7 TaskWorker fan-out via the seeded auto-task rule with target_role=NULL; PATCH /bip-feedback/:id submit path gated on beh-002:read + row-scope actor.employeeId === row.teacher_id OR counsellor/admin override + refuses already-submitted rows; GET /bip-feedback/pending row-scoped to actor.employeeId for non-counsellors, admins/counsellors see all). 1 Kafka emit topic. BehaviorPlansModule imports TenantModule + IamModule + KafkaModule and lives between DisciplineModule and the global guards.
apps/api/src/counselling/ → M27 Student Services Counselling (Cycle 11 Steps 5 + 6 + 7). CounsellingModule with **10 services + 8 controllers + 48 endpoints + 2 Kafka emits** (svc.referral.created from Step 5, svc.tier.changed from Step 7) + 1 dedicated FERPA gate. Step 7 adds MtssTierService (9 endpoints under cou-003 — list with row-scope to caseload-linked students for counsellors / school-wide for admins, admin-only `dashboard` rollup, partial UNIQUE pre-flight on `(student, year, domain) WHERE status='ACTIVE'` with conflicting tier id surfaced in 400, locked-row PATCH that re-emits svc.tier.changed on tier-value change, team-meetings + meeting-students); InterventionService (5 endpoints — list per tier with `latestProgress` inlined via DISTINCT ON, append-only progress entries with no UPDATE/DELETE methods exposed); **CoordinatedCareService — INTERSECTION KEYSTONE** (2 endpoints; controller-level `@RequirePermission('cou-007:read')` gates the narrower of the two intersection codes; service-level `assertIntersectionAccess(actor)` calls hasAnyPermissionInTenant twice for both `hlt-001:read` AND `cou-007:read` and 403s if either is missing — defence-in-depth; POST validates authorRole vs caller's actual team membership); **MandatoryReportService — IMMUTABLE after FILED** (4 endpoints — list with row-scope on reporter_person_id for non-admin reporters; admin-tier PATCH allows ONLY status + cps_response; DTO whitelist plus service-side immutableFields list as defence-in-depth; permanent retention via schema-side NO ACTION FK). Step 6 adds SessionService + SessionNoteService — the FERPA-keystone module. SessionService (6 endpoints under cou-001:read/write — list with row-scope to counsellor own; getById with inlined participants; INDIVIDUAL/GROUP shape validators on POST; locked-row PATCH; participants management with UNIQUE(session, student) catch; mark-attendance via separate PATCH /session-participants/:id; exports loadOrFail + studentBelongsToSession for the note service). **`SessionNoteService` — FERPA KEYSTONE**: 5 endpoints all gated on the dedicated `student_counseling_record:read` permission both at the controller (via @RequirePermission) and at the service (via assertFerpaAccess which calls permissionCheckService.hasAnyPermissionInTenant); teachers, parents, students NEVER hold this permission and receive 403 at the gate. Notes follow UNIQUE(session_id, student_id) so a group session carries one note per participant. **PATCH /session-notes/:id refuses any update when is_locked=true** with 400 "Note is locked and immutable. Create a follow-up session for additional observations." **PATCH /session-notes/:id/lock IRREVERSIBLE** — stamps is_locked + locked_at + locked_by atomically inside executeInTenantTransaction so the multi-column locked_chk schema invariant is satisfied; **no unlock endpoint by design** — corrections require a follow-up session. Step 5 surfaces stay unchanged. CaseloadService (5 endpoints under cou-001:read/write — list with row-scope + per-row manager check (counsellor sees own-caseload notes; teacher sees class students stripped; parent sees own children stripped); getById with inlined sessionCount + lastSessionDate + linkedBipId surfacing the Cycle 9 BIP via the Step 4 caseload_id backfill; POST counsellor/admin only via hasCounsellorScope (admin OR holds cou-001:write, the canonical counsellor signal in the IAM seed) with partial UNIQUE pre-flight; PATCH; close stamps closure_reason + closed_at + status in one locked tx). ReferralService (8 endpoints under cou-002:read/write — list with row-scope OR predicate covering assigned + unassigned-triage queue + own-submitted; getById with activity timeline; POST stamps referred_by from actor + copies default_priority + writes initial activity inside same tenant tx + emits svc.referral.created; state-machine triage / accept / start / complete / decline all use SELECT FOR UPDATE inside executeInTenantTransaction with activity rows written in same tx; counsellor-only at service layer via hasCounsellorScope; accept with openCaseload=true calls caseloads.createInternal to auto-open primary caseload). ReferralActivityService (IMMUTABLE per ADR-010 — sole writer is recordActivity(tx, ...) called by every state transition in same locked tx; no UPDATE / no DELETE methods; CASCADE on parent referral hard-delete mirrors Cycle 8 tkt_ticket_activity + Cycle 10 hlth_health_access_log). ReferralTypeService (3 endpoints — list with optional includeInactive, admin-only POST + PATCH; assertActive(id) used by ReferralService.create to copy default_priority + read requires_parent_notification at submission time). CounsellingModule imports TenantModule + IamModule + KafkaModule and lives between HealthRecordsModule and the global guards.
apps/api/src/library/    → M24 Library (Cycle 12 Steps 5 + 6). LibraryModule with 6 services + 6 controllers + DTO module + 28 endpoints under lib-001 + lib-002 codes + 1 Kafka emit topic (`lib.fine.issued`). Catalogue scope (Step 5, 12 endpoints): LocationService / CatalogueItemService with the **GIN full-text keystone** via `to_tsvector + plainto_tsquery + ts_rank` / CopyService with the **`GET /library/copies/barcode/:barcode` keystone**. Circulation scope (Step 6, 16 endpoints): **CheckoutService** with the keystone `checkout(input, actor)` running barcode-resolve → SELECT FOR UPDATE on copy → patron-type-resolve via `platform_students` first then `hr_employees` → policy lookup → max_checkouts validation → INSERT checkout + UPDATE copy state all in one tenant tx; **`returnCheckout(id, actor)` is the auto-fine + hold-reassignment keystone** — locks checkout, computes `days_overdue = GREATEST(CURRENT_DATE - due_date, 0)::int`, INSERTs OVERDUE fine when overdue + policy.overdueFinePerDay > 0, walks the hold queue (oldest PENDING flips to READY + copy walks to ON_HOLD_SHELF; otherwise copy returns to ON_SHELF), emits `lib.fine.issued` AFTER tx commits; `renew` validates ACTIVE/OVERDUE + renewal_count < policy.renewalsAllowed, extends due_date, resets to ACTIVE. **HoldService** — `placeHold` defaults patronId to actor.personId (self-service via lib-002:read controller gate; service-layer `hasLibrarianScope` is the gate for cross-patron placements); validates parent item has copies + refuses duplicate PENDING/READY hold from same patron; cancel patron-or-librarian via row-scope; collect librarian-only on READY rows. **FineService** — list row-scopes patrons to own with OUTSTANDING-first sort; getById 404 don't-leak-existence; pay librarian-or-admin; **waive school-admin only via `actor.isSchoolAdmin` (lib-002:admin gate at controller too)** — financial write-off is admin authority. Reading + Reviews scope (Step 7, 18 endpoints): **ReadingProgrammeService** (5 endpoints — list / get / leaderboard / create / patch; STUDENT actors receive `myProgress` inlined per programme); **ReadingLogService** (4 endpoints, **STUDENT-INPUT KEYSTONE**) — `log()` validates the catalogue item + INSERTs the log + on `completedDate` set runs the **programme-progress auto-upsert** via SCHOOL_WIDE / CLASS audience-matching SQL with `INSERT ... ON CONFLICT (programme_id, student_id) DO UPDATE` and a follow-up recompute of `is_complete` against programme thresholds, all inside one tenant tx; **ReadingListService** (6 endpoints) — multi-column `published_chk` lockstep on PATCH stamps `published_at` atomically on publish/unpublish; UNIQUE(school, name, year) name-collision catch + UNIQUE(list, item) duplicate-item catch; **ReviewService** (5 endpoints — list / submit / patch / hide / unhide) — UNIQUE(item, student) duplicate-review catch with PATCH-guidance message + soft-hide via `is_approved=false`. **`hasWriterScope` and `hasModeratorScope` on the reading + review services include `personType === 'STUDENT' return false` as the actual access boundary** because Student holds `lib-003:write` per the IAM seed (for self-service log + review submission) but is NOT a programme/list author or review moderator. `GET /catalogue/:id/reviews` is gated on `lib-001:read` (catalogue-read surface) so teachers see reviews on the catalogue page despite not holding lib-003:read. LibraryModule imports TenantModule + IamModule + KafkaModule and lives between WellbeingModule and the global guards.
apps/api/src/discipline/ → M20 SIS Discipline (Cycle 9 Step 4). DisciplineModule with 4 services + 3 controllers + 14 endpoints under beh-001:read / write / admin. CategoryService (CRUD on sis_discipline_categories with severity-aware sort CRITICAL→LOW; UNIQUE(school, name) → 400). ActionTypeService (CRUD on sis_discipline_action_types; requires_parent_notification flag drives ActionService.create fan-out). IncidentService (POST stamps reported_by from actor.employeeId; row-scope at buildVisibility — admin sees all + adminNotes; STAFF teacher sees reported_by=me OR student_id IN sis_class_teachers JOIN sis_enrollments ACTIVE with adminNotes stripped; GUARDIAN parent sees student_id IN sis_student_guardians JOIN sis_guardians ON g.person_id=me with adminNotes stripped; student/unknown AND FALSE; locked-row PATCH /:id/{review,resolve,reopen} — resolve sets resolved_by + resolved_at in same UPDATE per multi-column resolved_chk lockstep, reopen clears both). ActionService (POST validates parent incident is not RESOLVED + UNIQUE(incident, action_type) trap into 400 + dates_chk pre-check; emits beh.action.parent_notification_required when action_type.requires_parent_notification=true with portal-enabled guardian account IDs resolved at emit time via sis_student_guardians JOIN sis_guardians JOIN platform_users WHERE portal_access=true; PATCH locks row + reads existing dates inside tx; DELETE refused on RESOLVED — admin must reopen first). 3 Kafka emits — beh.incident.reported / beh.action.parent_notification_required (guardianAccountIds inline) / beh.incident.resolved (resolvedByAccountId + reportedById for self-suppress mirroring Cycle 8 follow-up 2). DisciplineModule imports TenantModule + IamModule + KafkaModule and lives between TicketsModule and the global guards.
apps/web/                → Next.js 14 frontend (App Router, Tailwind, React Query, Zustand)
apps/web/src/lib/        → api-client (Bearer + X-Tenant-Subdomain, single-flight 401→refresh), auth-store (Zustand), auth-context, query-client, shared TS types. Cycle 5 Step 7: scheduling-format.ts — formatting helpers (scheduleTypeLabel, periodTypeLabel, roomTypeLabel, dayOfWeekLabel, formatTime, bookingStatusLabel, changeRequestStatusLabel) plus ROOM_TYPES / PERIOD_TYPES / WEEKDAY_LABELS const arrays so the Schedule pages keep formatting consistent. Cycle 5 Step 8 extends the same module with CALENDAR_EVENT_TYPES + calendarEventTypeLabel + calendarEventChipClasses (per-eventType Tailwind chip palette used by both the calendar grid and the event detail modal) + coverageStatusLabel + coverageStatusPillClasses + todayIso() + addDaysIso(date, n) ISO date utilities. Cycle 6 Step 8: admissions-format.ts — status labels and pill class maps for periods/applications/offers/waitlist + NOTE_TYPE_LABELS + formatStudentName + formatDateOnly + formatDateTime + formatRelativeDeadline (renders responseDeadline as "in N days" / "in N weeks" / "expired YYYY-MM-DD") + todayIso + addDaysIso. Cycle 6 Step 10: billing-format.ts — RECURRENCE_OPTIONS / INVOICE_STATUSES / PAYMENT_METHODS / PAYMENT_STATUSES / REFUND_CATEGORIES const arrays + per-enum label maps + per-status pill class maps for invoices/payments/family-accounts/refunds/ledger-entry-types + formatCurrency (Intl.NumberFormat USD) + formatSignedCurrency (positive `+`, negative `−` for the ledger running-balance view) + formatDateOnly / formatDateTime mirrors so admissions-format.ts and billing-format.ts stay independent.
apps/web/src/components/ui/        → Avatar, StatusBadge, LoadingSpinner, EmptyState, PageHeader, Modal, Toast (provider+useToast), DataTable, cn helper
apps/web/src/components/shell/     → AppLayout (responsive drawer), Sidebar (persona + permission-driven; tile active-state uses AppDef.routePrefix when set, falling back to AppDef.href — added Cycle 5 Step 9 so /schedule/coverage etc. keep the Schedule tile lit), TopBar (avatar menu, sign-out, NotificationBell when user holds com-001:read OR com-002:read), inline SVG icons (Cycle 3 Step 8 adds BellIcon, ChatBubbleIcon, GradeIcon, MegaphoneIcon, CheckCircleIcon; Cycle 5 Step 7 adds CalendarIcon for the Schedule app tile; Cycle 6 Step 8 adds AcademicCapIcon for the Admissions app tile; Cycle 6 Step 10 adds BanknotesIcon for the Billing app tile)
apps/web/src/components/notifications/ → NotificationBell (top-bar bell + badge + dropdown; polls /notifications/inbox every 30s; uses describeNotification(item) for per-type title/subtitle and resolveDeepLink(item, user) for persona-aware link routing — exports both helpers for the /notifications page to reuse) — Cycle 3 Step 8
apps/web/src/components/dashboard/ → TeacherDashboard (Step 8), ParentDashboard (Cycle 1 Step 10; Cycle 2 Step 9 adds the per-child Grades section + "View grades" button), AdminDashboard (Step 11), StudentDashboard (Cycle 2 Step 9 — upcoming-assignment list + per-class average cards)
apps/web/src/components/classroom/ → ClassTabs (shared header for /classes/:id/* routes), AssignmentForm (create + edit), CategoryWeightModal (Step 7), GradeCellEditor (gradebook inline editor — Step 8), ProgressNoteModal (per-student upsert by class+term — Step 8), StudentClassGradesView (Step 9 — shared per-class breakdown UI used by both student and parent routes)
apps/web/src/components/scheduling/ → TimetableWeekView (Cycle 5 Step 9 — reusable periods × Mon-Fri grid driven by useBellSchedules; accepts slots + optional substitutions array for amber-highlight overrides; showTeacher / showRoom toggles), RoomAvailabilityChecker (Cycle 5 Step 9 — compact widget on /schedule/room-bookings powered by Step 5's `available: boolean | null` annotation on /rooms)
apps/web/src/hooks/      → React Query hooks. Cycle 1: useMyClasses, useClasses, useClass, useClassAttendance, useBatchSubmitAttendance, useAbsenceRequests, useMyChildren, useStudent, useStudentAttendance, useSubmitAbsenceRequest. Cycle 2 Step 7 (use-classroom.ts): useAssignmentTypes, useAssignments, useAssignment, useCreateAssignment, useUpdateAssignment, useDeleteAssignment, useCategories, useUpsertCategories. Cycle 3 Step 8 (use-notifications.ts): useNotificationInbox (polls /notifications/inbox every 30s, refetches on focus), useNotificationHistory ({ limit, type, before } params), useMarkAllNotificationsRead (invalidates ['notifications']). Cycle 3 Step 10 (use-announcements.ts): useAnnouncements ({includeDrafts, includeExpired}, 30s poll), useAnnouncement, useAnnouncementStats (30s poll, gated by id-null), useCreateAnnouncement, useUpdateAnnouncement, useMarkAnnouncementRead. Cycle 4 Step 8 (use-hr.ts): 16 hooks — useEmployees (refetch on focus), useEmployee, useMyEmployee, usePositions (5min stale), useEmployeeDocuments, useEmployeeCertifications, useExpiringCertifications (60s poll), useEmployeeCompliance, useComplianceDashboard (60s poll), useLeaveTypes (5min stale), useMyLeaveBalances, useLeaveRequests, plus 5 mutations: useSubmitLeaveRequest, useApproveLeaveRequest, useRejectLeaveRequest, useCancelLeaveRequest, useVerifyCertification. Cycle 5 Step 7 (use-scheduling.ts): 15 hooks — useBellSchedules (60s stale), useBellSchedule, useCreateBellSchedule, useUpdateBellSchedule(id), useUpsertPeriods(scheduleId), useSetDefaultBellSchedule; useTimetable(args), useTimetableForTeacher, useTimetableForClass, useTimetableForRoom, useCreateTimetableSlot, useUpdateTimetableSlot(id), useDeleteTimetableSlot; useRooms(args, 30s stale), useRoom, useCreateRoom, useUpdateRoom(id); useRoomBookings(args), useCreateRoomBooking, useCancelRoomBooking; useRoomChangeRequests(args), useCreateRoomChangeRequest, useApproveRoomChangeRequest(id), useRejectRoomChangeRequest(id). Cycle 5 Step 9 (same file): useTimetableForStudent(studentId) (calls the new row-scoped /timetable/student/:studentId endpoint). 31 total scheduling hooks. Cycle 6 Step 8 (use-enrollment.ts): 16 hooks — useAcademicYears (5min stale, GET /academic-years); useEnrollmentPeriods, useEnrollmentPeriod(id), useCreateEnrollmentPeriod, useUpdateEnrollmentPeriod(id), useCreateAdmissionStream(periodId), useCreateIntakeCapacity(periodId); useApplications(args, refetch on focus), useApplication(id), useCreateApplication, useUpdateApplicationStatus(id), useAddApplicationNote(id); useOffers (refetch on focus), useOffer(id), useIssueOffer(applicationId), useSetOfferConditionsMet(id), useRespondToOffer(id) (also invalidates ['notifications']); useWaitlist(args), useOfferFromWaitlist(id) (invalidates waitlist + applications + offers + periods because all four denormalisations change atomically). Cycle 6 Step 10 (use-billing.ts): 23 hooks — useFeeCategories (5min stale), useCreateFeeCategory; useFeeSchedules, useFeeSchedule(id), useCreateFeeSchedule, useUpdateFeeSchedule(id); useFamilyAccounts (refetch on focus), useFamilyAccount(id), useFamilyAccountBalance(id) (30s stale), useFamilyAccountLedger(id, args); useInvoices(args, refetch on focus), useInvoice(id), useCreateInvoice, useSendInvoice(id) (invalidates family-accounts + family-account because the CHARGE ledger entry shifts the balance), useCancelInvoice(id), useGenerateFromSchedule; usePayments(args, refetch on focus), usePayment(id), usePayInvoice(invoiceId) (invalidates invoice + invoices + payments + family-accounts + family-account); useRefunds(args), useIssueRefund(paymentId) (invalidates payments + payment + refunds + family-accounts + family-account); usePaymentPlan(id), useCreatePaymentPlan(invoiceId). All mutations invalidate the ['discipline'] key tree; lifecycle mutations also bump ['tasks'] + ['notifications'] since the Cycle 7 TaskWorker creates an admin-review AUTO task on beh.incident.reported and the Step 6 BehaviourNotificationConsumer enqueues IN_APP rows.
```
